1　引言

1 Introduction

如今称为 λ 演算与组合逻辑的形式系统，都发明于 20 世纪 20 年代，旨在非常一般的背景下描述函数抽象、应用与代换的最基本性质。λ 演算把抽象概念作为原始概念；组合逻辑则用称为基本组合子的某些原始算子定义它。

The formal systems that are nowadays called λ-calculus and combinatory logic were both invented in the 1920s, and their aim was to describe the most basic properties of function-abstraction, application and substitution in a very general setting. In λ-calculus the concept of abstraction was taken as primitive, but in combinatory logic it was defined in terms of certain primitive operators called basic combinators.

本文将概述这两个主题在整个 20 世纪的历史。

The present article will sketch the history of these two topics through the twentieth century.

我们假定读者熟悉当前文献中这些系统的众多版本中的至少一种。不过，少数关键技术细节仍会放在脚注中。¹ 下文常把“组合逻辑”缩写为 CL，把“λ 演算”缩写为 λ。我们区分 λ 或 CL 的“纯”版本，即仅有转换或归约理论，以及“应用”版本，即含逻辑常量、类型或数等额外概念的版本。

We shall assume the reader is familiar with at least one of the many versions of these systems in the current literature. A few key technical details will be given as footnotes, however.1 Often “combinatory logic” will be abbreviated to “CL” and “λ-calculus” to “λ”. We shall distinguish between “pure” versions of λ or CL (theories of conversion or reduction with nothing more) and “applied” versions (containing extra concepts such as logical constants, types or numbers).

理解早期历史时，值得记住：20 世纪初，逻辑与数学基础的局面比今天更不稳定；罗素悖论出现不久，哥德尔定理尚不为人知，而逻辑研究的重要方向之一，是构造意在为整个数学分析提供一致基础的系统。其中一些基于集合概念，另一些基于函数概念，何种基础更好并无普遍共识。λ 与 CL 正是在这一背景下发展起来，最初并非自主系统，而是以函数概念为基础的更复杂基础系统的一部分。

To understand the early history it is worth remembering that the situation in logic and the foundations of mathematics was much more fluid in the early 1900s than it is today; Russell’s paradox was relatively recent, Gödel’s theorems were not yet known, and a significant strand of work in logic was the building of systems intended to be consistent foundations for the whole of mathematical analysis. Some of these were based on a concept of set, others on one of function, and there was no general consensus as to which basis was better. In this context λ and CL were originally developed, not as autonomous systems but as parts of more elaborate foundational systems based on a concept of function.

今天，λ 与 CL 广泛用于高阶逻辑与计算。正如公共汽车底盘支撑车辆，却不为乘客所见，λ 或 CL 的各种版本支撑若干重要逻辑系统与编程语言。它们的大部分用途，也间接来自这些系统，正如孤立底盘本身用途有限。因此，要呈现均衡图景，本文其实应包含以函数为基础的高阶逻辑全部历史。但那会过于散漫，所以我们采取较有限的方法。不过，读者应始终记住更广背景。

Today, λ and CL are used extensively in higher-order logic and computing. Rather like the chassis of a bus, which supports the vehicle but is unseen by its users, versions of λ or CL underpin several important logical systems and programming languages. Further, λ and CL gain most of their purpose at second hand from such systems, just as an isolated chassis has little purpose in itself. Therefore, to give a balanced picture the present article should really include the whole history of function-based higher-order logic. However, it would then be too diffuse, so we shall take a more restricted approach. The reader should always keep the wider context in mind, however.

概括地看，λ 与 CL 历史分为三个主要时期：首先是 20、30 年代数年密集而成果丰硕的研究；其次是近 30 年相对平静的中期；然后在 60 年代末，高阶函数理论发展、与编程语言的联系及新技术发现，激发研究活动高涨。第一时期成果包括有史以来第一个谓词逻辑不可判定性的证明。第二时期成果很少引起非专业人士兴趣，却包括后来广泛应用的完备性、切消去、标准化等定理。第三时期自 60 年代起，成就包括模型构造与分析、多态类型系统发展、归约过程深入分析，以及读者可能熟知的许多其他成果。这一时期的高度活跃延续至今。

Seen in outline, the history of λ and CL splits into three main periods: first, several years of intensive and very fruitful study in the 1920s and ’30s; next, a middle period of nearly 30 years of relative quiet; then in the late 1960s an upsurge of activity stimulated by developments in higher-order function theory, by connections with programming languages, and by new technical discoveries. The fruits of the first period included the first-ever proof that predicate logic is undecidable. The results of the second attracted very little non-specialist interest, but included completeness, cut-elimination and standardization theorems (for example) that found many uses later. The achievements of the third, from the 1960s onward, included constructions and analyses of models, development of polymorphic type systems, deep analyses of the reduction process, and many others probably well known to the reader. The high level of activity of this period continues today.

本文按时间顺序描述早期工作；后期发展则在主题重叠所允许范围内分类讨论。每个后期主题都将较广泛地讨论，以展示 λ 与 CL 的一些使用背景。

The present article will describe earlier work in chronological order, but will classify later developments by topic, insofar as overlaps allow. Each later topic will be discussed in some breadth, to show something of the contexts in which λ and

1　本卷 [Seldin, 2007] 含 λ 演算简短介绍。许多计算机科学教材也有介绍。较长介绍见 [Hankin, 1994; Hindley and Seldin, 1986; Stenlund, 1972]，以及 [Krivine, 1990]（法文、英文）、[Takahashi, 1991]（日文）、[Wolfengagen, 2004]（俄文）。更深入叙述见 [Barendregt, 1981]。组合逻辑入门见 [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, Ch.2; Stenlund, 1972; Barendregt, 1981, Ch.7]；详述见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, Chs.5–9; Curry et al., 1972]。

1A short introduction to λ-calculus is included in [Seldin, 2007] in the present volume. Others can be found in many textbooks on computer science. There are longer introductions in [Hankin, 1994], [Hindley and Seldin, 1986], [Stenlund, 1972]; also in [Krivine, 1990] (in French and English), [Takahashi, 1991] (in Japanese), and [Wolfengagen, 2004] (in Russian). A deeper account is in [Barendregt, 1981]. For combinatory logic there are introductions in [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, Ch.2], [Stenlund, 1972], and [Barendregt, 1981, Ch.7]; more detailed accounts are in [Curry and Feys, 1958, Chs.5–9] and [Curry et al., 1972].

然而，由于篇幅有限且领域丰富，我们无法如愿做到全面，不幸必须省略一些重要子主题，敬请读者谅解。虽力求均衡，选材仍不可避免反映我们的经验。例如，柯里工作比丘奇工作占更多篇幅，只因我们更熟悉它，并非认为它更重要。

CL are being used. However, due to lack of space and the richness of the field we shall not be able to be as comprehensive as we would like, and some important subtopics will unfortunately have to be omitted; for this we ask the reader’s forgiveness. Although we shall try to keep a balance, our selection will inevitably reflect our own experience. For example Curry’s work will be given more space than Church’s simply because we know it better, not because we think it more important.

为便于追溯来源，本文附大量参考文献。需要详细来源证据时，会给出准确章节或页码。有时，我们给某思想的日期明显早于其发表日期，这依据原论文中的证据，例如手稿日期。顺便说，为思想注明日期与作者，不应解释为宣称该作者享有优先权；重要思想可能几乎同时独立发明，关键论文也可能在正式发表前非正式传阅多年。因此，优先权问题虽很有趣，却超出我们的裁断能力。

A large bibliography will be included for the reader’s convenience in tracing sources. When detailed evidence of a source is needed, the relevant precise section or pages will be given. Sometimes the date we shall give for an idea will significantly precede its date of publication; this will be based on evidence in the source paper such as the date of the manuscript. By the way, mention of a date and an author for an idea should not be interpreted as a claim of priority for that author; important ideas may be invented independently almost simultaneously, and key papers may be circulated informally for some years before being published, so questions of priority, although very interesting, are beyond our powers to decide.

2　前史

2 Pre-history

函数抽象与代换记号至少可追溯到 1889 年。在朱塞佩·皮亚诺的算术公理著作 [Peano, 1889, §VI] 中，对含变量 x 的任意项 α，由 α 确定的 x 的函数称为 α[x]；令 φ=α[x]，便给出方程 φx′=α[x]x′，并明确说明右侧表示在 α 中以 x′ 代 x 的结果。后来皮亚诺以其他函数抽象记号代替 α[x]，包括 α x 与 α|x，分别见 [Peano, 1958, p.277; Peano, 1895, Vol.3, §11]。

Notations for function-abstraction and substitution go back at least as far as 1889. In Giuseppe Peano’s book on axioms for arithmetic [Peano, 1889, §VI], for any term α containing a variable x, the function of x determined by α was called α[x], and for φ = α[x] the equation φ x′ = α[x]x′ was given, and its right-hand side was explicitly stated to mean the result of substituting x′ for x in α. Later, Peano used other function-abstraction notations instead of α[x]; these included α x and α|x, in [Peano, 1958, p.277] and [Peano, 1895, Vol.3 §11] respectively.

1891 年，戈特洛布·弗雷格讨论函数的一般概念，并引入把函数视为图像的观念，即 [Frege, 1891] 的 Wertverlauf。两年后，[Frege, 1893, Vol.1, §9] 出现抽象与应用记号：函数 Φ( ) 的图像记为 ἐΦ(ε)，把它应用于参数 Δ 的结果记为 Δ∩ἐΦ(ε)。同书还证明方程 a∩ἐf(ε)=f(a)，见第 1 卷第 73 页 §54。

In 1891 Gottlob Frege discussed the general concept of function and introduced the notion of a function as a graph (Wertverlauf in [Frege, 1891]). Two years later, notations for abstraction and application appeared in his [Frege, 1893, Vol.1 §9], where the graph of a function Φ( ) was denoted by ,ε Φ(ε), and the result of applying the function to an argument ∆was denoted by ∆∩ ,ε Φ(ε). In the same work Frege also proved the equation a ∩ ,ε f(ε) = f(a), [Frege, 1893, Vol.1 p.73, §54].

类抽象记号也约在同时出现。满足条件 α 的全部 x 构成的集合，在 [Peano, 1889, §V] 中记为 [x ε]α；皮亚诺自 1890 年起记为 x ε α，见 [Peano, 1958, Vol.2, p.110]。其同事布拉利-福尔蒂也用后一记号 [Burali-Forti, 1894, p.71, §3]。另一类抽象记号 ẑ(φz) 见 [Russell and Whitehead, 1913, Ch.1, p.25]；两位作者还以 φx̂ 表示由 φx 确定的一元命题函数。

Notations for class-abstraction too appeared at around the same time. The set of all x satisfying condition α was denoted by [x ε] α in [Peano, 1889, §V], and by x ε α in Peano’s work from 1890 onward, see [Peano, 1958, Vol.2 p.110]. The latter notation was also used by his colleague Burali-Forti [Burali-Forti, 1894, p.71 §3]. Another class-abstraction notation, ˆz(φ z), appeared in [Russell and Whitehead, 1913, Ch.1, p.25]. The latter two authors also used φ ˆx for the unary propositional function determined by φ x.

伯特兰·罗素在 1903—1905 年未发表笔记中使用过函数抽象记号，见 [Klement, 2003]。

A function-abstraction notation was used by Bertrand Russell in 1903–5 in unpublished notes, see [Klement, 2003].

然而，这些作者都没有给出代换与转换的形式定义，而这正是真正 CL 与 λ 的特征。这些定义直到 20 年代才出现。虽然 λ 看似比 CL 更直接表达有关概念，实际先发明的却是 CL。

However, none of these authors gave formal definitions of substitution and conversion, which are characteristic of CL and λ proper. These did not appear until the 1920s, and, although λ might seem to express the concepts involved more directly than CL, it was actually CL that was invented first.

3　20 世纪 20 年代：组合逻辑的诞生

3 1920s: Birth of Combinatory Logic

组合逻辑由摩西·伊里奇·申芬克尔发明。他于 1887 或 1889 年出生于乌克兰第聂伯罗彼得罗夫斯克，师从敖德萨数学家塞缪尔·沙图诺夫斯基；据 [Yanovskaya, 1948, p.31]，他是杰出学生。1914—1924 年，他属于德国哥廷根大卫·希尔伯特领导的、可能是当时最顶尖的数学研究小组。他于 1920 年 12 月 7 日在小组报告中介绍组合子，发表于 [Schönfinkel, 1924]。

Combinatory logic was invented by Moses Ilyich Sch ̈onfinkel. Sch ̈onfinkel was born in 1887 or ‘89 in Dniepropetrovsk in the Ukraine, and studied under the Odessa mathematician Samuel Shatunovsky – a brilliant pupil, according to [Yanovskaya, 1948, p. 31]. From 1914 to 1924 he belonged to what was probably the top mathematics research group of the time, that led by David Hilbert in G ̈ottingen, Germany. He introduced combinators to this group in a talk on 7 December 1920, which was published in [Sch ̈onfinkel, 1924].

申芬克尔的主要目标是消去约束变量，他认为它们只是辅助句法概念，见 [Schönfinkel, 1924, §1 末]。为此，他引入五个算子 Z、T、I、C、S，如今分别称为基本组合子 B、C、I、K、S。²

Sch ̈onfinkel’s main aim was to eliminate bound variables, which he saw as merely auxiliary syntactic concepts, see [Sch ̈onfinkel, 1924, end of §1]. To do this, he introduced five operators Z, T, I, C and S which would nowadays be called basic combinators B, C, I, K and S respectively.2

申芬克尔证明 K、S 足以定义另外三个基本组合子，并陈述一种组合完备性结果，附证明概要：从 K、S 及等价于全称量化谢费尔竖线，即 NAND 的逻辑算子出发，可不用约束变量生成谓词逻辑全部公式。但与当时数学惯例一样，他仅非形式处理相等与逻辑等价，没有引入形式转换规则。论文也未陈述显式抽象算法，尽管从内容看，他极可能知道一种。

Sch ̈onfinkel proved that K and S are adequate to define the other three basic combinators, and stated a form of combinatory completeness result (with a sketch proof): namely that from K and S, together with a logical operator equivalent to a universally quantified Sheffer stroke or Nand, one can generate all formulas of predicate logic without the use of bound variables. But he treated equality and logical equivalence only informally, as was common practice in mathematics at that time; he did not introduce formal conversion rules. Nor was an explicit abstraction algorithm stated in his paper, although from what was written it seems extremely likely that he knew one.

其间，申芬克尔指出，F(x,y) 这样的多变量应用，可替换为连续单参数应用 (f(x))(y)，其中 f 的输出 f(x) 也是函数，见 [Schönfinkel, 1924, §2]。计算机科学界如今称此过程为“柯里化”，得名于哈斯凯尔·柯里工作中的使用；不过柯里多次归功于申芬克尔，例如 [Curry, 1930, p.512; Curry and Feys, 1958, pp.8,11,30,106]，而弗雷格更早已使用它 [Frege, 1893, Vol.1, §4]。

Along the way, Sch ̈onfinkel pointed out that multi-variable applications such as F(x, y) could be replaced by successive single applications (f(x))(y), where f was a function whose output-value f(x) was also a function, see [Sch ̈onfinkel, 1924, §2]. This replacement-process is now known in the computer-science community as “currying”, from its use in the work of Haskell Curry; although Curry many times attributed it to Sch ̈onfinkel, for example in [Curry, 1930, p.512] and [Curry and Feys, 1958, pp. 8, 11, 30, 106], and it was used even earlier by Frege, [Frege, 1893, Vol.1 §4].

1924 年论文之后，申芬克尔未再发表组合子论文。事实上，仅另一篇论文署有其名：[Bernays and Schönfinkel, 1928]，研究判定问题的某些特例。两篇论文的发表准备，主要由热心同事完成。

After his 1924 paper, Sch ̈onfinkel published nothing more on combinators. In fact only one other paper bears his name: [Bernays and Sch ̈onfinkel, 1928], on certain special cases of the Entscheidungsproblem. Both papers were prepared for

2　按今日记号，即 [Curry and Feys, 1958, Ch.5]，其公理模式为 BXYZ=X(YZ)，CXYZ=XZY，IX=X，KXY=X，SXYZ=XZ(YZ)；组合子是基本组合子的任意应用组合。组合子集 S 称为组合完备，若对任意变量序列 x1,…,xn，及由其中全部或部分变量构成的任意组合 X，允许重复出现，都存在 S 中成员的组合 A，使 Ax1…xn=X。抽象算法是从 X 构造适当 A 的任意算法。下文以 [x1,…,xn].X 表示任意适当的 A。

2In today’s notation (following [Curry and Feys, 1958, Ch.5]) their axiom-schemes are BXY Z = X(Y Z), CXY Z = XZY , IX = X, KXY = X, SXY Z = XZ(Y Z), and a combinator is any applicative combination of basic combinators. A set S of combinators is called combinatorially complete when, for every sequence x1, ..., xn of variables and every combination X of all or some of these (possibly with some occurring more than once), there exists a combination A of members of S such that Ax1...xn = X. An abstraction algorithm is any algorithm for constructing a suitable A from X. In what follows, the notation “[x1, ..., xn].X” will denote any suitable A.

较早论文由海因里希·贝曼整理，较晚论文由保罗·伯奈斯整理。据称，到 1927 年他已患精神疾病，住进疗养院 [Curry, 1927, p.3; Kline, 1951, p.47]。莫斯科的亚历山大·库济切夫说，申芬克尔晚年在莫斯科生活艰难贫困，由少数朋友帮助，患病多年后于 1942 年在当地医院去世。战时条件迫使邻居在其去世后焚烧其手稿取暖。³

publication largely by helpful colleagues: the earlier one by Heinrich Behmann and the later by Paul Bernays. By 1927, he was said to be mentally ill and in a sanatorium [Curry, 1927, p.3], [Kline, 1951, p.47]. Alexander Kuzichev in Moscow relates that Sch ̈onfinkel’s later years were spent in Moscow in hardship and poverty, helped by a few friends, and he died in a hospital there in 1942 following some years of illness. After his death, wartime conditions forced his neighbours to burn his manuscripts for heating.3

与此同时，组合子思想再次出现，但形式大幅改变。1925 年，约翰·冯·诺伊曼发表关于集合论公理化的博士论文 [Neumann, 1925]。虽是集合理论，其形式系统却以函数而非集合为基础；其中一组公理假定某些类似组合子的算子，使由变量和常量经配对、函数应用建立的全部表达式具有组合完备性，见第 225 页。⁴

However, in the meantime the combinator idea had appeared again, though in a very modified form. In 1925, John von Neumann published his doctoral thesis on axiomatizing set theory, [Neumann, 1925]. Despite its being a theory of sets, his formal system was function-based not set-based, and one group of his axioms postulated certain combinator-like operators which gave combinatory completeness for all expressions built from variables and constants by pairing and function-application, [Neumann, 1925, p.225].4

我们不知道冯·诺伊曼的思想是否来自申芬克尔。他早在 1925 年之前就访问哥廷根并与当地逻辑小组合作，很难想象他未在那里得知申芬克尔工作。但 [Neumann, 1925] 中概念形式大幅改变，而且该文与 [Neumann, 1928] 都未提申芬克尔。

(We do not know whether von Neumann’s idea came from Sch ̈onfinkel’s. Von Neumann visited G ̈ottingen well before 1925 and collaborated with the logic group there, and it is hard to imagine that he did not learn of Sch ̈onfinkel’s work there. But the form in which the concept appeared in [Neumann, 1925] was very modified, and his [Neumann, 1925] and [Neumann, 1928] did not mention Sch ̈onfinkel.)

冯·诺伊曼公理经多次改变，包括从函数基础改为集合与类基础，最终演化为如今熟知的冯·诺伊曼-伯奈斯-哥德尔系统 NBG。其中，组合子公理的对应物是有限列类存在公理，组合完备性定理的对应物是一般类存在定理。与如今称为 ZF 的策梅洛-弗兰克尔系统不同，它给出集合论的有限公理化。但它没有从逻辑中完全消去约束变量；与申芬克尔不同，冯·诺伊曼并不以此为目标。

The von Neumann axioms eventually evolved, with many changes, including from a function base to a sets-and-classes base, into the nowadays well known Von-Neumann-Bernays-Gödel system NBG. In that system the analogue of von Neumann’s combinator axioms was a finite list of class-existence axioms, and the analogue of his combinatory completeness theorem was a general class-existence theorem. That system gave a finite axiomatization of set theory, in contrast to the Zermelo-Fraenkel system known nowadays as ZF. It did not, however, eliminate bound variables completely from the logic; unlike Sch ̈onfinkel, von Neumann did not have that as an aim.

CL 的下一个重大进展独立于申芬克尔与冯·诺伊曼：哈斯凯尔·柯里在美国重新发明组合子概念。

The next major step in CL was independent of both Sch ̈onfinkel and von Neumann: the combinator concept was re-invented in the U.S.A. by Haskell Curry.

柯里生于 1900 年。其父母在美国波士顿附近经营公共演讲学校，如今称柯里学院；他从父母处获得语言学兴趣，影响后来许多工作。他在哈佛开始学业时原想学医，很快转向数学；对基础问题的强烈倾向最终使他转向逻辑。⁵

Curry was born in 1900. His parents ran a school of public speaking near Boston, U.S.A. (now called Curry College), and from them he derived an interest in linguistics which coloured much of his later work. He began his student career at Harvard University intending to work in medicine but quickly changed to mathematics, and a strong underlying inclination towards foundational questions drew him eventually to logic.5

命题逻辑中，代换规则明显比分离规则及其他演绎规则复杂；柯里开始研究时，代换操作大体未经分析。⁶ 特别地，《数学原理》缺少代换规则的明确陈述。

In propositional logic the rule of substitution is significantly more complex than modus ponens and the other deduction-rules, and at the time Curry began his studies the substitution operation was largely unanalyzed.6 In particular, an explicit

3　关于他的更多情况所知甚少。我们主要依据 [Thiel, 1995; Yanovskaya, 1948, pp.31–34; Kline, 1951] 及 A. S. Kuzichev 2005 年通信。据 Thiel，他生于 1889 年 9 月 4 日；据 Kuzichev，则为 1887 年。4　有关公理见 [Neumann, 1925, §3, 公理组 II]，评论见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, pp.10–11]。这里选用突出其与 CL 相似性的记号。冯·诺伊曼使用两个不互斥的原子谓词“x 是函数”“x 是参数”，及配对 ⟨,⟩、应用 ( ) 两种构项操作。配对另有两个投影函数，此处省略。

(For pairing, he postulated two projection-functions; we omit them here.)

（∃函数 I）（∀参数 x）(Ix)=x；（∀参数 x）（∃函数 Kx）（∀参数 y）(Kxy)=x；（∃函数 App）（∀参数 f,x）f 是函数 ⊃ (App⟨f,x⟩)=(fx)；（∀函数 f,g）（∃函数 Sf,g、Bf,g）（∀参数 x）(Sf,g x)=⟨(fx),(gx)⟩ ∧ (Bf,g x)=(f(gx))。

5　柯里资料依据 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§1S,6S,8S; Curry, 1980; Seldin, 1980b; Seldin, 1980a]，以及与 Jonathan Seldin 的讨论；其 [Seldin, 2007] 比本文更详细介绍柯里与丘奇。柯里于 1982 年去世。6　命题逻辑代换规则说：从公式 Q，可推出 [P1/p1,…,Pn/pn]Q，其中后者由 Q 中可能出现的命题变量 p1,…,pn 分别代入公式 P1,…,Pn 得到。

5For information on Curry we are indebted to [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§1S,6S,8S], [Curry, 1980], and [Seldin, 1980b; Seldin, 1980a], also to discussions with Jonathan Seldin, whose [Seldin, 2007] gives a fuller account of Curry and Church than the present one. Curry died in 1982. 6The substitution rule in propositional logic states that from a formula Q we may deduce [P1/p1, . . . , Pn/pn]Q, where the latter is obtained from Q by substituting formulas P1, . . . , Pn for propositional variables p1, . . . , pn which may occur in Q.

罗素后来承认《数学原理》缺少该规则的明确陈述，见 [Russell, 1919, Ch.14, p.151]。而权威教材初版 [Hilbert and Ackermann, 1928, Ch.3, §5, p.53] 还错误陈述谓词逻辑的相应规则，见 [Church, 1956, §49, pp.290–291] 的评论。

statement of the substitution rule was missing from Principia Mathematica [Russell and Whitehead, 1913], as was admitted later by Russell himself, see [Russell, 1919, Ch.14, p.151]. Also the corresponding rule for predicate logic was stated incorrectly in the first edition of a leading textbook [Hilbert and Ackermann, 1928, Ch.3 §5 p.53], see remarks in [Church, 1956, §49 pp. 290–291].

约 1926—1927 年，柯里开始寻找把代换分解为更简单步骤的方法，为此引入与申芬克尔实质相同的组合子概念。1927 年末，他发现 [Schönfinkel, 1924]，在笔记 [Curry, 1927, p.1] 中承认：“这篇论文预先做出了我所做的大部分工作。”但他没有放弃，而是赴哥廷根，于 1930 年在伯奈斯指导下完成博士论文 [Curry, 1930]。其中建立组合子形式系统，证明 {B,C,K,W} 的组合完备性，见第 528 页主定理 II。⁷

Around 1926–27, Curry began to look for a way to break the substitution process down into simpler steps, and to do this, he introduced a concept of combinator essentially the same as Sch ̈onfinkel’s. Then in late 1927 he discovered [Sch ̈onfinkel, 1924], and a note in his files [Curry, 1927, p.1] admits “This paper anticipates much of what I have done”. He did not abandon his own work, however, but travelled to G ̈ottingen and in 1930 completed a doctoral thesis under the guidance of Bernays, [Curry, 1930]. In it he set out a formal system of combinators with a proof of the combinatory completeness of {B, C, K, W} [Curry, 1930, p.528 Hauptsatz II].7

每个这类完备性证明的关键，都是抽象算法。柯里论文的算法不仅最早印行，也比后来许多算法高效得多。他从以序列 x1,…,xn 构造 X 的角度，处理寻找使 Ax1…xn 转换为 X 的 A 的问题。首先，A 必须从序列删除 X 中不出现的变量，可用 K、B；然后按 X 中出现次数重复变量，可用 W、B；再按 X 中出现顺序重排变量，可用 C、B；最后插入 X 的括号，可用 B 加 I 或 CKK。⁸

Crucial to every such completeness proof is an abstraction algorithm. The one in Curry’s thesis was not only the first to appear in print, but was also much more efficient than many that followed it. He saw the problem of producing an A such that Ax1...xn converted to X, from the point of view of building X from the sequence x1, . . . , xn. First, A must remove from x1, . . . , xn all variables which do not occur in X; this can be done using K and B. Then A must repeat variables as often as they occur in X; this can be done using W and B. Next, A must re-arrange the variables into the order in which they occur in X; this can be done using C and B. Finally, A must insert the parentheses that occur in X, and this can be done using B (together with I or CKK).8

但柯里算法细节颇复杂，后来被更易描述的算法取代。它们是多遍算法：先构造 [xn].X，再构造 [xn−1].[xn].X，依次得到 [x1,…,xn].X。关键是对 X 符号数作直接而极简单的归纳，为所有 x、X 构造 [x].X。

But the details of Curry’s algorithm were rather complex, and it was later replaced by ones that were much simpler to describe. These algorithms were multisweep; i.e. they built [x1, . . . , xn].X by building first [xn].X, then [xn−1].[xn].X, etc., and their key was to build [x].X for all x and X by a direct and very simple induction on the number of symbols in X.

最早出现于 [Church, 1935, §3, p.278]。丘奇只用 I、J 两个基本组合子，其中 JUXYZ=UX(UZY)；J 由罗瑟于 1933 年提出 [Rosser, 1935, p.128]。⁹

The first to appear was in [Church, 1935, §3 p.278]. Church used just two basic combinators I and J, where JUXY Z = UX(UZY ); the latter had been proposed by Rosser in 1933 [Rosser, 1935, p.128].9

但如今教材中基于 S、K 的简单算法归于保罗·罗森布卢姆 [Rosenbloom, 1950, p.117]。他似乎至少在印行文献中，首先认识到：在 [x].X 的归纳步骤使用 S，会使主要性质 ([x].X)Y=[Y/x]X 的证明变得平凡。¹⁰ 这一算法及其若干变体，成为普遍接受的标准。¹¹

But the simple algorithm based on S and K that is used in textbooks today is due to Paul Rosenbloom [Rosenbloom, 1950, p.117]. He seems to have been the first person, at least in print, to have realised that by using S in the induction step for [x].X the proof of its main property ([x].X)Y = [Y/x]X was made trivial.10 This algorithm, with some variants, became generally accepted as standard.11

7　W 的公理模式为 WXY=XYY。8　细节见 [Curry, 1930, Part II, §§C–E]，概要见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §6S2]。9　基 {I,J} 允许在 x 出现于 X 时构造 [x].X，但 x 不出现时不行。这对应下文第 7 页的 λI 演算。丘奇算法也见 [Church, 1941, p.44]。10　其实并非如此。[Seldin, 2007] 指出，柯里阅读 [Rosser, 1942] 时就认识到 S 的这一巧妙用法，但因战时工作，直到 [Curry, 1949, p.393] 才发表。因此，算法直接归纳的形式归于丘奇，S 的巧妙使用则由柯里与罗森布卢姆独立发现。11　[Curry and Feys, 1958, §§6A2–3] 比较若干 [x].X 算法。柯里假定基本组合子取自 B、C、I、K、S，并对 X 归纳，使用下列全部或部分条款构造 [x].X，其中 U 不含 x，V 则可含 x：

7W has the axiom-scheme WXY = XY Y . 8See [Curry, 1930, Part II, §§C–E] for details, or [Curry and Feys, 1958, §6S2] for an outline. 9The basis {I, J} allows [x].X to be built when x occurs in X but not when x is absent. This corresponds to the λI-calculus, see p.7 below. Church’s algorithm is also in [Church, 1941, p.44]. 10No he wasn’t. [Seldin, 2007] points out that Curry realized this neat use of S while reading [Rosser, 1942], though, due to war work, he did not publish it until [Curry, 1949, p.393]. Thus the algorithm’s direct inductive form is due to Church and its neat use of S to Curry and Rosenbloom independently. 11Some [x].X-algorithms were compared in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§6A2–3]. Curry assumed the basic combinators were some of B, C, I, K, S, and [x].X was built by induction on X using some or all of the following clauses (in which U does not contain x, but V may):

（a）[x].U≡KU；（b）[x].x≡I；（c）[x].Ux≡U；（d）[x].UV≡BU([x].V)；（e）[x].VU≡C([x].V)U；（f）[x].X1X2≡S([x].X1)([x].X2)。

(a) [x]. U ≡KU, (d) [x]. UV ≡BU([x]. V ), (b) [x]. x ≡I, (e) [x]. V U ≡C([x]. V )U, (c) [x]. Ux ≡U, (f) [x]. X1X2 ≡S([x]. X1)([x]. X2).

例如，基为 {I,K,S} 时，算法 (fab) 尽可能先用（f），再用（a），其他两条不适用时才用（b）。另一算法 (abcf) 先尝试（a），再（b）、（c），前三条均不适用时才用（f）；通常产生更短的 [x].X。柯里-罗森布卢姆算法是 (fab)。若基为 {K,S}，可先定义 I≡SKK，再用 (fab) 或 (abcf)。若基为 {B,C,K,W}，可定义 S≡B(B(BW)C)(BB)，以及 I≡CKK 或 WK，再用 (abcdef)。

For example, let the basis be {I, K, S}. Then algorithm (fab) builds [x].X by first applying (f) whenever possible, then (a), then (b) when the other two do not apply. Alternatively, (abcf) first tries to apply (a), then (b), then (c), and (f) only when the other three do not apply; it usually produces shorter terms [x].X. The Curry-Rosenbloom algorithm was (fab). If the basis is {K, S} one can first define I ≡SKK and then use (fab) or (abcf). If the basis is {B, C, K, W} one can define S ≡B(B(BW)C)(BB) and I ≡CKK or WK, and then use (abcdef).

然而，直接归纳算法虽描述更简洁，使用效率却不高：其输出远长于柯里最初算法。70 年代，当人们希望把组合子用于实用编程语言，开始关注效率时，新发明的算法反而更像柯里原始算法，例如 [Kearns, 1973; Abdali, 1976; Turner, 1979; Piperno, 1989]。

However, although neater to describe, the direct inductive algorithms were not so efficient to use: they produced considerably longer outputs than Curry’s first one, and in the 1970s when the desire arose to use combinators in practical programming languages, and attention began to focus on efficiency, the algorithms that were invented then were more like Curry’s original, see for example [Kearns, 1973; Abdali, 1976; Turner, 1979; Piperno, 1989].

除第一个抽象算法外，柯里论文还包含转换的第一个形式定义。此外，定义含一组有限公理，他由此证明规则（ζ）可容许：¹²

Besides the first abstraction algorithm, Curry’s thesis contained the first formal definition of conversion. Further, this definition included a finite set of axioms from which he proved the admissibility of rule (ζ):12

Ux=Vx⇒U=V(x∉FV(UV))

因此，理论诞生后不久，就发现（ζ）可有限公理化。相等理论还被证明在某种意义下一致，见 [Curry, 1930, p.528, Hauptsatz I]，但未使用强归约概念或合流定理。

Thus the discovery that (ζ) could be finitely axiomatized was made very soon after the birth of the theory. The theory of equality was also proved consistent in a certain sense, see [Curry, 1930, p.528 Hauptsatz I], but without using a concept of strong reduction or a confluence theorem.

[Curry, 1930] 的系统还含一些逻辑常量。但未纳入它们全部通常规则，省略否定，且逻辑实际上未发展到转换理论之外。事实上，与冯·诺伊曼不同，柯里目标不是迅速建立足以推出数学重要部分的系统，而是在尽可能一般的背景中彻底研究基本逻辑概念。

The system in [Curry, 1930] also contained some logical constants. But not all their usual rules were included, negation was omitted, and logic was not actually developed beyond the theory of conversion. In fact, unlike von Neumann, Curry aimed, not at quickly building a system strong enough to deduce a significant part of mathematics, but at making a thorough study of basic logical notions in as general a setting as possible.

这种对一般性的兴趣导致：对柯里和申芬克尔而言，每个组合子都可应用于任何其他组合子，甚至自身；集合论函数通常不允许这样。后来，其他几位主要逻辑学家从语义角度批评这种自由，例如威拉德·V. 奎因 [Quine, 1936a, p.88]。事实上，柯里使用组合子与其数学哲学紧密相连：对他而言，形式系统不是描述某些先在对象，而是从若干原始成分按规则归纳建立的结构；他不要求系统语义是全函数。在他看来，一个系统不必每部分都在某种基于集合论的语义中有意义，也可富有成效地用于数学应用；有用性比语义的全定义性更重要。¹³

A consequence of this interest in generality was that for Curry, as for Sch ̈onfinkel, every combinator was allowed to be applied to every other combinator and even to itself, in a way not commonly accepted for set-theoretic functions. This freedom was later criticized on semantic grounds by several other leading logicians, e.g. Willard V. Quine in [Quine, 1936a, p.88]. In fact Curry’s use of combinators was connected very closely to his philosophy of mathematics: for him, a formal system was not a description of some pre-existing objects, but simply a structure built by induction from some primitives using certain rules, and he did not demand that a semantics for such a system be a total function. In his view a system could be used fruitfully in applications of mathematics without necessarily every part of it having a meaning in some set-theory-based semantics, and usefulness was more important than semantic totality.13

[Curry, 1964] 指出，由其原始抽象算法构造的组合子，比用 K、S 的简单算法所得组合子，更易在集合论语义中解释。但如上所述，他本人完全乐于使用较简单算法。

In [Curry, 1964] he pointed out that the combinators constructed by his original abstraction algorithm were much easier to interpret in a set-theoretic semantics than those built by simpler algorithms using K and S. (But, as mentioned above, he himself was perfectly happy to use the simpler algorithms.)

不过，撇开语义问题，到 20 年代末，组合子概念已提供两种有用形式技术：计算上高效地避免约束变量的方法，以及集合论的有限公理化。

However, semantic questions apart, by the end of the 1920s the combinator concept had provided two useful formal techniques: a computationally efficient way of avoiding bound variables, and a finite axiomatization of set theory.

4　20 世纪 30 年代：λ 的诞生与 CL 的青年期

4 1930s: Birth of λ and Youth of CL

4.1　早期 λ 演算

4.1 Early λ-calculus

λ 演算由阿隆佐·丘奇约于 1928 年发明，首次发表于 [Church, 1932]。丘奇于 1903 年生于华盛顿特区，就读于普林斯顿大学。他一直在普林斯顿任职至 1967 年，不过 1928—1929 年访问过哥廷根和阿姆斯特丹。¹⁴

The λ-calculus was invented in about 1928 by Alonzo Church, and was first published in [Church, 1932]. Church was born in 1903 in Washington D.C. and studied at Princeton University. He made his career at Princeton until 1967, though in 1928–29 he visited G ̈ottingen and Amsterdam.14

12　见 [Curry, 1930, p.832, Satz 4] 或 [Curry, 1932, p.558, Theorem 5]。规则（ζ）使柯里相等关系等价于如今的 βη 相等，即外延相等。13　见 [Curry, 1951]、[Curry, 1963, Chs.2–3] 或 [Seldin, 1980a]。20 年代，形式系统一般概念仍在澄清，柯里是先驱之一。

12See [Curry, 1930, p.832 Satz 4] or [Curry, 1932, p.558 Theorem 5]. Rule (ζ) makes Curry’s equality equivalent to what is nowadays called βη-equality or extensional equality. 13See [Curry, 1951], [Curry, 1963, Chs. 2–3] or [Seldin, 1980a]. In the 1920s the general concept of formal system was still in the process of clarification and Curry was one of the pioneers in this.

约 1928 年，他开始构造形式系统，希望为逻辑提供比罗素类型论或策梅洛集合论更自然、且不含自由变量的基础，理由见 [Church, 1932, pp.346–347]。他选择函数而非集合为基础，原始概念包括抽象 λx[M] 和应用 {F}(X)，本文记为“λx.M”与“(FX)”。

Around 1928 he began to build a formal system with the aim of providing a foundation for logic which would be more natural than Russell’s type theory or Zermelo’s set theory, and would not contain free variables (for reasons he explained in [Church, 1932, pp. 346–347]). He chose to base it on the concept of function rather than set, and his primitives included abstraction λx[M] and application {F}(X), which we shall call here “λx.M” and “(FX)”.

顺便问，丘奇为何选择“λ”？[Church, 1964, §2] 明确说，它源自怀特海与罗素类抽象所用的“x̂”：先改为“∧x”，以区分函数抽象与类抽象，再为便于印刷把“∧”改为“λ”。[Rosser, 1984, p.338] 也报告这一来源。另一方面，丘奇晚年告诉两位询问者，选择更具偶然性：需要一个符号，就碰巧选了“λ”。

(By the way, why did Church choose the notation “λ”? In [Church, 1964, §2] he stated clearly that it came from the notation “ˆx” used for class-abstraction by Whitehead and Russell, by first modifying “ˆx” to “∧x” to distinguish functionabstraction from class-abstraction, and then changing “∧” to “λ” for ease of printing. This origin was also reported in [Rosser, 1984, p.338]. On the other hand, in his later years Church told two enquirers that the choice was more accidental: a symbol was needed and “λ” just happened to be chosen.)

如前所述，丘奇并非第一个引入显式函数抽象记号的人。但他首先为记号陈述显式形式转换规则，并深入分析后果。据 [Scott, 1980a, p.260] 所引信件，他直到 1935 年才读弗雷格工作；我们推测他也未见皮亚诺 1889 年记号。

As mentioned earlier, Church was not the first to introduce an explicit notation for function-abstraction. But he was the first to state explicit formal conversion rules for the notation, and to analyse their consequences in depth. (He did not read Frege’s work until 1935, according to a letter cited in [Scott, 1980a, p.260], and we assume he did not see Peano’s 1889 notation either.)

[Church, 1932] 中的系统是无类型逻辑，量化不受限制，但没有排中律，并含显式形式 λ 转换规则。然而，发表后几乎立即发现矛盾。一年后系统修订 [Church, 1933]；丘奇希望哥德尔最近针对 [Russell and Whitehead, 1913] 系统的不完全性定理，不适用于其修订系统，并希望找到该系统一致性的有限主义证明，见 [Church, 1933, pp.842–843]。当时，哥德尔定理的全部威力还只是逐渐为人普遍理解。

Church’s system as it appeared in [Church, 1932] was a type-free logic with unrestricted quantification but without the law of excluded middle. Explicit formal rules of λ-conversion were included. However, almost immediately after publication a contradiction was found in it. The system was revised a year later, [Church, 1933], and in the revised paper Church stated a hope that Gödel’s recent incompleteness theorems for the system of [Russell and Whitehead, 1913] did not extend to his own revised system, and that a finitary proof of the consistency of this system might be found, [Church, 1933, pp. 842–843]. At that time the full power of Gödel’s theorems was only slowly becoming generally understood.

1933 年论文末，丘奇引入如今称为丘奇数码的 λ 项来表示正整数 [Church, 1933, §9]：

At the end of his 1933 paper, Church introduced the representation of the positive integers by the λ-terms now known as the Church numerals [Church, 1933, §9]:

1≡λxy.xy;Succ≡λxyz.y(xyz);n=βλxy.x(⋯(xy)⋯)

他定义全部正整数集合的方法，与 [Russell, 1903, §123] 定义全部有限基数集合的方法实质相同，只是罗素集合包含零。表示零的自然 λ 项应为 λxy.y；丘奇虽在语言中包含该项，却禁止归约 (λxy.y)FY▷Y，并回避零。

He defined the set of all positive integers in essentially the same way as the set of all finite cardinal numbers in [Russell, 1903, §123], except that Russell’s set included zero. The natural λ-term to represent zero would have been λxy.y, but although Church included this term in his language, he forbade the reduction (λxy.y)FY ▷ Y and avoided zero.

丘奇之名常与 λI 演算联系：只有 x 自由出现于 M，λx.M 才算项。但他并不像人们常想的那样严格局限于此版本。1932、1933 年，他允许非 λI 项 λx.M 存在，但不允许其“活跃”：若 x 不自由出现于 M，就不许收缩 (λx.M)N，见 [Church, 1932, pp.352,355]。1940 年类型论论文中，系统一致性疑虑较小，虽可能尚未真正证明，他便乐于允许非 λI 项活跃 [Church, 1940, pp.57,60]。直到 1941 年著作，才连其存在也禁止 [Church, 1941, p.8]。¹⁵

(Church’s name is often associated with the λI-calculus, the version of λ-calculus in which λx.M is only counted as a term when x occurs free in M. But he did not limit himself so strictly to this version as is often thought. In 1932 and ’33 he allowed non-λI-terms λx.M to exist but not to be “active”, i.e. he did not allow a term (λx.M)N to be contracted when x did not occur free in M, see [Church, 1932, pp. 352, 355]. In his 1940 paper on type theory, where consistency would have been less in doubt, although probably not yet actually proved, he was happy for non-λI-terms to be active [Church, 1940, pp. 57, 60]. Only in his 1941 book did he forbid their very existence [Church, 1941, p. 8].)15

14　丘奇生平另见 [Manzano, 1997, §2] 及讣告 [Enderton, 1995]；其工作另见 [Manzano, 1997; Anderson, 1998; Enderton, 1998; Sieg, 1997]，λ 演算影响见 [Barendregt, 1997]。他于 1995 年去世。15　无限制 λ 演算常称 λK 演算。

14For more on Church’s life, see [Manzano, 1997, §2] and the obituary [Enderton, 1995]. For more on his work, see [Manzano, 1997], [Anderson, 1998], [Enderton, 1998], [Sieg, 1997], and (on the impact of λ-calculus) [Barendregt, 1997]. He died in 1995. 15The unrestricted λ-calculus is often called the λK-calculus.

1931—1934 年，丘奇在普林斯顿得到两位杰出研究生斯蒂芬·克林与巴克利·罗瑟帮助。四年卓越合作中，小组对丘奇 1933 年逻辑及底层纯 λ 演算作出一系列重大发现。不幸，对 1933 年逻辑而言，发现之一就是不一致性：他们在系统中证明理查德悖论的一个变体 [Kleene and Rosser, 1935]。

From 1931 to 1934 in Princeton University, Church received the help of two outstanding graduate students, Stephen Kleene and Barkley Rosser, and in a remarkable four years of collaboration this group made a series of major discoveries about both Church’s 1933 logic and the underlying pure λ-calculus. Unfortunately for the 1933 logic, one of these discoveries was its inconsistency, as a variant of the Richard paradox was proved in the system, [Kleene and Rosser, 1935].

相比之下，最初看似浅薄的纯 λ 演算，却出乎意料地丰富。至少克林感到惊讶，见 [Kleene, 1981, p.54]。归约理论研究从残余方法开始，并用它证明丘奇-罗瑟合流定理 [Church and Rosser, 1936, p.479]，从而保证纯系统一致性。与组合逻辑的关系也得到澄清，并给出组合弱归约合流性的简单证明，见 [Rosser, 1935, 尤其 pp.145–146]。¹⁶

In contrast, the pure λ-calculus, which had at first seemed shallow, turned out to be surprisingly rich. (Well, Kleene was surprised, anyway, [Kleene, 1981, p.54].) The study of its reduction-theory was begun using the method of residuals, which was applied to prove the Church-Rosser confluence theorem [Church and Rosser, 1936, p.479], and this ensured the consistency of the pure system. The system’s relation to combinatory logic was also clarified, and a simple proof of the confluence of combinatory weak reduction was given; see [Rosser, 1935, esp. pp. 145–146].16

此外，λ 可定义数值函数形成的类远比预期广泛，最终证明它等价于旨在形式化非形式“有效可计算函数”概念的两个定义：埃尔布朗-哥德尔递归函数，见 [Kleene, 1936; Church, 1936b]，以及图灵可计算函数 [Turing, 1937a]。因此，丘奇猜想 λ 可定义性恰好刻画非形式有效可计算性，即丘奇论题。¹⁷ 可定义性研究的高潮，是丘奇否定解决希尔伯特长期悬而未决的一阶逻辑判定问题：先在 [Church, 1936b] 证明纯 λ 演算可转换性问题递归不可判定，再推出一阶谓词逻辑有效性不存在递归判定过程，见 [Church, 1936a]。

Further, the λ-definable numerical functions were found to form a much more extensive class than expected, and this class was eventually proved equivalent to two definitions whose purpose was to formalize the informal concept of effectively calculable function: the Herbrand-Gödel recursive functions, see [Kleene, 1936] and [Church, 1936b], and the Turing computable functions, [Turing, 1937a]. In view of this, Church conjectured that λ-definability exactly captured the informal concept of effective calculability (Church’s Thesis).17 The high point of this definability work was Church’s negative solution of Hilbert’s long-standing Entscheidungsproblem for first-order logic: first he proved in [Church, 1936b] that the convertibility problem for pure λ-calculus was recursively undecidable, then he deduced that no recursive decision procedure existed for validity in first-order predicate logic, see [Church, 1936a].

但几乎紧接着，英国的阿兰·图灵也独立证明该结果。30 年代剑桥，拓扑学家马克斯·纽曼一直兼有数学基础兴趣，1935 年初讲授相关课程，图灵参加。同年稍后，图灵写成如今著名的可计算性论文 [Turing, 1936]，随后访问普林斯顿，在丘奇指导下攻读博士，时间为 1936—1938 年。

But this result was also proved independently almost immediately afterwards by Alan Turing in England. In Cambridge in the 1930s the topologist Max Newman had maintained a side-interest in the foundations, and in early 1935 he taught a course on this topic which Turing attended. Later that year Turing wrote his now famous paper on computability, [Turing, 1936]. He then visited Princeton and took a doctorate under Church (in 1936–38).

顺便说，博士工作期间，图灵给出第一个发表的不动点组合子，即 [Turing, 1937b] 的项 Θ。当时只认为略有趣味，但鉴于后来此类组合子的重要性，见 §8.1.2，我们在此稍作离题讨论。

Incidentally, in the course of his doctoral work Turing gave the first published fixed-point combinator, [Turing, 1937b, term Θ]. This was seen as only having minor interest at that time, but in view of the later importance given to such combinators (see §8.1.2 below), we digress here to discuss them.

不动点组合子是满足 Yx 可转换为 x(Yx) 的任意闭项 Y。图灵给出的是 (λxy.y(xxy))(λxy.y(xxy))。

A fixed-point combinator is any closed term Y such that Yx converts to x(Yx). Turing’s was (λxy.y(xxy))(λxy.y(xxy)).

下一个见 [Rosenbloom, 1950, pp.130–131, Exs.3e,5f]：λx.W(Bx)(W(Bx))，可转换为 λx.(λy.x(yy))(λy.x(yy))。后者常称柯里的 Y；实际上，柯里直到 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §5G] 才明确给出不动点组合子。但 [Church, 1932, p.347] 与 [Curry, 1934a, §5] 叙述罗素悖论时，都提到 (λy.N(yy))(λy.N(yy))，N 表示否定；柯里对该项的使用可追溯至1929 年致希尔伯特的一封信。¹⁸ 注意，虽常用不动点组合子 λ 定义递归，但自然数上的递归并不真正需要它们；[Kleene, 1936] 表示递归函数时就未使用。

The next one to appear was in [Rosenbloom, 1950, pp.130–131, Exs. 3e, 5f]. It was λx. W(Bx)(W(Bx)), which is convertible to λx. (λy.x(yy))(λy.x(yy)). The latter has often been called Curry’s Y; in fact Curry gave no explicit fixed-point combinator before [Curry and Feys, 1958, §5G], but the accounts of Russell’s paradox in [Church, 1932, p.347] and [Curry, 1934a, §5] both mentioned (λy.N(yy))(λy.N(yy)), where N represented negation, and Curry’s use of the latter term dated back to a letter to Hilbert in 1929.18 Note that although fixed-point combinators are often used in λ-defining recursion, they are not really necessary for recursion on the natural numbers; none was used in the λ-representation of the recursive functions in [Kleene, 1936].

16　一般归约理论入门见 [Klop, 1992]，较窄范围见 [Barendregt, 1981, §3.1]；λ 情形见 [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, §1C 与附录1、2] 或 [Barendregt, 1981, Chs.11–15]。记号：可归约关系 ▷ 合流，即有丘奇-罗瑟性质，当且仅当 (U▷X 且 U▷Y)⇒(∃Z)(X▷Z 且 Y▷Z)。局部合流则把前件改为 U▷₁X、U▷₁Y，其中 ▷₁ 表示一步归约。一步归约称为收缩；X▷₁Y 通过把 X 中某个称为可约式的项出现，替换为其收缩结果得到。在 λ 中，β 可约式为 (λx.M)N，其收缩结果是 [N/x]M。不含可约式的项称正规形，不能再归约。17　丘奇论题历史评论见 [Kleene, 1981; Kleene, 1952, §62; Rosser, 1984; Davis, 1982; Gandy, 1988; Sieg, 1997]。

16For an introduction to reduction-theory in general, see [Klop, 1992] or (less general) [Barendregt, 1981, §3.1], or (in λ) [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, §1C & Appendices 1 & 2] or [Barendregt, 1981, Chs. 11–15]. Some notation: a reducibility relation ▷is confluent, or has the Church-Rosser property, iff(U ▷X & U ▷Y ) =⇒(∃Z) (X ▷Z & Y ▷Z). It is locally confluent iff(U ▷1 X & U ▷1 Y ) =⇒(∃Z) (X ▷Z & Y ▷Z), where “▷1” means one-step reducibility. A one-step reduction is called a contraction. A contraction X ▷1 Y is made by replacing an occurrence in X of a term called a redex by another term called its contractum. In λ, a (β-) redex is any term (λx.M)N and its contractum is [N/x]M. A term containing no redexes is called a normal form; it cannot be reduced. 17Comments on the history of Church’s Thesis are in: [Kleene, 1981], [Kleene, 1952, §62], [Rosser, 1984], [Davis, 1982], [Gandy, 1988] and [Sieg, 1997].

回到普林斯顿小组：应用方面的成功虽不及纯 λ 演算，结果却绝非全然消极。发现克林-罗瑟不一致性后，丘奇以基于 λδ 演算的自由变量逻辑替代 1933 年系统，并通过扩展丘奇-罗瑟定理证明一致性。实质上，δ 区分正规形，见 [Church, 1935] 或 [Church, 1941, §20]，及评论 [Curry, 1937]。但新系统对大量数学过弱，似未在丘奇后来工作中起作用。事实上，30 年代后期，他从设计一般无类型逻辑，退到以 λ 演算为基础重新表述简单类型论这一较小目标。他成功了：[Church, 1940] 发表流畅自然的系统，成为此后大量类型论工作的基础，见 §5.1 与 §8。

Returning to the Princeton group: their successes in pure λ-calculus were not really matched on the applied side, but the results were by no means completely negative. After the discovery of the Kleene-Rosser inconsistency, Church replaced his 1933 system by a free-variable logic based on the λδ-calculus, which he proved consistent by extending the Church-Rosser theorem. (In essence δ was a discriminator between normal forms; see [Church, 1935] or [Church, 1941, §20], and the review [Curry, 1937].) But the new system was too weak for much mathematics and seems to have played no role in Church’s future work. In fact in the later 1930s Church retreated from the task of designing a general type-free logic to the much less ambitious one of re-formulating simple type theory on a λ-calculus base. In this he succeeded, publishing a smooth and natural system in [Church, 1940] that has been the foundation for much type-theoretic work since (see §5.1 and §8 below).

4.2　20 世纪 30 年代的 CL

4.2 CL in the 1930s

柯里在整个 30 年代继续早期研究：在尽可能一般背景中分析抽象、全称量化与蕴涵的最基本性质。他分析克林-罗瑟对丘奇系统不一致性的证明，到 1942 年，在自己的逻辑系统中发现一个极简单的不一致性，如今称柯里悖论 [Curry, 1942b]。这表明，在极一般背景下，组合完备性与无限制使用蕴涵某些简单性质不相容。¹⁹

The work of Curry through the 1930s continued his earlier study of the most basic properties of abstraction, universal quantification and implication in as general a setting as possible. He analysed the Kleene-Rosser proof of the inconsistency of Church’s system, and by 1942 he had found in a logical system of his own a very simple inconsistency now called the Curry paradox [Curry, 1942b]. This showed combinatory completeness to be incompatible with unrestricted use of certain simple properties of implication in a very general setting.19

柯里还开始发展类型论。档案中有 1928 年类型笔记；他于 1930 年作报告，并写成 [Curry, 1934a; Curry, 1936]。²⁰ 其方法不同于罗素及丘奇后来方法：向基于组合子的逻辑加入函数性常量 F，希望 Fabf 大致表示 (∀x)(x∈a⊃fx∈b)。²¹ 与丘奇不同，Fabf 不蕴涵 f 的定义域恰为 a，只要求包含 a。在柯里函数性理论中，一个项可有无限多个类型；其 F 公理确实给每个原子组合子 B、C、K、W 赋无限多个类型表达式。例如 [Curry, 1934a, p.586] 或 [Curry, 1936, p.378]：

Curry also began to develop a type theory. A note on types dated 1928 occurs in his files, and he gave a talk on this topic in 1930 and wrote it up in [Curry, 1934a] and [Curry, 1936].20 He took a different approach from Russell and from the one that Church later took: he added to his combinator-based logic a functionality constant F, with the intention that an expression such as Fabf should mean approximately (∀x)(x ∈a ⊃fx ∈b).21 This approach differed from Church’s in that Curry’s Fabf did not imply that the domain of f was exactly a, only that it included a. In Curry’s functionality-theory a term could have an infinite number of types. Indeed his axioms for F included axioms assigning an infinity of type-expressions to each atomic combinator B, C, K, W; for example (from [Curry, 1934a, p.586] or [Curry, 1936, p.378])

公理（FB）：(∀x,y,z) F(Fxy)(F(Fzx)(Fzy)) B。

Ax. (FB): (∀x, y, z)  F(Fxy)(F(Fzx)(Fzy))  B  .

18　见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §5S]。图灵不动点组合子满足 Yx▷x(Yx)，而罗森布卢姆和柯里的只满足“=”，不满足“▷”。更多见 [Barendregt, 1981, §§6.1,6.5; Hindley and Seldin, 1986, §3B; Curry et al., 1972, §11F7]。19　柯里悖论一般形式如下，参见 [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, pp.268–269]。设逻辑系统 S 有表达式类 P，满足 X,Y∈P⇒(X⊃Y)∈P，有公理模式 (X⊃(X⊃Y))⊃(X⊃Y)，X,Y∈P，以及规则（Eq）X,X=Y⊢Y、（MP）X,X⊃Y⊢Y。对每个 Z∈P，令 Z′=λy.((yy)⊃((yy)⊃Z))，y 不出现于 Z，令 Z*=Z′Z′。易证 Z*=Z*⊃(Z*⊃Z)=(Z*⊃(Z*⊃Z))⊃(Z*⊃Z)。假设对所有 Z，有 Z∈P⇒Z*∈P，则 P 中每个 Z 均在 S 可证。因为公理给出 ⊢(Z*⊃(Z*⊃Z))⊃(Z*⊃Z)，两次用 Eq 得 ⊢Z*⊃(Z*⊃Z) 与 ⊢Z*，再两次用 MP 得 ⊢Z。柯里在 [Curry, 1934a, pp.588–589] 对罗素悖论的处理与此颇相似。20　感谢 J. P. Seldin 提供柯里 1928 年笔记 T 28.12.13.A，重印于 [Seldin, 2002]。21　仅为近似；见 [Seldin, 2007] 对 Π、F 的讨论。

18Stated in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §5S]. By the way, Turing’s fixed-point combinator satisfied Yx ▷x(Yx), but Rosenbloom’s and Curry’s only satisfied “=” not “▷”. For more on fixed-point combinators, see [Barendregt, 1981, §§6.1, 6.5], [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, §3B], or [Curry et al., 1972, §11F7]. 19A general form of Curry’s paradox runs thus, cf. [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, pp.268–269]. Suppose a system S of logic has a class P of expressions such that X, Y ∈P =⇒(X ⊃Y ) ∈P, and has an axiom-scheme (X ⊃(X ⊃Y )) ⊃(X ⊃Y ) (for all X, Y ∈P), and rules (Eq): X, X =Y ⊢Y and (MP): X, X ⊃Y ⊢Y . For every Z ∈P, let Z′ be λy.((yy) ⊃((yy) ⊃Z)), where y does not occur in Z, and let Z∗be Z′Z′. It is easy to prove that Z∗= Z∗⊃(Z∗⊃Z) = (Z∗⊃(Z∗⊃Z)) ⊃(Z∗⊃Z). Suppose Z ∈P =⇒Z∗∈P, for all Z. Then every Z in P is provable in S. Because, the axiom-scheme gives ⊢(Z∗⊃(Z∗⊃Z)) ⊃(Z∗⊃Z), so by rule (Eq) twice, ⊢Z∗⊃(Z∗⊃Z) and ⊢Z∗; hence by (MP) twice, ⊢Z. (Curry’s treatment of the Russell paradox in [Curry, 1934a, pp.588–589] was interestingly similar to this argument.) 20We are very grateful to J. P. Seldin for showing us Curry’s 1928 note (T 28.12.13.A, reproduced in [Seldin, 2002]). 21But only approximately; see the discussions of Π and F in [Seldin, 2007].

柯里方法如今称为多态。相比之下，丘奇式类型论中，对项 f:α→β，其定义域恰为 α，类型唯一为 α→β。

Curry’s approach was what we would now call polymorphic. In contrast, in a Church-style type-theory, for a term f α→β, its domain would be exactly α and its type would be uniquely α →β.

柯里使用类型还在另一点不同于罗素与丘奇：罗素引入类型表达式，以限制语言、避免某些麻烦表达式；丘奇后来目标相同。但柯里允许所有组合子进入语言，不论麻烦与否，只给其中一些赋标签，说明其行为。

Curry’s use of types differed from Russell and Church in another way. Russell had introduced type-expressions to help him restrict his language to avoid certain troublesome expressions, and Church later had the same aim. But Curry allowed into his language all combinators, troublesome or placid, and simply assigned labels to some of them to tell us how they would behave.

另一位早期采用多态类型方法者是奎因。[Quine, 1937, pp.78–79] 建议把集合论概括公理模式限制于他所称分层、如今会称为可赋型的公式，并在第 79 页清晰论证多态方法。其系统如今称 NF，即“新基础”。历史超出本文范围；分层与可赋型等价由 [Newman, 1943, Thm.4] 证明，NF 一般研究见 [Forster, 1995]。

Another early user of a polymorphic approach to types was Quine. In [Quine, 1937, pp. 78–79] he proposed restricting the comprehension axiom-scheme in set theory to formulae he called stratified, but which we would now call typable, and on p.79 he made a very clear argument in favour of a polymorphic approach. His proposed system is nowadays called NF for “New Foundations”. Its history is beyond our scope, but the equivalence between stratification and typability was proved in [Newman, 1943, Thm. 4], and a general study of NF is in [Forster, 1995].

柯里于 30 年代注意到命题即类型对应。最简单形式是：可赋给组合子的类型表达式，与直觉主义逻辑可证蕴涵公式之间的同构。至少对原子组合子，最早线索见档案中 1930 年笔记，印行于 [Curry, 1934a; Curry, 1934b]。²² 在两文第 588 页与第 850 页，他用组合子名给蕴涵公理命名，例如：

The propositions-as-types correspondence was noticed by Curry in the 1930s. In its simplest form this is an isomorphism between the type-expressions assignable to combinators and the implicational formulas provable in intuitionistic logic. The first hint of this, at least for atomic combinators, occurs in a note dated 1930 in Curry’s files, and appeared in print in his papers [Curry, 1934a] and [Curry, 1934b].22 In [Curry, 1934a, p.588] and [Curry, 1934b, p.850] he gave his axioms for implication the names of combinators, for example

公理（PB）：(∀x,y,z) P(Pxy)(P(Pzx)(Pzy))。

Ax. (PB): (∀x, y, z) P(Pxy)(P(Pzx)(Pzy))  ,

其中 Pxy 是 x⊃y 的记法。“PB”显然源自与公理 FB 的相似性，尽管直到 [Curry, 1942a, p.60, fn.28] 才在印行文献明确陈述 P 到 F 的翻译。[Curry, 1936, pp.391–394] 证明，若定义：

where Pxy was his notation for x ⊃y. His name “(PB)” was clearly motivated by the axiom’s similarity to Ax. (FB), although he did not state the P-to-F translation explicitly in print until later, [Curry, 1942a, p.60, footnote 28]. In [Curry, 1936, pp.391–394] he proved that if F was defined as

F≡[a,b,f].(∀x)(ax⊃b(fx))，

F ≡ [a, b, f]. (∀x)(ax ⊃b(fx))

在含组合子及适当 ∀ 规则的系统中，每个 F 公理都可由相应 P 公理推出。

in a system with combinators and suitable rules for ∀, then each F-axiom would be deducible from the corresponding P-axiom.

对非原子组合子，柯里多年后才提及命题即类型对应；首次包含复合组合子的讨论是 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9E]。那里明确证明：若在某个受限系统中赋型，把 F 替换为 P 后，可赋给组合子的全部类型表达式集，恰好成为直觉主义蕴涵逻辑全部可证公式集。该系统没有 ∀，以公理模式替代此前原子组合子的 F 公理，主要规则为：

For non-atomic combinators the propositions-as-types correspondence was not mentioned by Curry until many years later; in fact the first discussion that included composite combinators was in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9E]. There it was proved explicitly that when F was replaced by P, the set of all type-expressions assignable to the combinators became exactly the set of all formulas provable in intuitionistic implicational logic, provided type-assignment was made in a certain restricted system. This system had no ∀, had axiom-schemes instead of the previous F-axioms for the atomic combinators, and its main rule was:

规则 F：FABF，AX ⊢ B(FX)。

Rule F: FABF, AX ⊢ B(FX)

若把 F 换为 P，并删去 F、X、FX，它就成为分离规则。系统仅有另一条规则，保证相互可转换项获得相同类型，见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9A3, Rule Eq′]；它不影响可赋类型的总集合，后来命题即类型研究中将其去除。去除后，人们看清赋型推导，即有类型项，与命题推导之间也有对应。此事实、λ 中的对应物，以及对应的一些可能扩展，由威廉·霍华德在 1969 年传阅、发表于 [Howard, 1980] 的手稿中指出。沿用该文，这一对应常称公式即类型，²³ 或柯里-霍华德同构。

(which would become the rule of modus ponens if F was replaced by P and F, X, FX were deleted). It had only one other rule, to ensure that interconvertible terms received the same types, [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9A3, Rule Eq′]; this rule did not affect the total set of assignable types, and was dropped in later studies of propositions-as-types. After dropping that rule, it became clear that there was also a correspondence between type-assignment deductions (or typed terms) and propositional deductions. This fact, and its analogue for λ, and some possible extensions of the correspondence, were pointed out by William Howard in a manuscript circulated in 1969 and published in [Howard, 1980]. The correspondence is often called formulae-as-types, following [Howard, 1980]23, or the Curry-Howard isomorphism.

22　感谢 J. P. Seldin 提供柯里 1930 年笔记 T 30.07.15.B，重印于 [Seldin, 2002]。[Curry, 1934b] 基于 1932 年报告。

22We thank J. P. Seldin for a copy of Curry’s 1930 note (T 30.07.15.B, reproduced in [Seldin, 2002]). The paper [Curry, 1934b] was based on a talk given in 1932.

60 年代，其他若干研究者也独立发现命题即类型对应；下文 §8.1.4 将进一步讨论。

The propositions-as-types correspondence was also discovered independently by several other workers in the 1960s; this period will be discussed further in §8.1.4 below.

部分由于命题即类型，[Curry and Feys, 1958] 作者把根岑方法用于类型论；这如今标准，当时却新颖。他们由此给出可赋型项正规化定理的第一个发表证明，见 §5.4。

Partly as a consequence of propositions-as-types, the authors of [Curry and Feys, 1958] were led to apply Gentzen’s methods to type-theory, an idea which is standard now but was new then. This gave them the first published proof of the normalization theorem for typable terms, see §5.4 below.

30 年代，另一位使用组合子的逻辑学家是耶鲁的弗雷德里克·菲奇。其工作始于博士论文 [Fitch, 1936]，描述基于组合子的无类型逻辑：通过禁止 X 含重复变量的某些抽象 [x1,…,xn].X，避免罗素悖论。随后数十年，他发展系统 CΔ，与论文系统很不同，但仍无类型、基于组合子，量化与抽象不受限制，见 [Fitch, 1963]。CΔ 定理集不是递归可枚举的，见第 87 页；但系统足以处理数学分析的重要部分，并有非有限主义一致性证明。他在学生教材 [Fitch, 1974] 中提出进一步修改的系统。

In the 1930s another logician who made use of combinators was Frederic Fitch in Yale University. Fitch’s work began with his doctoral thesis [Fitch, 1936], which described a type-free combinator-based logic that avoided Russell’s paradox by forbidding certain cases of abstraction [x1, ..., xn].X where X contained repeated variables. He then developed over several decades a system C∆, very different from the one in his thesis but still type-free and combinator-based, with unrestricted quantification and abstraction; see [Fitch, 1963]. The set of theorems of C∆was not recursively enumerable, [Fitch, 1963, p.87], but the system was adequate for a considerable part of mathematical analysis and had a (non-finitary) consistency proof. He presented a further modified system in his student-level textbook on CL, [Fitch, 1974].

顺便说，组合子并非消去约束变量的唯一方法；另一技术是代数逻辑。非常粗略地说，CL 把抽象代数化，后者把量化代数化。1936 年，奎因勾勒如何原则上用关系代数实现这一点 [Quine, 1936b]。这类代数更早已见于 [Peirce, 1883; Schröder, 1905, Vol.III]。1938 年，利奥波德·勒文海姆向阿尔弗雷德·塔尔斯基提出，全部数学可在皮尔士-施罗德代数中表达，见 [Löwenheim, 1940]。1941 年，塔尔斯基发展称为关系代数理论的二元关系抽象理论，并用它证明集合论可不用变量形式化，见 [Tarski, 1941; Tarski, 1953]。无变量集合论的详细形式化见 [Tarski and Givant, 1987]，该书在塔尔斯基 1983 年去世前不久完成。

Incidentally, combinators are not the only way of eliminating bound variables: an alternative technique is algebraic logic. Very roughly speaking, while CL algebraizes abstraction the latter algebraizes quantification. In 1936 Quine sketched how this could be done in principle by means of an algebra of relations, [Quine, 1936b]. (Such algebras had been published earlier in [Peirce, 1883] and [Schröder, 1905, Vol.III].) In 1938 Leopold Löwenheim proposed to Alfred Tarski that all of mathematics could be expressed in a Peirce-Schröder algebra, see [Löwenheim, 1940], and in 1941 Tarski developed an abstract theory of binary relations, called the theory of relation algebras, and used it to prove that set theory could be formalized without variables, see [Tarski, 1941] and [Tarski, 1953]. A detailed formalization of set theory without variables was carried out in the book [Tarski and Givant, 1987], completed just before Tarski died in 1983.

不过，要形式化一般一阶逻辑，塔尔斯基理论仍不足。为补救，他与合作者自 40 年代末发展柱状代数理论。保罗·哈尔莫斯 1956 年的多元代数，也给出类似约束变量代数处理，见 [Halmos, 1962]。[Bernays, 1958] 与 [Craig, 1974] 也对代数方法作出重要贡献。威廉·克雷格此前曾贡献 CL，见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§5H,7E]。

However, for a formalization of first-order logic in general, Tarski’s theory was inadequate, and to remedy this he and his co-workers developed the theory of cylindric algebras from the late 1940s onward. A similar algebraic treatment of bound variables was also given by the polyadic algebras of Paul Halmos in 1956, see [Halmos, 1962]. Other significant contributions to the algebraic approach were made by [Bernays, 1958], and [Craig, 1974]. William Craig had previously contributed to CL, see [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§5H, 7E].

代数逻辑历史更多见 [Tarski and Givant, 1987] 与 [Givant and Andréka, 2002] 的引言。[Quine, 1972] 很好比较代数逻辑与无类型 CL。从基于 ZF、NBG 等标准集合论的语义看，代数逻辑的优点是保留一阶逻辑限制，因而保留语义简单性；如前所述，无限制无类型组合子在标准集合论中难解释得多。

For more on the history of algebraic logic, see the introductions to [Tarski and Givant, 1987] and [Givant and Andréka, 2002]. A good comparison of algebraic logic with type-free CL is in [Quine, 1972]. From the viewpoint of a semantics based on a standard set theory such as ZF or NBG, an advantage of algebraic logic is that it retains the restrictions of first-order logic, and therefore its semantic simplicity; as noted earlier, unrestricted type-free combinators are much harder to interpret in standard set theories.

数学家卡尔·门格尔采取另一代数方法。

A different algebraic approach was taken by the mathematician Karl Menger.

23　不过，霍华德本人后来批评此名称，称“类型应视为抽象对象，而公式是类型的名称”[Howard, 1980, p.479]。顺便说，[Frege, 1879, §13] 的蕴涵公理是 K、S、C 的主类型。通过命题即类型，也许可把它们看作对组合子基概念的部分预见，至少对可赋型项而言。但这可能牵强，特别是 [Frege, 1879] 没有显式完备性概念。

23Although this name was later criticised by Howard himself who said “a type should be regarded as an abstract object whereas a formula is the name of a type”, [Howard, 1980, p.479]. By the way, in [Frege, 1879, §13] the axioms for implication were the principal types of K, S and C. These might be seen, via propositions-as-types, as a partial anticipation of the concept of combinator basis, at least for the typable terms. But this is probably far-fetched; in particular, [Frege, 1879] had no explicit concept of completeness.

门格尔方法把受限形式的函数复合代数化，发表于 40—60 年代一系列论文，例如 [Menger, 1944; Menger, 1964]。但其系统比塔尔斯基系统更受限制，已发展的数学也较少，见评论 [Lercher, 1966]。

Menger’s method involved an algebraization of a limited form of function-composition, and was expounded by him in a series of papers from the 1940s to the ’60s, for example [Menger, 1944] and [Menger, 1964]. But his system is more restricted than Tarski’s, and less mathematics has been developed in it; see the review [Lercher, 1966].

5　1940 与 1950 年代：巩固时期

5 1940s and 1950s: Consolidation

20 世纪 30 年代中期以后的许多年里，λ 和 CL 都没有引起逻辑学界普遍的兴趣。这两个系统后来会在高阶逻辑研究中发挥自然的作用，但在那个早期阶段，该主题的形式元理论几乎还未开始发展。λ 形式体系原本是为详细描述代换与应用而设计的，用于其他目的时显得繁重得令人却步。它曾给出判定问题不可解的第一个证明，但图灵通过其计算机器模型给出的证明清晰得多；甚至克林在后来发展可计算性理论时，也更偏好其他系统。

For many years after the mid-1930s, neither λ nor CL attracted much interest among logicians in general. Both systems would later play a natural role in studies of higher-order logic, but the formal metatheory of that topic had hardly begun to be developed at that early period. The λ-formalism had been designed to describe substitution and application in detail and seemed off-puttingly heavy for other purposes. It had given the first proof of the unsolvability of the Entscheidungsproblem, but Turing’s proof via his machine model of computation was much more transparent, and even Kleene preferred other systems for his subsequent development of computability theory.

不过，在阐述方面，丘奇以其易读的入门教材 [Church, 1941]，在向非专业读者介绍 λ 上迈出了一步。保罗·罗森布卢姆也在综合逻辑教材中加入了 CL 一节 [Rosenbloom, 1950, Ch.III, §4]。这两个主题在技术方面也取得进展，为后来的扩展奠定了基础。这些进展大致可分为四个主题：简单类型论、抽象归约理论、CL 与 λ 中的归约，以及推理系统。

However, on the expository side, an advance in opening up λ to non-specialists was made by Church with his readable introductory textbook [Church, 1941]. And Paul Rosenbloom included a section on CL in his general logic textbook [Rosenbloom, 1950, Ch.III, §4]. Advances were made in both subjects on the technical side too, and these helped to lay a foundation for the expansion that came later. They can be split roughly into four main themes: simple type theory, abstract reduction theory, reductions in CL and λ, and illative systems.

5.1　简单类型论

5.1 Simple type theory

丘奇的简单类型论是一个以函数为基础的系统，源自弗兰克·拉姆齐和莱昂·赫维斯泰克在 20 世纪 20 年代为简化 [Russell and Whitehead, 1913] 的类型论而提出的思想。²⁴ 丘奇于 1937—1938 年在普林斯顿讲授这一系统，随后发表于 [Church, 1940]。图灵听过这些课，后来作出了一些技术贡献，包括首次证明该系统的弱正规化定理，不过这一证明近 40 年未发表，见 [Turing, 1980] 及下文 §8.2。

Church’s simple type theory was a function-based system, stemming from ideas of Frank Ramsey and Leon Chwistek in the 1920s, for simplifying the type theory of [Russell and Whitehead, 1913].24 Church lectured on his system in Princeton in 1937–38 before publishing it in [Church, 1940], and his lectures were attended by Turing, who later made some technical contributions. These included the first proof of the system’s weak normalization theorem, although this lay unpublished for nearly 40 years, see [Turing, 1980] and §8.2 below.

从 20 世纪 40 年代起，一系列普林斯顿博士论文分析并扩展了丘奇的系统。其中最著名的或许是莱昂·亨金 1947 年的论文，发表于 [Henkin, 1950]，以及彼得·安德鲁斯的论文，发表于 [Andrews, 1965]。亨金给出了类型化 λ 模型的两种定义，即标准模型与一般模型，²⁵ 并证明了简单类型论相对于一般模型的完备性。安德鲁斯扩展丘奇的系统，建立了一套自然流畅的超限类型理论。

Church’s system was analysed and extended in a series of Princeton Ph.D. theses from the 1940s onward, of which perhaps the best known are Leon Henkin’s in 1947, published in [Henkin, 1950], and Peter Andrews’, published in [Andrews, 1965]. Henkin gave two definitions of model of typed λ (standard and general models),25 and proved the completeness of simple type theory with respect to general models. Andrews extended Church’s system to make a smooth theory of tranfinite types.

丘奇自己的兴趣在 1941 年教材出版后有所变化。他把大量时间和精力投入符号逻辑协会的事务；他于 1936 年参与创办该协会，此时仍是其主要推动者。他也负责编辑《符号逻辑杂志》，尤其是内容极为全面的书评与书目部分。在研究方面，他开始关注内涵逻辑和相关蕴涵逻辑。对于前者，他在 [Church, 1951a] 与 [Church, 1973] 中给出公理化，两者均以其类型论的修改版本为基础。对于相关蕴涵，他在 [Church, 1951b] 中给出的表述，在命题即类型对应下，实质上等价于纯 λI 项的简单类型论。

Church’s own interests underwent some evolution after his 1941 textbook appeared. Much of his time and energy was spent on the business of the Association for Symbolic Logic, of which he had been a co-founder in 1936 and in which he was still a driving force, and on editing the Journal of Symbolic Logic, especially its very comprehensive Reviews and Bibliography sections. On the research side he became interested in intensional logic and the logic of relevant implication. For the former he made axiomatizations in [Church, 1951a] and [Church, 1973], both based on modifications of his type theory. For relevant implication he gave a formulation, in [Church, 1951b], which was essentially equivalent, under the propositions-as-types correspondence, to the simple type theory of pure λI-terms.

24　关于丘奇系统的起源以及图灵贡献的概述，见 [Gandy, 1977]。25　两类模型的区别见下文 §8.1.2；细节见 [Henkin, 1950, pp.83–85]。

24See [Gandy, 1977] for a sketch of the origins of Church’s system (and of Turing’s contributions). 25See §8.1.2 below for the difference between these; details are in [Henkin, 1950, pp.83–85].

5.2　抽象归约理论

5.2 Abstract reduction theory

关于抽象归约理论的最早出版物，可能是数学家阿克塞尔·图厄讨论有限树重写的论文 [Thue, 1910]。²⁶ 图厄的论文甚至包含一个引理：如果每次收缩都严格减小某个有限度量，那么局部合流性蕴涵合流性 [Thue, 1910, §4 Satz 3]。这与 30 年后纽曼的引理十分相似：若所有归约路径都有限，则局部合流性蕴涵合流性 [Newman, 1942, Thm. 3]。

Probably the first publication on abstract reduction-theory was a paper by the mathematician Axel Thue on rewriting finite trees [Thue, 1910].26 Thue’s paper even included a lemma that local confluence implies confluence if every contraction strictly decreases a finite measure [Thue, 1910, §4 Satz 3], very like Newman’s lemma of 30 years later that local confluence implies confluence if all reduction paths are finite [Newman, 1942, Thm. 3].

不过，这一领域抽象研究的主要启发来自 [Newman, 1942]。20 世纪 20 年代，纽曼证明了组合拓扑中的一个关键合流性猜想；十年后，他对逻辑的兴趣，可能还有他与图灵的联系，使他开始研究 λ 的合流性定理，以及丘奇和罗瑟在证明中使用的剩余项方法 [Church and Rosser, 1936]。²⁷ [Newman, 1942] 的第一部分在非常一般的环境中研究合流性；第二部分则给出关于剩余项概念的一组抽象公理，并由此推出合流性。不幸的是，由于一个涉及变量的错误，他的公理未能把 λ 包含为特例。约 20 年后，戴维·施罗尔在博士论文 [Schroer, 1965] 中首次纠正了这一问题。

But the main inspiration for abstract work in this field was [Newman, 1942]. In the 1920s, Newman proved a crucial confluence conjecture in combinatorial topology, and a decade later his interest in logic (and probably his link with Turing) led him to study the confluence theorem for λ and the method of residuals that Church and Rosser had used in its proof [Church and Rosser, 1936].27 The first part of [Newman, 1942] was a study of confluence in a very general setting; in the second, Newman gave a set of abstract axioms about the concept of residual, from which he deduced confluence. Unfortunately, due to an error involving variables, his axioms failed to cover λ as a special case. This was first corrected about 20 years later by David Schroer in his Ph.D. thesis [Schroer, 1965].

纽曼的分析在 20 世纪 70 年代导向了一般方法，用来为加入额外算子，例如原始递归算子的 λ 系统，证明合流性及标准化等其他关键定理。（这些结果的概述见 [Hindley, 1978b, pp.269–270]。）两项有影响的合流性抽象研究是 [Rosen, 1973] 和 [Huet, 1980]。

Newman’s analysis led in the 1970s to general methods for proving confluence and other key theorems, such as standardization, for systems of λ augmented by extra operators, for example a primitive recursion operator. (A summary of these results is in [Hindley, 1978b, pp.269–270].) Two influential abstract studies of confluence were [Rosen, 1973] and [Huet, 1980].

20 世纪 80 年代，扬-威廉·克洛普从其洞见深刻的论文 [Klop, 1980] 开始，彻底重组并发展了归约的抽象理论。由此演化出组合归约系统与项重写系统这两类重要的抽象理论。今天，这些领域仍在积极研究中，但它们已远超 λ 与 CL 的边界；[Klop, 1992]、[Klop et al., 1993] 提供了很好的综述，[Terese, 2003] 则是全面的教材，因此这里不再详述。

In the 1980s the abstract theory of reductions was re-organised completely and developed by Jan-Willem Klop, beginning with his penetrating thesis [Klop, 1980], and from this work the important abstract theories of combinatory reduction systems and term rewriting systems evolved. These are being actively pursued today, but go far beyond the boundaries of λ and CL and are the subjects of good surveys in [Klop, 1992] and [Klop et al., 1993] and a comprehensive textbook [Terese, 2003], so we shall not give details of them here.

5.3　CL 与 λ 中的归约

5.3 Reductions in CL and λ

除抽象归约理论外，对两种最重要的具体归约的分析也有进展，即 CL 中的弱归约与 λ 中的 β 归约。如前所述，丘奇和罗瑟在包含合流性证明的论文 [Church and Rosser, 1936] 中，已经开始了这项研究。

Besides abstract reduction theory, there was also progress in analysing the two most important concrete reductions, weak reduction in CL and β-reduction in λ. As mentioned earlier, Church and Rosser had begun the study of these in the paper containing their confluence proof, [Church and Rosser, 1936].

1950 年后，柯里继续了他们的计划。与许多其他学者一样，他的研究在第二次世界大战期间完全中断，直到 20 世纪 40 年代较晚时才恢复势头。此后约 20 年，他把主要精力用于编写两部包含大量新成果的大型专著，即 [Curry and Feys, 1958] 与 [Curry et al., 1972]。其中一部分工作是详细研究 λβ 归约，包括首次证明其标准化定理 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §4E1]。他还首次研究了 λβη 归约，包括合流性与 η 延后定理 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §4D]。（不过，他对后一定理的证明有一处缺口，15 年后由罗伯特·内德佩尔特发现并补上，见 [Nederpelt, 1973, Thm. 7.28]。）

After 1950 their programme was continued by Curry. His research had been suspended completely during the Second World War, like that of many other academics, and did not recover momentum until quite late in the 1940s. Then for about 20 years he put his main effort into compiling two large monographs containing much new work, [Curry and Feys, 1958] and [Curry et al., 1972]. Part of this effort consisted of a detailed study of λβ-reduction, including the first proof of its standardization theorem, [Curry and Feys, 1958, §4E1]. He also made the first study of λβη-reduction, including its confluence and η-postponement theorems, [Curry and Feys, 1958, §4D]. (But his proof of the latter contained a gap, which was noticed and remedied fifteen years later by Robert Nederpelt, [Nederpelt, 1973, Thm. 7.28].)

1965 年，施罗尔的论文首次陈述并证明了 λβ 的展开有限性定理 [Schroer, 1965, Part I, Thm. 6.20]。不过，他的工作从未发表，直到约八年后，这一定理才被重新发现，见下文 §7.2。

In 1965 the thesis of Schroer gave the first statement and proof of the finiteness-of-developments theorem for λβ, [Schroer, 1965, Part I, Thm. 6.20]. But Schroer’s work was never published and this theorem was not re-discovered until about 8 years later, see §7.2 below.

CL 中的弱归约比 λ 中的 β 归约简单得多，[Curry et al., 1972, §11B] 对它作了直接而系统的分析。

Weak reduction in CL is much simpler than β-reduction in λ, and was given a straightforward systematic analysis in [Curry et al., 1972, §11B].

柯里还为 CL 定义了一种类似 λβη 归约的强归约 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §6F]。²⁸ 其目的是使后面对逻辑的讨论，同样适用于基于 CL 和基于 λ 的语言。强归约确实在相当程度上做到了这一点，但其元理论后来被发现很复杂。虽然罗杰·欣德利和布鲁斯·勒彻在 20 世纪 60 年代作出简化，带来一些改进，但最终看来，凡是可以使用强归约的地方，直接用 λ 代替 CL 大概更容易。因此，1972 年后强归约或多或少被放弃。不过，20 世纪 80 年代，穆罕默德·梅兹吉什定义并研究了一种 β 强归约，见 [Mezghiche, 1984]。研究一种在抽象下保持、又避免绑定变量复杂性的组合归约，这一想法至今仍诱人地具有吸引力。

Curry also defined a strong reduction for CL analogous to λβη-reduction, [Curry and Feys, 1958, §6F].28 His purpose was to make later discussions of logic applicable equally to languages based on CL and on λ. Strong reduction indeed allowed this to be done to a significant extent, but its metatheory turned out to be complicated, and although simplifications were made by Roger Hindley and Bruce Lercher in the 1960s which produced some improvement, it eventually seemed that wherever strong reduction could be used, it would probably be easier to use λ instead of CL. Strong reduction was therefore more or less abandoned after 1972. However, in the 1980s a β-strong reduction was defined and studied by Mohamed Mezghiche, see [Mezghiche, 1984], and the idea of working with a combinatory reduction which is preserved by abstraction but avoids the complexity of bound variables is still tantalisingly attractive.

26　见 [Steinby and Thomas, 2000]，其中包含 [Thue, 1910] 的英文摘要。27　若项 X 包含可归约式出现 R₁,…,Rₙ，且通过收缩 R₁ 得到 X ▷₁ Y，则 Y 的某些部分可在某种意义上视为 R₂,…,Rₙ 的后代，称为它们的剩余项。λ 中的细节见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§4B1–2]、[Hindley and Seldin, 1986, Def.A1.3] 或 [Church and Rosser, 1936, pp.473–475]。若归约 Y 时收缩其中一个剩余项，再收缩这些剩余项的剩余项，如此继续，则归约 X ▷₁ Y ▷… 称为 R₁,…,Rₙ 的展开。若展开有限，且最后的项不再包含 R₁,…,Rₙ 的任何剩余项，就称为完全展开。

26See [Steinby and Thomas, 2000], which contains an English summary of [Thue, 1910]. 27If a term X contains redex-occurrences R1, ..., Rn, and X ▷1 Y by contracting R1, certain parts of Y can be considered to be descendants of R2, ..., Rn in a sense; they are called residuals of R2, ..., Rn; for details in λ, see [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§4B1–2] or [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, Def. A1.3], or [Church and Rosser, 1936, pp.473–475]. If we reduce Y by contracting one of these residuals, and then a residual of these residuals, etc., the reduction X ▷1 Y ▷... is called a development of R1, ..., Rn. If it is finite and its last term contains no residuals of R1, ..., Rn, it is called complete.

5.4　推理系统

5.4 Illative systems

从纯 CL 与 λ 转向其应用：尽管类型论在 20 世纪 50 年代取得重要进展，一些逻辑学家仍认为类型限制强于必要程度，无类型高阶逻辑依然值得进一步研究。这类逻辑的大多数系统都以某种形式包含组合子的类似物，不过这里仅提及 CL 或 λ 更突出的那些系统。

Turning from pure to applied CL and λ: although type-theory made important advances in the 1950s, some logicians felt that type-restrictions were stronger than necessary, and that type-free higher-order logic was still worth further study. Most systems of such logic contain analogues of combinators in some form, but here we shall only mention those in which CL or λ were more prominent.

柯里关于应用系统的工作，发表于 [Curry and Feys, 1958] 和 [Curry et al., 1972]。他把这类系统称为 illative，来自意为“被推断”的拉丁词 illatum。虽然此时他研究的一个主要主题是类型与项的关系，但他从未放弃对无类型逻辑的兴趣。

Curry’s work on applied systems (which he called illative, from the Latin “illatum” for “inferred”) was published in the books [Curry and Feys, 1958] and [Curry et al., 1972]. He never gave up his interest in type-free logic, although one of his main themes of study at this time was the relation between types and terms.

在他与罗伯特·费斯合著的 1958 年著作中，推理系统部分最重要的贡献，或许是把根岑的技术引入类型论。如 §4.2 所述，这种方法在 1958 年并不像今天这样显然。费斯曾在 [Feys, 1946a; Feys, 1946b] 中论述组合逻辑与自然演绎；柯里则于 1948 年在圣母大学的一系列一阶逻辑讲座中强调根岑方法的重要性。在 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9F] 中，他们提出基于相继式的类型赋予系统，并证明其切消去定理，由此推出简单类型论的弱正规化定理 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9F6, Cor. 9F9.2]；这是该定理首次发表的证明。（其他证明将在 §8.2 讨论。）该书还描述了其他类型论性质，包括 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9E] 中的命题即类型对应，见上文 §4.2 及下文 §8.1.4，以及 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §8S2] 中与语法范畴的联系，见下文 §8.1.1。

In his 1958 book with Robert Feys, perhaps the authors’ most important contribution in the illative sections was to introduce Gentzen’s techniques into type theory. As we mentioned in §4.2, this approach was not as obvious in 1958 as it is now. Feys had written on combinatory logic and on Natural Deduction in [Feys, 1946a; Feys, 1946b], and Curry had emphasized the importance of Gentzen’s methods in a series of lectures on first-order logic at the University of Notre Dame in 1948. In [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9F] they presented a sequent-based system of type-assignment and a proof of a cut-elimination theorem for this system, from which they deduced the weak normalization theorem for simple type theory [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9F6, Cor. 9F9.2]; this was the first published proof of this theorem. (Other proofs will be discussed in §8.2.) Further type-theoretic properties described in that book included the propositions-as-types correspondence in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9E], see §4.2 above and §8.1.4 below, and the link with grammatical categories in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §8S2], see §8.1.1 below.

在 [Curry et al., 1972] 中，柯里的主要目标是在 λ 与 CL 的环境中，以尽可能少的限制、强调构造性方法，对基本逻辑概念作彻底分析。²⁹ 该书详细比较了基于三种不同概念的逻辑系统：(i) 上文 §4.2 所述的函数性常量 F；(ii) Ξ，其中 ΞAB 大致等价于 (∀x)(Ax ⊃ Bx)；(iii) ∀ 与 ⊃。这些系统的表述极大受益于乔纳森·塞尔丁在论文 [Seldin, 1968] 中引入的结果与简化，不过书中讨论了每个系统的许多变体，使整体处理相当复杂。

In [Curry et al., 1972], Curry’s main aim was to make a thorough analysis of basic logical concepts in the settings of λ and CL, with as few restrictions as possible and an emphasis on constructive methods.29 The book contained a detailed comparison of logical systems based on three different concepts: (i) F, the functionality constant described in §4.2 above, (ii) Ξ, where ΞAB was approximately equivalent to (∀x)(Ax ⊃Bx), and (iii) ∀and ⊃. The formulation of these systems benefitted greatly from results and simplifications introduced by Jonathan Seldin in his thesis [Seldin, 1968], but many variants of each system were discussed and the resulting treatment was rather complex.

柯里在 [Curry and Feys, 1958, Appendix A] 与 [Curry et al., 1972, §§15A1, 15A8] 中提出广义函数性概念 G。用 ∀ 和 ⊃ 表达，GABF 大致意指 (∀x)(Ax ⊃ (Bx)(Fx))。³⁰ 塞尔丁于 1975 年把这一提议发展成完整的高阶类型论，见 [Seldin, 1979] 或 [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, 1st edn., Ch.17]。后来他开始关注蒂埃里·科康更强的构造演算，见 [Seldin, 1997]，于是其柯里风格推理系统研究，逐渐与其他人的高阶系统研究汇合。下文 §8.3 将讨论这些内容。

A proposal for a generalized functionality concept G was made by Curry in [Curry and Feys, 1958, Appendix A] and [Curry et al., 1972, §§15A1, 15A8]. In terms of ∀and ⊃, GABF was intended to mean approximately (∀x)( Ax ⊃ (Bx)(Fx) ).30 This proposal was developed into a fully-fledged higher-order type-theory by Seldin in 1975, see [Seldin, 1979] or [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, 1st edn., Ch. 17]. Seldin later became interested in Thierry Coquand’s calculus of constructions, which was stronger, see [Seldin, 1997], and thus his work on Curry-style illative systems gradually merged with the work of others on higher-order systems. These will be discussed in §8.3 below.

回到 20 世纪 50 年代：当时，在无类型高阶逻辑的系统构造路线中，主要贡献者是菲奇。如 §4.2 所述，他的工作持续到 20 世纪 70 年代。他的一些技术思想得到其他研究者的赏识和采用，例如科拉多·伯姆，见 §6.3，以及所罗门·费弗曼，见 [Feferman, 1984, p.106]。但他的形式系统本身似乎很少被使用。

Returning to the 1950s: at that time the main contributor to the system-building approach to type-free higher-order logic was Fitch. As described in §4.2, his work on this continued into the 1970s. Some of his technical ideas were appreciated and used by other workers, for example Corrado Böhm, see §6.3, and Solomon Feferman, see [Feferman, 1984, p.106]. But his formal systems themselves seem to have been little used.

不过，其他人延续了系统构造的传统。1974 年，安德烈·肖万在其国家博士论文中，对无类型高阶逻辑及组合子的作用作了深思熟虑而有趣的分析，并描述了一个基于部分运算或部分函数概念的形式系统。不幸的是，该论文只有少量内容发表，见 [Chauvin, 1979]。

The system-building tradition was continued by others, however. In 1974 in his thesis for Doctorat d‘ Etat, André Chauvin produced a thoughtful and interesting analysis of type-free higher-order logic and the role of combinators, and described a formal system based on the notion of partial operation or function. Unfortunately only a little of this thesis was published, [Chauvin, 1979].

部分函数概念也是费弗曼从 1975 年至 20 世纪 80 年代多篇重要论文的基础。他考察无类型系统的可能性，特别是作为构造数学与范畴论基础的可能性，并提出若干具体系统，其中“部分”组合子发挥作用；例如见 [Feferman, 1975a; Feferman, 1975b; Feferman, 1977; Feferman, 1984] 及 [Aczel and Feferman, 1980]。

The concept of partial function was also behind several substantial papers by Feferman from 1975 through the 1980s, in which he examined the possibilities for type-free systems, particularly as foundations for constructive mathematics and for category theory, and proposed several actual systems in which “partial” combinators played a role, see for example [Feferman, 1975a; Feferman, 1975b; Feferman, 1977; Feferman, 1984] and [Aczel and Feferman, 1980].

彼得·阿克泽尔在一篇有影响的论文 [Aczel, 1980] 中，对紧密基于 λ 的数学基础作了分析。

An analysis of the foundations based closely on λ was made by Peter Aczel in an influential paper [Aczel, 1980].

从 1967 年起，马丁·邦德尔开展无类型推理系统研究。他仔细研究以往系统中出现的不一致性，提出若干避开这些问题的新系统，包括其中可以推出全部 ZF 集合论的系统；例如见依赖于 [Bunder, 1983b] 的 [Bunder, 1983a] 和 [Bunder, 1983c]。[Barendregt et al., 1993] 是一篇很好的简短概述。

From 1967 onward, work on type-free illative systems was undertaken by Martin Bunder. He made a careful study of the inconsistencies that had arisen in past systems, and proposed several new systems that avoided these, including some in which all of ZF set theory can be deduced; see, for example, [Bunder, 1983a] and [Bunder, 1983c], depending on [Bunder, 1983b]. A good short overview is in [Barendregt et al., 1993].

邦德尔和巴伦德雷赫特与维尔·德克斯、赫尔曼·赫弗斯合作，发表了其无类型推理系统与纯类型系统等其他高阶形式系统之间的有益比较，见 [Dekkers et al., 1998] 与 [Bunder et al., 2003]。

With Wil Dekkers and Herman Geuvers, Bunder and Barendregt published useful comparisons between their type-free illative systems and other higher-order formal systems such as Pure Type Systems: [Dekkers et al., 1998] and [Bunder et al., 2003].

1990 年，兰德尔·霍姆斯提出 λ 与 CL 系统，其中抽象受分层规则限制，灵感来自奎因“新基础”系统 [Quine, 1937] 的分层概念；见 [Holmes, 1991]，简短概述见 [Holmes, 1995]。

In 1990, Randall Holmes formulated systems of λ and CL in which abstraction was restricted by stratification rules inspired by the stratification concept in Quine’s “New Foundations” system [Quine, 1937]: see [Holmes, 1991], or [Holmes, 1995] for a short overview.

在莫斯科，独立于上述全部工作，亚历山大·库济切夫从 20 世纪 70 年代起提出一系列基于组合子的无类型系统；例如见 [Kuzichev, 1980; Kuzichev, 1983; Kuzichev, 1999] 及其中参考文献。

In Moscow, independently of all the above, a series of type-free combinatorbased systems was proposed by Alexander Kuzichev from the 1970s onward; see, for example, [Kuzichev, 1980; Kuzichev, 1983; Kuzichev, 1999] and the references therein.

28　在 CL 中，βη 强归约 >−βη 的定义，是在弱归约的定义规则上加入额外规则 (ξ)：若 X >−βη Y，且 x 为任意变量，则 [x]abcf.X >−βη [x]abcf.Y，其中 [x]abcf 按抽象算法 (abcf) 求值，见上文 §3。29　关于柯里研究计划的进一步评论，见 [Seldin, 1980a, pp.22–27]。

28In CL, βη-strong reduction >−βη is defined by adding to the rules defining weak reduction the extra rule (ξ): if X >−βηY and x is any variable, then [x]abcf.X >−βη [x]abcf.Y , where [x]abcf is evaluated by abstraction-algorithm (abcf), see §3 above. 29For further comments on Curry’s programme see [Seldin, 1980a, pp.22–27].

30　在基于 G 的系统中，函数输出值的类型可能依赖于其参数的值。具有这种性质的类型系统称为依赖类型系统。其他依赖类型系统将在 §8.3 提及。

30In a G-based system the type of the output-value of a function may depend on the value of its argument. Type systems with this property are called dependent. Other systems of dependent types are mentioned in §8.3.

6　编程语言

6 Programming languages

回到 20 世纪 60 年代：大约此时，λ 与 CL 开始吸引逻辑学界之外一个新群体的兴趣，即从事编程语言理论与实践的计算机科学家。最早公开建议把 CL 或 λ 直接用作编程语言的，或许是逻辑学家菲奇于 1957 年的工作，尽管只针对一类有限问题：在论文 [Fitch, 1958] 中，他展示了如何用 CL 表示时序逻辑电路。³¹ 不过，主要参与者是计算机科学家；下面将考察其中三位。

Returning to the 1960s: at about this time, λ and CL began to attract the interest of a new group outside the community of logicians, namely computer scientists involved in the theory and practice of programming languages. Perhaps the first published suggestion that CL or λ be used directly as a programming language came from the logician Fitch in 1957, although for only a restricted class of problems: in his paper [Fitch, 1958] he showed how sequential logic-circuits could be represented in CL.31 But the main characters concerned were computer scientists: we shall look at three of these.

6.1　约翰·麦卡锡与 LISP

6.1 John McCarthy and LISP

1956 至 1960 年间，美国的约翰·麦卡锡开发了计算机语言 LISP，它面向表处理，并采用今天称为函数式编程的程序组织方式。³²

From 1956 to ’60, John McCarthy in the U.S.A. developed the computer language LISP, which was a list-processing language with a function-abstraction facility. McCarthy’s goal was to apply LISP eventually to problems in non-numerical computation, especially in the newborn field of artificial intelligence, and to encourage the style of program-organization that is nowadays called functional programming.32

LISP 并不直接以 λ 演算为基础，尽管它受到 λ 的一些影响，其抽象记号甚至称为 LAMBDA。LISP 的代换过程并未定义得与 λ 完全相同，尤其是标识符按所谓“动态绑定”规则处理。这一选择简化了通过解释器实现 LISP 的工作，却使程序员使用绑定变量大为复杂。³³

LISP was not directly based on λ-calculus, although it owed something to λ (and its abstraction notation was even called “LAMBDA”). LISP’s substitution procedure was not defined exactly the same as in λ, in particular identifiers were handled according to so-called “dynamic binding” rules. This choice simplified LISP’s implementation by means of interpreters, but it greatly complicated the use of bound variables by the programmer.33
