麦卡锡强调函数式编程方法，也影响了理论研究。他的贡献包括两篇讨论计算理论数学基础的重要论文 [McCarthy, 1963b; McCarthy, 1963a]。其中系统地在函数式形式体系内使用条件表达式，递归定义作用于 LISP 的 S 表达式这类符号表达式上的函数，并深入研究其形式性质。以该形式体系表达的递归函数之间的等式，通过递归归纳法证明；下文 §8.1.2 还会结合斯科特将其推广为不动点归纳法的工作 [Scott, 1969e] 再次提到。除函数的递归定义外，[McCarthy, 1963a, §2.6] 也讨论集合的递归定义，例如把 S 表达式集合本身定义为方程 S ≅ A + (S × S) 的解。

McCarthy’s emphasis on a functional approach to programming also had influence on the theoretical side, where his contributions included two important papers on a mathematical basis for the theory of computation, [McCarthy, 1963b; McCarthy, 1963a]. There we find a systematic use of conditional expressions within a functional formalism for recursively defining functions over symbolic expressions like the S-expressions of LISP, together with a thorough study of their formal properties. Equalities among recursive functions expressed in that formalism were proved by means of recursion induction, to be mentioned again in §8.1.2 in connection with its generalization as fixed-point induction by Scott [Scott, 1969e]. Along with recursive definitions of functions, [McCarthy, 1963a, §2.6] also discussed recursive definitions of sets, like the definition of the set of S-expressions itself as a solution of the equation S ∼= A + (S × S).

31　我们最近获悉，菲奇实际上并非第一位：柯里在 1952 年的一次报告中已建议在编程中使用组合子 [Curry, 1954]；见 [Seldin, 2007]。32　这一名称至少可追溯到 P. M. 伍德沃德 1963 年的一篇 LISP 教程 [Fox, 1966, p.41]。33　相关评论见 [Moses, 1970]。LISP 背后的思想见 [McCarthy, 1960]、[McCarthy, 1963a]；截至 1996 年的历史见 [McCarthy, 1981]、[Steele and Gabriel, 1996]。修正了变量绑定问题的一个著名 LISP 后裔是约始于 1975 年的 Scheme，见 [Dybyg, 1996]。34　更精确地说，是加入了处理跳转的某些原始操作的 λ 项。

31We have recently learned that actually Fitch was not the first; Curry suggested using combinators in programming, in a talk in 1952, [Curry, 1954]; see [Seldin, 2007]. 32This name dates back at least to a 1963 tutorial on LISP by P. M. Woodward [Fox, 1966, p.41]. 33See [Moses, 1970] for comments. For the ideas behind LISP see [McCarthy, 1960], [McCarthy, 1963a]; for its history to 1996 see [McCarthy, 1981], [Steele and Gabriel, 1996]. One notable descendant of LISP with the variable-binding problem fixed is Scheme, see [Dybyg, 1996], dating from about 1975. 34More precisely, λ-terms extended with some primitive operations for dealing with jumps.

6.2　彼得·兰丁

6.2 Peter Landin

20 世纪 60 年代初，在英国，彼得·兰丁提出用 λ 项编码编程语言 Algol 60 的构造，见 [Landin, 1965]。³⁴ LISP 因动态绑定而难以与 λ 精确对应，但 Algol 的分块结构却与 λ 处理名称的方式完全匹配。事实上，兰丁的工作使人们可以把 λ 本身看作编程语言，而且是特别适合理论研究的语言。基于这一立场，并与 Algol 的翻译工作并行，他于 1963 年描述了一台抽象机器，用于归约被视为程序的 λ 项，即 [Landin, 1964, pp.316–318] 与 [Landin, 1966a] 中的 SECD 机。它是一个转换系统，状态由四部分构成：

In the early 1960s in England, Peter Landin proposed the use of λ-terms to code constructs of the programming language Algol 60, see [Landin, 1965].34 While for LISP a precise correspondence with λ was hindered by the use of dynamic binding, for Algol its block structure perfectly matched the way names were handled in λ. In fact, Landin’s work made it possible to look at λ itself as a programming language, and one especially suited for theoretical purposes. Taking this standpoint, and in parallel with his translation of Algol, Landin described in 1963 an abstract machine for reducing λ-terms considered as programs, the SECD-machine of [Landin, 1964, pp. 316–318] and [Landin, 1966a]. This consisted of a transition system whose states were made of four components:

  • 栈 S，用于保存求值的中间结果；

    a stack S for storing intermediate results of evaluation,

  • 环境 E，把值关联到自由标识符；

    an environment E, that associates values to free identifiers,

  • 控制部分 C，由驱动求值过程的表达式列表组成；

    a control C consisting of a list of expressions that drives the evaluation process,

  • 转储 D，记录一个形如 (S′,E′,C′,D′) 的完整机器状态。

    a dump D that records a complete state of the machine, of the form (S′, E′, C′, D′).

机器的转换规则实现了今天称为按值调用的求值顺序，因此其结果并不与 λ 项的最左优先归约完全一致。SECD 机的设计虽然受到更早的编程语言解释装置影响，尤其是 [Gilmore, 1963] 设计的 LISP 解释器，³⁵ 但它是第一个以形式方式呈现的此类机器。可以公允地说，它为把 λ 演算视作编程语言、研究其形式操作语义开辟了道路；十年后，这一研究从 [Plotkin, 1975] 开始得到系统阐述。

The transition rules of the machine implemented the order of evaluation now known as call by value, so their results did not agree exactly with those of leftmost-first reduction of λ-terms. While the design of the SECD-machine was influenced by similar earlier devices for the interpretation of programming languages, notably the LISP interpreter devised in [Gilmore, 1963],35 it was however the first to be presented in a formal way, and may fairly be said to have opened the way to the study of the formal operational semantics of λ-calculus considered as a programming language, that would find a systematic exposition a decade later starting with [Plotkin, 1975].

6.3　科拉多·伯姆：作为编程语言的 λ 演算

6.3 Corrado Böhm: λ-calculus as a programming language

在上述发展中，λ 与 CL 开始为编程语言理论贡献思想，其影响至今仍在持续；但反过来，编程经验在当时对 λ 与 CL 的“纯”理论影响有限。例外是科拉多·伯姆从 20 世纪 60 年代初起的工作。³⁶

In the above developments λ and CL began to contribute ideas to the theory of programming languages, and their influence is still active; but in the opposite direction, programming experience had only a limited impact on the “pure” theory of λ and CL at that time. The exception was the work of Corrado Böhm from the early 1960s onward.36

伯姆于 1951 年在苏黎世联邦理工学院取得博士学位，其论文包含历史上第一次用编译器自身语言完整描述该编译器的工作 [Böhm, 1954]。写论文时，他接触到伯奈斯，后者向他介绍了可计算函数的经典形式体系，包括图灵机以及波斯特和图厄系统。这些计算模型的多样性，使伯姆在随后几年寻求一种包容它们的形式体系。这一目标是他对 λ 演算产生兴趣的起源。他约于 1954 年通过丘奇教材 [Church, 1941] 接触 λ 演算，但直到 20 世纪 50 年代末，注意到 LISP 使用函数抽象后，才认识到其重要性。同事沃尔夫·格罗斯提醒他注意 [Curry and Feys, 1958] 及 [Rosenbloom, 1950] 第三章；1960—1961 年，两人一起开始研究组合子、λ 演算与递归函数论。到 1964 年，他们认识到，λ 项和组合子可直接作为“一种非常灵活的机器、程序和算法描述语言”的基础 [Böhm and Gross, 1966, p.36]。这就是 CUCH 语言的起点，即同时使用 CUrry 的组合子与 CHurch 的 λ 项的系统。它独立于大约同时从斯特雷奇和兰丁工作中涌现的思想而发展，但部分受到 [McCarthy, 1963a, §3] 中 λ 抽象记号，以及保罗·吉尔摩解释器 [Gilmore, 1963, §3] 中 β 归约用法的影响，尤其受到 [Fitch, 1958] 用组合子描述时序电路的影响。后者事实上构成 CUCH 最早的应用之一，与模拟电路的表示一起描述于 [Böhm and Giovannucci, 1964]；另一应用是玛丽莎·文图里尼·齐利在 1962—1964 年未发表的工作中，对艾弗森编程语言 APL 各运算的编码。³⁷

Böhm gained his Ph.D. in 1951 at the ETH in Zurich, with a thesis which included the first-ever description of a complete compiler in its own language, [Böhm, 1954]. While working on his thesis he came in contact with Bernays, who introduced him to the classical formalisms for computable functions. These included Turing machines and Post and Thue systems, but the variety of these models of computation led Böhm in the following years to search for an inclusive formalism. This aim was the origin of his interest in λ-calculus, with which he became acquainted around 1954 from Church’s textbook [Church, 1941], but whose importance he realized only at the end of the 1950s after noticing that functional abstraction was used in LISP. A colleague, Wolf Gross, drew his attention to [Curry and Feys, 1958] and to the third chapter of [Rosenbloom, 1950], and in the years 1960-61 Böhm and Gross together began to study combinators, λ-calculus and recursive function theory. By 1964 they had realized that λ-terms and combinators could be used directly as the basis of “a very flexible description language for machines, programs and algorithms”, [Böhm and Gross, 1966, p.36]. This was the starting point of the CUCH language (a system using both CUrry’s combinators and CHurch’s λ-terms), which was developed independently of the ideas that were emerging at about the same time from the work of Strachey and Landin, but was influenced partly by the use of λ-abstraction notation in [McCarthy, 1963a, §3] (and by the use of βreduction in Paul Gilmore’s interpreter [Gilmore, 1963, §3]), and especially by the use of combinators to describe sequential circuits in [Fitch, 1958]. The latter gave in fact one of the first applications of CUCH (described in [Böhm and Giovannucci, 1964] together with the representation of analogue circuits), as well as the encoding of the operations of Iverson’s programming language APL in unpublished work of Marisa Venturini Zilli in 1962-64.37

[Böhm and Dezani, 1972] 描述了一台抽象 CUCH 机；20 世纪 90 年代，斯特凡诺·格里尼在未发表的工作中实际实现了它。CUCH 机在 λ 演算抽象解释器史上值得关注，因为它使用最左优先归约策略，而不是兰丁那台更广为人知的 SECD 机所实现的按值调用顺序。

An abstract CUCH-machine was described in [Böhm and Dezani, 1972], and had an actual implementation in the 1990s, in unpublished work by Stefano Guerrini. The CUCH machine is of interest for the history of abstract interpreters for the λ-calculus, for it used a leftmost-first reduction strategy instead of the call-by-value order realized by the more widely known SECD-machine of Landin.

不过，伯姆及其学生对 λ 与 CL 的主要影响，来自对纯系统的发现。[Böhm, 1966] 与 [Böhm and Gross, 1966] 对 CUCH 的介绍，建立了一种统一语言，用于表述无类型 λ 与 CL 句法方面的技术问题。解决这些问题主导了伯姆后来相当一部分研究。§7.1 将讨论这些内容。

But the main influence of Böhm and his students on λ and CL was through discoveries about the pure systems. The presentation of CUCH in [Böhm, 1966] and [Böhm and Gross, 1966] set up a uniform language for formulating technical problems about the syntax of untyped λ and CL, and the solution of such problems dominated much of Böhm’s later research activity. We shall discuss these in §7.1.

35　相关工作的讨论见 [Landin, 1966a, §20]。SECD 机的教材式介绍见 [Burge, 1978]。36　我们对伯姆工作的重建，得益于 [Dezani et al., 1993, pp.1–8] 中的科学传记，以及伯姆于 2001 年 4 月 11 日的私人通信。

35See [Landin, 1966a, §20] for a discussion of related work. A textbook treatment of the SECDmachine is contained in [Burge, 1978]. 36Our reconstruction of Böhm’s work has been helped by the scientific biography in [Dezani et al., 1993, pp. 1–8], and a personal communication by Böhm, April 11th, 2001.

7　句法方面的发展

7 Syntactical developments

从 20 世纪 60 年代开始，对 λ 与 CL 兴趣的复苏，带来了句法研究的一些新进展。

The revival of interest in λ and CL that began in the 1960s brought with it some new advances in the study of their syntax.

证明论研究者对哥德尔 Dialectica 论文 [Gödel, 1958] 的研究，尤其受到斯坦福大学格奥尔格·克赖泽尔的推动，从 1963 年起导出了 λ 与 CL 各种类型化扩张的正规化证明。下文 §8.2 将讨论这些工作。

Work by proof-theorists on Gödel’s Dialectica paper [Gödel, 1958], stimulated particularly by Georg Kreisel in Stanford University, led to normalization proofs for various typed extensions of λ and CL from 1963 onward. These will be discussed in §8.2 below.

1963 年，达纳·斯科特在斯坦福一次秋季数学基础研讨班上讲授 λ [Scott, 1963]。其部分动机是“对约翰·麦卡锡论文中提出的函数系统有某种不满”，并希望“其背后的哲学……将导向一种供计算机使用的通用编程语言”[Scott, 1963, p.1.2]。斯科特的句法创新包括一种新的自然数表示，其前驱组合子远比丘奇数已知的前驱组合子简单，³⁸ 以及下述简洁而一般的 λ 不可判定性新定理：若两个非空项集对展开，即逆向归约封闭，则它们不能被递归分离；也就是说，不存在全递归函数，对其中一个集合的成员输出 1，对另一个集合的成员输出 0。见 [Scott, 1963, §9]、[Barendregt, 1981, §6.6] 或 [Curry et al., 1972, §13B2]。顺便说，柯里独立证明了一个类似的不可判定性定理，虽然比斯科特的略不一般，而且晚了几年，但当时还没见过斯科特的定理；见 [Curry, 1969b, p.10] 或 [Curry et al., 1972, p.251, footnote 7]。

In 1963 Dana Scott gave lectures on λ to an autumn seminar on foundations at Stanford [Scott, 1963]. These were partly motivated by “a certain lack of satisfaction with systems for functions suggested in papers of John McCarthy”, with the hope “that the underlying philosophy will . . . lead to the development of a general programming language for use on computers” [Scott, 1963, p. 1.2]. Scott’s syntactical innovations included a new representation of the natural numbers for which the predecessor combinator was much simpler than those known for Church’s numerals,38 and the following neat and general new undecidability theorem for λ: if two non-empty sets of terms are closed under expansion (i.e. reversed reduction), then the pair cannot be recursively separated, i.e. there is no total recursive function which outputs 1 on the members of one set and 0 on the other; see [Scott, 1963, §9] or [Barendregt, 1981, §6.6] or [Curry et al., 1972, §13B2]. Incidentally, Curry independently proved a similar undecidability theorem, though his was slightly less general than Scott’s and was made a few years later (but before seeing Scott’s theorem), see [Curry, 1969b, p.10] or [Curry et al., 1972, p.251, footnote 7].

斯科特对 λ 理论的下一项重大贡献出现在 1969 年的语义方面，彻底改变了该主题本身及其更一般的背景，即高阶函数研究；见下文 §9.1。

Scott’s next major contribution to the theory of λ was on the semantic side in 1969 and radically changed both the subject itself and its more general context, the study of higher-order functions; see §9.1 below.

1971 年，亨克·巴伦德雷赫特那篇有影响的论文 [Barendregt, 1971] 问世。其动机部分来自语义，因此将在 §9.2 第 46 页讨论；但其结果和方法是句法性的，推动了 20 世纪 70 年代大量关于纯 λ 句法性质的研究。§7.2 将介绍其中一些；1981 年以前的许多结果被收入专著 [Barendregt, 1981]，该书成为 λ 核心性质的标准参考文献。

In 1971 appeared the influential thesis of Henk Barendregt, [Barendregt, 1971]. Part of its motivation was semantical, so we shall discuss it in §9.2 p.46, but its results and methods were syntactical and stimulated much work on syntactical properties of pure λ in the 1970s. Some of this work will be described in §7.2, and many of its pre-1981 results were incorporated into the monograph [Barendregt, 1981], which became the standard reference for the core properties of λ.

37　近 20 年后，[Böhm, 1982] 描述了巴克斯函数式编程系统 [Backus, 1978] 的一种组合子表示；该系统在许多方面类似 APL。38　斯科特数码为 0 ≡ K、succ ≡ λuxy.yu，见 [Scott, 1963, §3] 或 [Curry et al., 1972, pp.260–261]。其前驱为 pred ≡ λx.x0I。丘奇数的一些已知前驱见 [Curry et al., 1972, pp.218,226]。人们也提出并使用其他数码系统，例如 [Barendregt, 1976, §4.1]、[Wadsworth, 1980] 及伯姆的工作，见下文。数码系统的一般讨论与例子见 [Curry et al., 1972, §§13A1,13C1] 和 [Barendregt, 1981, §§6.4,6.8]。

37Almost 20 years later, [Böhm, 1982] described a combinatory representation of Backus’ systems of functional programming, [Backus, 1978], that are in many respects similar to APL. 38Scott’s numerals were 0 ≡K, succ ≡λuxy. yu, see [Scott, 1963, §3] or [Curry et al., 1972, pp. 260–261]. For them, pred ≡λx. x0I. Some predecessors known for the Church numerals are given in [Curry et al., 1972, pp. 218, 226]. Other numeral systems have been proposed and used, for example in [Barendregt, 1976, §4.1], in [Wadsworth, 1980], and by Böhm, see below. Numeral systems in general have been discussed, with examples, in [Curry et al., 1972, §§13A1, 13C1] and [Barendregt, 1981, §§6.4, 6.8].

7.1　来自编程方面的贡献

7.1 Contributions from the programming side

在意大利，伯姆关于基于 λ 的语言 CUCH 的工作，使他提出一系列 λ 句法的技术问题。这些问题本身，以及后来被吸引来参与其工作的学生们的活动，都对该主题的发展产生重要影响。第一个问题出现在 1964 年一次报告末尾 [Böhm, 1966, p.194]：对任意两个不同的闭正规形 F、G，能否构造 λ 项 ΔF,G，以区分二者，使得

In Italy, the work of Böhm on the λ-based language CUCH led him to ask a series of technical questions about the syntax of λ which came to have an important influence on the development of the subject, both through the questions themselves and through the later activities of the students he attracted to his work. The first of these questions, at the end of a talk given in 1964 [Böhm, 1966, p.194], was whether it was possible to discriminate between any two distinct closed normal forms F, G by constructing a λ-term ∆F,G such that

ΔF,GF=βηλxy.xΔF,GG=βηλxy.y

几年后，伯姆本人对 βη 正规形证明了肯定答案 [Böhm, 1968]；这一结果如今称为伯姆定理。³⁹ 中岛礼二与巴伦德雷赫特独立分析了伯姆的证明，见 [Nakajima, 1975] 与 [Barendregt, 1977, §6]。由这些分析产生了如今熟知的伯姆树概念，以及把它用于 λ 模型研究的方法，见下文 §9.2。⁴⁰ [Barendregt, 1981, Ch.10] 细致说明了证明背后的思想，包括用上下文提取伯姆树子树的技术，称为 Böhming out。

A few years later the answer “yes” was proved for βη-normal forms by Böhm himself, [Böhm, 1968]; this result is now known as Böhm’s theorem.39 Böhm’s proof of his theorem was analysed by Reiji Nakajima and by Barendregt independently, see [Nakajima, 1975] and [Barendregt, 1977, §6], and from these analyses came the now well-known concept of Böhm tree, as well as the use of this concept in the study of models of λ (see §9.2 below).40 In [Barendregt, 1981, Ch. 10] there is a careful account of the ideas behind the proof (including the name Böhming out for the technique of extracting subtrees of a Böhm tree by means of contexts).

伯姆在 [Böhm, 1968, p.4] 提出的另一个猜想，如今称为值域定理：组合子 F 的值域，是所有形如 FM 的闭 λ 项的 βη 可转换等价类所构成的集合；定理断言，这个集合要么无限，要么是单元素集。迈希尔与巴伦德雷赫特在一年内独立证明了它。受迪尔克·范达伦一条评论启发的构造性版本，以及关于各个证明的一些历史说明，见 [Barendregt, 1993]。

Another conjecture of Böhm’s, made in [Böhm, 1968, p.4], was what is now called the Range theorem: the range of a combinator F is the set of all βη-convertibility classes of closed λ-terms of the form FM, and the theorem states that this set is either infinite or a singleton. This theorem was proved within a year by Myhill and Barendregt independently. A constructive version of the theorem, prompted by a remark of Dirk van Dalen, together with some historical remarks on the various proofs, is in [Barendregt, 1993].

39　伯姆定理的一个组织清晰的证明见 [Krivine, 1990, pp.68–74]。一个已用 CAML 语言机械化的更完整证明见 [Huet, 1993]；它处理了通常被忽略、但涉及无限树时变得严重的绑定变量问题。40　不过，对正规形项而言，伯姆树概念已在 [Böhm and Dezani, 1974] 中出现。

39A well organised proof of Böhm’s theorem is in [Krivine, 1990, pp. 68–74]. A fuller proof which has been mechanised in the language CAML is in [Huet, 1993]; it deals with problems about bound variables which are usually ignored but become serious when infinite trees are involved. 40Although for terms in normal form the Böhm tree concept had already appeared in [Böhm and Dezani, 1974].

伯姆希望把 CUCH 用作真正的编程语言，因此研究把数据结构编码为 λ 项的各种方式。他以丘奇数编码自然数 [Böhm, 1966, Appendix II]，但也考虑其他表示，例如

Böhm’s aim of using CUCH as a real programming language led him to study various ways of coding data structures as λ-terms. He coded natural numbers as the Church numerals [Böhm, 1966, Appendix II], although he also considered alternative representations, for example

n≡λx.⟨x,…,x⟩n times

如 [Böhm, 1966, Appendix VI] 所述。向量 ⟨X₁,…,Xₙ⟩ 表示为形如 λx.xX₁…Xₙ 的项，投影 Uᵢⁿ 定义为 λx₁…xₙ.xᵢ [Böhm, 1966, Appendix IV]。伯姆把数据与程序都看作高阶函数，应以正规形项或强正规化项表示。这使他开始研究给所有正规形构成的集合赋予代数结构的问题，这在他的工作中变得尤为重要。他希望找到适合把数据结构表示为初始代数的正规形代数结构，并能在其上用正规形项表示迭代函数、原始递归函数，甚至全递归函数。他从 1983 年起探索这种表示，并在一长串著作中报告结果：[Böhm and Berarducci, 1985]、[Böhm, 1986; Böhm, 1988b; Böhm, 1988a; Böhm, 1989]。其中第一篇与罗马的亚历山德罗·贝拉尔杜奇合作，研究在二阶 λ 演算中表示数据结构；如 §8.3 将讨论，它对多态 λ 演算的研究影响很大。

as described in [Böhm, 1966, Appendix VI]. Vectors ⟨X1, . . . , Xn⟩were represented as terms of the form λx.xX1 . . . Xn with projections U n i defined by λx1 . . . xn.xi, [Böhm, 1966, Appendix IV]. Böhm saw both data and programs as higher-order functions that should be represented by terms in normal form (or by strongly normalizing terms). This led him to the problem of structuring the set of all normal forms algebraically, which became especially important in his work. He wished to find an algebraic structure of normal forms suitable for representing data structures as initial algebras, and over which iterative, primitive recursive and even total recursive functions could be represented by terms in normal form. He explored this representation from 1983 onwards, and reported the results in a long series of works: [Böhm and Berarducci, 1985], [Böhm, 1986; Böhm, 1988b; Böhm, 1988a; Böhm, 1989]. The first of these, with Alessandro Berarducci in Rome, investigated the representation of data structures in second order λ-calculus and, as we shall discuss in §8.3, was very influential in investigations of polymorphic λ-calculi.

在组合逻辑中寻求代数结构，也导向对组合子模 η 转换的幺半群结构的研究，扩展了丘奇早期的思想 [Church, 1937]，甚至通过研究可逆项导向组合群。1976 年，伯姆在都灵的合作者、也是其前学生的玛丽安焦拉·德扎尼，⁴¹ 刻画了 λβη 演算中可逆且可正规化的项 [Dezani, 1976]；克洛普与扬·贝赫斯特拉把结果推广到全部项 [Bergstra and Klop, 1980]。随后，德扎尼与弗朗索瓦丝·埃尔米纳使用交类型的早期版本，描述了正规形的极大幺半群 [Dezani and Ermine, 1982]。[Böhm and Dezani, 1989] 进一步研究了组合子的幺半群。（截至 1980 年的详细结果见 [Barendregt, 1981, Ch.21]。）

The quest for algebraic structure in combinatory logic led also to the study of the monoid structure of combinators (modulo η-conversion), extending an early idea of Church [Church, 1937], and even to combinatory groups through the study of invertible terms. In 1976 his co-worker and former student Mariangiola Dezani41 in Turin made a characterization of the invertible normalizable terms of λβη-calculus, [Dezani, 1976], and her result was extended to all terms by Klop and Jan Bergstra, [Bergstra and Klop, 1980]. With Francoise Ermine, Dezani then gave a description of maximal monoids of normal forms, using an early version of intersection types, [Dezani and Ermine, 1982]. Monoids of combinators were studied further in [Böhm and Dezani, 1989]. (A detailed account of the results in this area until 1980 is in [Barendregt, 1981, Ch. 21].)

采取类似的代数方法，伯姆约于 1977 年提出，把组合子视为广义数码，从而对组合逻辑作简单的重新解释 [Böhm, 1979; Böhm, 1980]；其启发来自：丘奇数 m、n 的幂 mⁿ 恰好就是 nm。他把组合子视为带有和、积、幂运算的结构，满足通常算术公理，另加一个用于配对的非算术常量。

Taking a similar algebraic approach, around 1977 Böhm proposed a simple reinterpretation of combinatory logic by regarding combinators as generalized numerals [Böhm, 1979; Böhm, 1980]; this was suggested by the fact that exponentiation mn of Church numerals m, n is just nm. He regarded combinators as a structure with sum, product and exponentiation with the usual arithmetical axioms, plus a non-arithmetical constant for pairing.

麻省理工学院的詹姆斯·H. 莫里斯是另一位有影响的贡献者。他与伯姆一样，受到对编程语言兴趣的推动；论文 [Morris, 1968] 承认在思想上受惠于兰丁，不过其中大多数结果涉及无类型和简单类型 λ 演算的句法。

James H. Morris at the Massachusetts Institute of Technology was another influential contributor who, like Böhm, was motivated by an interest in programming languages; his thesis [Morris, 1968] acknowledged an intellectual debt to Landin, although most of the results in it concerned the syntax of untyped and simply typed λ-calculus.

该论文首次把“纯”简单类型 λ 独立表述出来，不再包含丘奇与柯里系统中的额外逻辑公理和规则。莫里斯的表述就是今天称为“柯里风格”的方式：给无类型的纯 λ 项赋予类型。在 [Morris, 1968, p.107] 中，他以一种与图灵未发表笔记实质相同的方法证明弱正规化，见下文 §8.2；在 [Morris, 1968, pp.100–105] 中，他描述并验证了可赋型性的检验方法，见下文 §8.5。

That thesis contained the first formulation of “pure” simply typed λ in isolation without the extra logical axioms and rules that the systems of Church and Curry had contained. Morris’ formulation was what is nowadays called “Curry-style”; types were assigned to untyped pure λ-terms. In [Morris, 1968, p.107] he proved weak normalization by a method essentially the same as in Turing’s unpublished note, see §8.2 below, and in [Morris, 1968, pp.100–105] he described and verified a test for typability, see §8.5 below.

莫里斯还给出了最早应用伯姆定理的例子之一。这个结果虽是句法性的，后来却在 λ 的模型论中发挥重要作用。他建议把 λ 项 M 看作部分函数，由那些对 ⟨⟨M₁,…,Mₙ⟩,N⟩ 构成，其中从 MM₁…Mₙ 开始归约，最终停在正规形 N。这一想法的问题在于，βη 转换并不与部分函数相等重合，因此他在项上引入下述预序 [Morris, 1968, p.50]：

Morris’ thesis also contained perhaps the first application of Böhm’s Theorem, in a result which, although syntactical, later played a significant role in the model theory of λ. Morris suggested looking at a λ-term M as a partial function consisting of those pairs ⟨⟨M1, . . . , Mn⟩, N⟩such that reduction starting at MM1 . . . Mn halts with the normal form N. The problem with this idea was that βη-conversion does not coincide with equality of partial functions, so Morris introduced the following preorder on terms [Morris, 1968, p.50]:

给定 λ 项 M、N：若对所有上下文 C[ ]，只要 C[M] 有正规形，C[N] 就有相同正规形，则称 M 扩张 N。

Given λ-terms M, N: M extends N if, for all contexts C[ ], if C[M] has a normal form then C[N] has the same normal form.

由扩张预序生成的项间等价关系，后来称为上下文等价。莫里斯应用刚发表的伯姆定理，证明它实质上是纯 λ 项上非平凡地扩张 βη 可转换性的最大同余 [Morris, 1968, p.53, Cor.5]。

The equivalence relation on terms generated by the extension preorder came later to be called contextual equivalence. Morris proved it to be, essentially, the largest congruence on (pure) λ-terms that non-trivially extends βη-convertibility, by applying the newly-published Böhm Theorem [Morris, 1968, p.53, Cor. 5].

莫里斯扩张预序定义中值得注意的一点，是使用了上下文：它后来成为定义无类型项和有类型项等价关系时无处不在的工具，见后文 §9.2 和 §10。莫里斯还通过复杂的归约分析，证明通常归于柯里的不动点组合子 Y，在扩张预序下产生最小不动点 [Morris, 1968, Ch.III, §C, Cor.7(a)]。这一结果被用来为麦卡锡递归归纳原理的一种形式提供例证性的论证。

A point worth noting in Morris’ definition of the extension preorder was the use of contexts: these became an ubiquitous tool for the definition of equivalences on both untyped and typed terms (see §9.2 and §10 later). Morris also proved that the fixed-point combinator Y commonly attributed to Curry yields the least fixed point under the extension preorder [Morris, 1968, Ch. III, §C, Cor. 7(a)], by a complicated analysis of reductions. This result is used to justify, by way of example, a form of McCarthy’s principle of Recursion Induction.

41　婚后姓名为 Mariangiola Dezani Ciancaglini。

41Married name Mariangiola Dezani Ciancaglini.

在论文接近末尾处，莫里斯论证了在简单类型 λ 演算中允许循环类型表达式的用处，例如类型方程 α = α → β 的解。他指出：“对于循环类型表达式可能指称哪种对象，我们只有有限的直觉，或许是某个函数集合，其中有些函数以自身作为参数和值”[Morris, 1968, p.123]。不到一年后，达纳·斯科特便用 λ 演算的集合论模型解答了这一难题，见下文 §9.1。

Near the end of his thesis, Morris argued for the usefulness of allowing circular type-expressions in simply typed λ-calculus, like the solution of the type equation α = α →β, remarking that “we have only a limited intuition about what kind of an object a circular type expression might denote (possibly, a set of functions, some of which have themselves as arguments and values)”, [Morris, 1968, p.123]. The answer to this puzzle was to be found less than one year later by Dana Scott with his set-theoretic model for the λ-calculus, see §9.1 below.

7.2　归约理论

7.2 Theory of reductions

自 [Church and Rosser, 1936] 首次证明 λβ 归约的合流性以来，逻辑学界一直普遍觉得，应该存在更短的证明。§5.2 所述的抽象合流性证明研究没有解决这一问题，因为其主要目标是一般性，而不是专门为 λβ 寻找短证明。

Ever since the original proof of the confluence of λβ-reduction in [Church and Rosser, 1936], a general feeling had persisted in the logic community that a shorter proof ought to exist. The work on abstract confluence proofs described in §5.2 did not help, as it was aimed mainly at generality, not at a short proof for λβ in particular.

相比之下，CL 的第一个合流性证明被认为已经相当简单；关键思想是把一组互不重叠的可归约式的同时收缩，计为一个单位步骤，而证明这些单位步骤序列的合流性很容易，见 [Rosser, 1935, p.144, Thm.T12]。

For CL, in contrast, the first confluence proof was accepted as reasonably simple; its key idea was to count the simultaneous contraction of a set of non-overlapping redexes as a single unit step, and confluence of sequences of these unit steps was easy to prove, [Rosser, 1935, p.144, Thm. T12].

1965 年，威廉·泰特在斯坦福由斯科特和麦卡锡组织的 λ 研讨班上，给出了 CL 的一个简短合流性证明。关键是通过对项结构归纳，极为简洁地定义单位步归约。后来发现，泰特的单位步骤与罗瑟的实质相同，但其归纳定义直接得多。而且，它可以改用于 λβ。（1965 年研讨班上已注意到这种可能，见 [Tait, 2003, p.755 footnote]。）泰特没有直接发表该方法，但于 1968 年秋把 CL 证明展示给佩尔·马丁-勒夫。后者在类型论研究中将它改用于 λβ，并把 λβ 证明收入手稿 [Martin-Löf, 1971b, pp.8–11, §2.5]。

Then in 1965 William Tait presented a short confluence proof for CL to a seminar on λ organized by Scott and McCarthy at Stanford. Its key was a very neat definition of a unit-step reduction by induction on term-structure. Tait’s units were later seen to be essentially the same as Rosser’s, but his inductive definition was much more direct. Further, it could be adapted to λβ. (This possibility was noted at the seminar in 1965, see [Tait, 2003, p.755 footnote]). Tait did not publish his method directly, but in the autumn of 1968 he showed his CL proof to Per MartinLöf, who then adapted it to λβ in the course of his work on type theory and included the λβ proof in his manuscript [Martin-Löf, 1971b, pp.8–11, §2.5].

马丁-勒夫把泰特证明改用于 λβ 的工作，很快得到其他研究者赏识，出现在 [Barendregt, 1971, Appendix II]、[Stenlund, 1972, Ch.2]、[Hindley et al., 1972, Appendix 1] 中，也出现在他自己的报告 [Martin-Löf, 1972b, §2.4.3] 中。⁴²

Martin-Löf’s λβ-adaptation of Tait’s proof was quickly appreciated by other workers in the subject, and appeared in [Barendregt, 1971, Appendix II], [Stenlund, 1972, Ch. 2] and [Hindley et al., 1972, Appendix 1], as well as in a report by MartinLöf himself, [Martin-Löf, 1972b, §2.4.3].42

在 λ 中，泰特结构归纳法定义的每个单位步骤，原来都是某组可归约式，不必互不重叠，的一次最小者优先展开。柯里已在 [Curry and Feys, 1958, p.126] 中引入这种展开，但仅间接使用；欣德利在论文中大量使用过它们 [Hindley, 1969a, p.547, “MCD”]，但只是在非常抽象的环境中。依照高桥正子的叫法，它们如今通常称为并行归约。[Takahashi, 1989] 进一步精炼了泰特—马丁-勒夫证明，也把将归约分解为这种单位步骤的方法，用于简化 λ 归约其他主要定理的证明。

In λ, each unit step defined by Tait’s structural-induction method turned out to be a minimal-first development of a set of redexes (not necessarily disjoint). Curry had introduced such developments in [Curry and Feys, 1958, p.126], but had used them only indirectly; Hindley had used them extensively in his thesis, [Hindley, 1969a, p.547,“MCD”], but only in a very abstract setting. They are now usually called parallel reductions, following Masako Takahashi. In [Takahashi, 1989] the Tait-Martin-Löf proof was further refined, and the method of dividing reductions into these unit steps was also applied to simplify proofs of other main theorems on reductions in λ.

泰特的结构归纳法如今是证明 λ 与 CL 合流性的标准方法。不过，其他一些证明能提供该方法所不能提供的额外归约洞见，例如见 [Barendregt, 1981, Chs.3, 11–12] 的分析。

Tait’s structural-induction method is now the standard way to prove confluence in λ and CL. However, some other proofs give extra insights into reductions that this method does not, see for example the analysis in [Barendregt, 1981, Chs. 3, 11–12].

除合流性外，λβ 归约研究的主要主题还包括展开有限性、标准化与完全展开等价性定理，它们都直接或间接依赖剩余项概念。前两个定理已在 [Church and Rosser, 1936, Lemma 1] 中对 λI 项证明；第二个还在 [Rosser, 1935, p.142, Thm.T9] 中对 CL 证明。但完整 λK 演算中的对应结果更微妙，直到很久以后才出现。

Besides confluence, major themes in the study of λβ-reductions have been given by theorems on finiteness of developments, standardisation and the equivalence of complete developments, all depending either directly or indirectly on the concept of residual. The first two of these theorems were proved for λI-terms in [Church and Rosser, 1936, Lemma 1], and the second was proved for CL in [Rosser, 1935, p.142, Thm. T9]; but their analogues for the full λK-calculus are more subtle and did not come until much later.

42　较早的手稿 [Martin-Löf, 1971b] 没有发表，因为从命题即类型对应的观点看，其中描述的系统不一致，见下文 §8.1.4；但其 λβ 合流性证明并无错误。

42The earlier manuscript [Martin-Löf, 1971b] was not published, as the system described in it was inconsistent from the standpoint of the propositions-as-types correspondence, see §8.1.4 below; but its confluence proof for λβ was not faulty.

λK 的展开有限性定理，由施罗尔在未发表的论文 [Schroer, 1965, Part I, Thm.6.20] 中首次证明。后来不同方法又重新证明了它：马丁·海兰德于 1973 年 [Hyland, 1975, Thm.3.6]，欣德利于 1976 年 [Hindley, 1978a, Thm.1]，以及巴伦德雷赫特、贝赫斯特拉、克洛普、福尔肯于 1976 年 [Barendregt et al., 1976, pp.14–17]。最后一个证明使用了特别简洁的标记技术，以及标记归约的强正规化定理，见 [Barendregt, 1981, §11.2]。

The finiteness-of-developments theorem for λK was first proved by Schroer in his unpublished thesis [Schroer, 1965, Part I, Thm. 6.20]. It was re-proved later using different methods: by Martin Hyland in 1973 [Hyland, 1975, Thm. 3.6], by Hindley in 1976 [Hindley, 1978a, Thm. 1], and by Barendregt, Bergstra, Klop and Volken in 1976 [Barendregt et al., 1976, pp.14–17]. The latter proof used a particularly neat labelling technique and a strong normalization theorem for labelled reductions, see [Barendregt, 1981, §11.2].

沿归约追踪项各组成部分的标记技术，最早用于 [Hyland, 1976, §2] 与 [Wadsworth, 1978, §4] 中逼近项极限定理的证明，见下文 §9.2。在 [Wadsworth, 1976, p.508] 中，沃兹沃思把这一想法归于海兰德 1972 年的一次通信。让-雅克·莱维于 1974 年扩展了它，见 [Lévy, 1976, pp.106–114] 及 [Lévy, 1978, Ch.II]，并用于证明“由内向外”归约的完备性及其他主要归约定理。[Barendregt, 1981, Ch.14] 是关于标记的良好一般性介绍。

Labelling techniques to follow components of terms down through reductions were first used in [Hyland, 1976, §2] and [Wadsworth, 1978, §4], in proofs of the limit theorem for approximants, see §9.2 below. In [Wadsworth, 1976, p.508] the idea was attributed by Wadsworth to a 1972 communication from Hyland. It was extended by Jean-Jacques Lévy in 1974 [Lévy, 1976, pp. 106–114] and in [Lévy, 1978, Ch. II], and applied to prove the completeness of “inside-out” reductions and the other main theorems on reductions. A good general account of labelling is in [Barendregt, 1981, Ch. 14].

λK 的标准化定理最早见于 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §4E1]，但其证明依赖最左优先展开的有限性。1975 年，格尔德·米奇克大幅简化了柯里的证明 [Mitschke, 1979]。标准化的一个推论是最左归约的共尾性，即一个项的最左归约有限，当且仅当该项有正规形。20 世纪 70 年代初，巴伦德雷赫特指出，实践中最常使用的是拟最左归约，而非最左归约，并证明了这些归约对应的共尾性定理 [Barendregt, 1981, Thm.13.2.6]。⁴³ 在他 1976 年与贝赫斯特拉、克洛普、福尔肯合著的报告中，更一般的归约策略概念被引入并研究，见 [Barendregt et al., 1976, pp.33–43] 或 [Barendregt, 1981, Ch.13]。

The standardization theorem for λK first appeared in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §4E1], though with a proof that depended on the finiteness of leftmost-first developments. In 1975 Curry’s proof was much simplified by Gerd Mitschke, [Mitschke, 1979]. A consequence of standardization is the cofinality of leftmost reductions, i.e. that the leftmost reduction of a term is finite if and only if the term has a normal form. In the early 1970s Barendregt pointed out that the reductions most often used in practice were quasi-leftmost, not leftmost, and proved the corresponding cofinality theorem for these reductions too [Barendregt, 1981, Thm. 13.2.6].43 In his 1976 report with Bergstra, Klop and Volken the more general concept of reduction strategy was introduced and studied, see [Barendregt et al., 1976, pp.33–43] or [Barendregt, 1981, Ch. 13].

λK 中完全展开的基本等价性定理，最早在 [Curry and Feys, 1958, pp.113–130] 中陈述并证明。1978 年，莱维把它加强为更强的等价性，见 [Lévy, 1978, pp.37,65] 或 [Barendregt, 1981, §§12.2.1,12.2.5]。莱维的等价性定理导向 β 归约的新观点，体现在论文 [Klop, 1980] 中：合流性和标准化证明所涉及的变换，被看作归约图的转换。过去 40 年一直为 β 归约理论寻找的、相当整齐的底层结构，终于获得。（相关介绍见 [Barendregt, 1981, Chs.11–14]。）

The basic equivalence theorem for complete developments in λK was first stated and proved in [Curry and Feys, 1958, pp.113–130]. In 1978 it was improved by Lévy to a stronger equivalence, see [Lévy, 1978, pp. 37, 65] or [Barendregt, 1981, §§12.2.1, 12.2.5]. Lévy’s equivalence theorem led to a new view of β-reductions, embodied in the thesis [Klop, 1980]. The transformations involved in proofs of confluence and standardization were seen as conversions of reduction diagrams, and the reasonably tidy underlying structure that had been sought for the theory of β-reduction for the previous forty years was at last achieved. (An account is in [Barendregt, 1981, Chs. 11–14].)

对于 λβη，如 §5.3 所述，合流性与 η 步骤的延后，自柯里 20 世纪 50 年代的工作起就已知晓。但尽管有人尝试，始终没有证明类似 β 情形的标准化定理，也没有证明最左归约的任何共尾性定理。这些定理首次成功的证明出现在 [Klop, 1980, Ch.IV]。

For λβη, confluence and the postponement of η-steps were known since Curry’s work in the 1950s, as was mentioned in §5.3, but no standardization theorem had been proved analogous to that for β, nor had any cofinality theorem been proved for leftmost reductions, despite attempts. The first successful proofs of these theorems were made in [Klop, 1980, Ch. IV].

回到 λβ：受编程需求推动，使归约更高效的问题在 20 世纪 70 年代开始受到关注。其中一个问题，是寻找在某种合理意义下最优的、从项到其正规形的归约。对 CL，约翰·斯特普尔斯于 1972 年研究过此问题，但工作直到九年后才发表 [Staples, 1981]。1974 年，让·维耶曼针对递归程序系统，提出一条“延迟规则”，用于归约带有由递归程序定义的函数符号的一阶项 [Vuillemin, 1974a, §3]。这条规则在其系统中可证明最优，且易于实现；但在 λ 演算中，最优性问题要困难得多。不过，莱维在论文 [Lévy, 1978] 中，通过深入分析从一个项开始的 β 归约格，并利用标记技术定义可在归约中共享的某些可归约式族，把维耶曼的思想推广到 λ。⁴⁴ 莱维还证明了由内向外 β 归约的完备性，这几乎与最左归约相反；该结果曾在 [Welch, 1975] 中被猜测，见 [Lévy, 1976]。

Returning to λβ: problems involved in making reduction more efficient began to attract attention in the 1970s, stimulated by programming needs. One such problem was that of finding a reduction of a term to its normal form that was optimal in some reasonable sense. For CL this problem was studied in 1972 by John Staples, though his work was not published until 9 years later, [Staples, 1981]. In 1974 for a system of recursive programs, Jean Vuillemin formulated a “delay rule” for reducing first-order terms with function symbols defined by a recursive program, [Vuillemin, 1974a, §3]. This rule in Vuillemin’s system could be proved optimal and easily implementable, but the problem of optimality turned out to be more difficult in the case of λ-calculus. However, Lévy extended Vuillemin’s idea to λ in his thesis [Lévy, 1978], by an in-depth analysis of the lattice of β-reductions starting at a term, and by exploiting his labelling technique to define certain families of redexes that can be shared through reductions.44 Lévy also proved the completeness of inside-out β- reductions (almost the opposite of leftmost reductions), which had been conjectured in [Welch, 1975], see [Lévy, 1976].

代换是另一个从计算角度引起兴趣的主题。其正确定义多年前已确定，但高效实现如今成为重要问题；例如 20 世纪 50 年代 LISP 把某些细节过度简化，后来不得不纠正，见上文 §6.1。考虑到实现问题，哲尔吉·雷韦斯于 1978 年提出一种有趣的新方法替代 β 归约。它生成通常的等价关系 =β，具有合流性，与通常 β 归约有相同正规形，并把代换分解为更小步骤；见 [Révész, 1978] 或 [Révész, 1985]。

Substitution was another topic which aroused interest from a computing point of view. Its correct definition had been settled years ago, but its efficient implementation now became an important problem; for example in LISP in the 1950s some of the details had been over-simplified and had had to be corrected, see §6.1 above. With the implementation problem in mind, in 1978 Gyorgy Révész proposed an interesting new alternative to β-reduction. It generated the usual equivalence relation =β, was confluent, had the same normal forms as the usual β-reduction, and broke substitutions into smaller steps; see [Révész, 1978] or [Révész, 1985].

高效实现代换的另一方法，是扩展 λ 语言，引入带适当归约规则的显式代换算子。米奇克与巴里·罗森为证明 λβ 归约合流性而独立想到这一想法，见 [Mitschke, 1973, pp.150–151]、[Rosen, 1973, §9]。它在 20 世纪 90 年代导向若干至今仍在发展的形式系统；相关论文的例子包括 [Komori, 1990]、[Abadi et al., 1991]、[Kamareddine and Nederpelt, 1993]、[Curien et al., 1996]、[Benaissa et al., 1996]、[Kamareddine and Rios, 1997]、[Bloo and Geuvers, 1999] 和 [Kamareddine and Rios, 2000]。

Another approach to the problem of implementing substitution efficiently was to extend the λ-language by introducing an explicit substitution operator with suitable reduction rules. This idea, which had occurred independently to Mitschke and Barry Rosen for use in confluence proofs for λβ-reduction ([Mitschke, 1973, pp. 150–151], [Rosen, 1973, §9]), led in the 1990s to several formal systems which are still being developed; a few examples of papers on these are [Komori, 1990], [Abadi et al., 1991], [Kamareddine and Nederpelt, 1993], [Curien et al., 1996], [Benaissa et al., 1996], [Kamareddine and Rios, 1997], [Bloo and Geuvers, 1999], and [Kamareddine and Rios, 2000].

代换的一个特例，是高效改变绑定变量的问题。1972 年，德布鲁因描述了一种完全避免这一问题的形式体系：给项中的全部 λ 编号，再用数字索引替换绑定变量，指明由哪个 λ 绑定它们 [Bruijn, 1972]。德布鲁因的形式体系在 20 世纪 80 年代用于 ML 编程语言的编译器，也用于许多显式代换系统。

A special case of substitution was the problem of changing bound variables efficiently. In 1972 de Bruijn described a formalism which avoided this problem completely, by numbering all λs in a term and replacing bound variables by numberindices to show which λ bound them, [Bruijn, 1972]. De Bruijn’s formalism was used in the compiler of the programming language ML in the 1980s, and has been used in many explicit substitution systems.

43　归约是拟最左的，当且仅当对每个非最左步骤，其后总有某个最左步骤。44　实现最优归约所需共享的适当数据结构，于 1990 年独立描述于 [Lamping, 1990] 与 [Kathail, 1990]。随后 [Gonthier et al., 1992] 与 [Asperti, 1995] 把该技术与吉拉尔的交互几何 [Girard, 1989a] 联系起来。

43A reduction is quasi-leftmost iff, for every non-leftmost step, some later step is leftmost. 44A suitable data structure for implementing the sharing required for optimal reductions was described in 1990, independently in [Lamping, 1990] and [Kathail, 1990]. Then in [Gonthier et al., 1992] and [Asperti, 1995] the technique was related to Girard’s geometry of interaction [Girard, 1989a].

8　类型

8 Types

8.1　类型论的一般发展

8.1 The general development of type theories

在罗素与怀特海《数学原理》的类型论，以及赫维斯泰克和拉姆齐的简化版本中 [Russell and Whitehead, 1913; Chwistek, 1922; Ramsey, 1926]，对于类型应是哪种实体，人们意见并不一致。在 [Russell, 1903, §497] 中，已至少存在两种理解：

In the type theory of Russell and Whitehead’s Principia Mathematica and its simplifications by Chwistek and Ramsey [Russell and Whitehead, 1913; Chwistek, 1922; Ramsey, 1926], there was little agreement as to what kind of entities types should be. Already in [Russell, 1903, §497] we find at least two ways in which types can be intended:

（1）类型是命题函数的有意义范围：给定命题函数 φ(x)，存在一类对象，即 x 的类型，使得 φ 应用于该类型的成员时总有值。

(1) Types as ranges of significance of propositional functions: given a propositional function φ(x), there is a class of objects, the type of x, such that φ has a value whenever it is applied to a member of this type.

（2）类型是集合：个体构成最低类型，其上是个体的集合、个体集合的集合，如此无限继续。

(2) Types as sets: individuals form the lowest type of objects, then there are sets of individuals, sets of sets of individuals and so on, ad infinitum.

第一种解释与语法范畴理论有关。把类型视为集合，当然也是早期集合论发展的基础，参见 [Quine, 1963, Ch.XI]；但从 λ 的观点看，尤其重要的是它构成 [Church, 1940] 中简单类型层级定义的基础，以及大量类型论集合论模型研究的基础。

The first interpretation of types is related to the theory of grammatical categories. The view of types as sets underlies, of course, the early developments in set theory, cf. [Quine, 1963, Ch. XI], but most notably from a λ-viewpoint it underlies the definition of the hierarchy of simple types in [Church, 1940] and much of the research on set-theoretical models of type theories.

后来，把类型论传统嫁接到 λ 与 CL 上，并对所得理论的模型进行数学研究，促成了类型新解释的发现。劳威尔于 1969 年、兰贝克于 20 世纪 70 年代引入一个统一的新范式，对类型论研究产生了且仍在产生重大影响 [Lawvere, 1969a; Lawvere, 1969b; Lambek, 1974; Lambek, 1980b; Lambek, 1980a]：

The later grafting of the type-theoretic tradition onto λ and CL, and the mathematical study of the models of the resulting theories, contributed to the discovery of new ways in which types could be interpreted. A new unifying paradigm that became (and still is) very influential in research in type theory was introduced by Lawvere in 1969 and Lambek in the 1970s [Lawvere, 1969a; Lawvere, 1969b; Lambek, 1974; Lambek, 1980b; Lambek, 1980a]:

（3）类型是范畴中的对象，尤其是笛卡尔闭范畴中的对象。其定义提供一般框架，表达多参数函数与以函数为值的一元函数之间的集合论双射。

(3) Types as objects in a category, especially a cartesian closed category, whose definition provides a general framework for a formulation of the set-theoretical bijection between functions of several arguments and unary functions with functions as values.

此外，证明论和直觉主义数学语义的研究表明，在许多情形下，以下观点富有成效：

In addition, research in proof-theory and the semantics of intuitionistic mathematics showed that it was in many cases fruitful to view

（4）类型是命题：在 λ 或 CL 中，给定类型的项充当该类型所编码命题之证明的编码。

(4) Types as propositions: in either λ or CL, terms of a given type play the role of codes for proofs of (the proposition coded by) that type.

下面依次追溯这些线索的历史发展。⁴⁵

We shall now follow the historical development of each of these threads in turn.45

8.1.1　类型作为语法范畴

8.1.1 Types as grammatical categories

范畴语法理论约始于 1900 年，源自埃德蒙德·胡塞尔试图建立意义组合所遵循的纯粹先验规律理论 [Husserl, 1922, Fourth Investigation]。通过卡齐米日·特瓦尔多夫斯基的教学，胡塞尔思想影响了利沃夫逻辑学派，尤其是莱斯涅夫斯基逻辑系统的设计 [Leśniewski, 1929]，在那里它们取代了罗素的逻辑类型。阿伊杜凯维奇首次给出一个自足的范畴语法系统形式化 [Ajdukiewicz, 1935]。系统假定语言中每个词可赋予一个或多个指标，符号化地表示该词所属的语法范畴。从名词短语指标 n 和命题指标 s 出发，可形成无限多个新指标 α/β：这种短语放在指标 β 的短语之前，会产生指标 α 的短语。指标分别为 α、β 的两个词连接后，指标为 αβ；允许把形如 (α/β)β 的子序列替换为 α。还可扩充基本系统，形成指标 α\β：这种短语放在指标 α 的短语之后，会产生指标 β 的短语；相应规则允许把 α(α\β) 替换为 β [Bar-Hillel, 1953; Bar-Hillel, 1959; Lambek, 1958; Lambek, 1961]。以 x → y 表示每个范畴 x 的表达式也属于范畴 y，可以证明例如 xy → z 蕴涵 y → x\z 这样的定理。兰贝克引入类似根岑的演算来推导这类定理，并通过切消去定理证明其可判定性 [Lambek, 1958]。

The theory of categorial grammars began in about 1900 with the attempt of Edmund Husserl to develop a theory of pure, a priori laws that underlie the composition of meanings, [Husserl, 1922, Fourth Investigation]. Through the teaching of Kazimierz Twardowski, Husserl’s ideas influenced the Lw ́ow school of logicians, and in particular the design of the logical systems of Le ́sniewski [Le ́sniewski, 1929], where they replaced the logical types of Russell. Ajdukiewicz was the first to give a self-contained formalization of a system of categorical grammar, [Ajdukiewicz, 1935]. There, it is assumed that to each word of a language can be assigned one or more indices, that are symbolic representations of the grammatical categories to which the word belongs. Starting from indices n for noun phrases and s for propositions, one can obtain infinitely many new indices α/β for phrases that yield a phrase of index α whenever prefixed to a phrase of index β. The concatenation of two words with respective indices α and β has index αβ, and one is allowed to replace each subsequence of the form (α/β)β by α. This basic system can be extended by forming the index α\β for phrases that yield a phrase of index β whenever suffixed to a phrase of index α, with a rule that allows a subsequence of the form α(α\β) to be replaced by β, [Bar-Hillel, 1953; Bar-Hillel, 1959; Lambek, 1958; Lambek, 1961]. Writing x →y to mean that every expression of category x has also category y, one can prove theorems like, for example, that xy →z implies y →x\z. Lambek introduced a Gentzen-like calculus for deriving theorems of this form, and proved its decidability through a cut-elimination theorem, [Lambek, 1958].

柯里对自然语言的形式研究感兴趣，因而了解范畴语法理论，⁴⁶ 并注意到其函数性理论（见 §4.2）有一种语法解释，可把类型视为语法范畴。这符合他早期关于函数性理论的想法：它应成为区分推理组合逻辑系统各项范畴的工具，其思想是“类型论实质上是一种说明某些组合不是命题的手段”，见 [Curry, 1980, §9]。[Curry, 1961] 描述了函数性的语法解释：⁴⁷ 在仅有应用操作的形式系统中，组合 Fαβ 是这样一类函子的范畴，它们接受范畴 α 的参数，产生范畴 β 的结果。例如，若名词与名词短语的范畴为 N，则形容词范畴为 FNN，作为形容词修饰语的副词范畴为 F(FNN)(FNN)，把形容词变成副词的后缀“-ly”的范畴为 F(FNN)(F(FNN)(FNN))。

Curry, because of his interest in the formal study of natural languages, knew about the theory of categorial grammar,46 and noticed that his theory of functionality (see §4.2) had a grammatical interpretation in which types could be regarded as grammatical categories. This was in harmony with his early ideas on the theory of functionality, which was intended to be a tool for drawing categorial distinctions between the terms of systems of illative combinatory logic, with the idea that “a theory of types is essentially a device for saying that certain combinations are not propositions”, see [Curry, 1980, §9]. The grammatical interpretation of functionality was described in [Curry, 1961];47 it regarded the combination Fαβ as the category of those functors that take an argument of category α and give a result of category β, in the context of a formal system whose only operation is application. (For example, if nouns and noun-phrases have category N, then adjectives have category FNN, adverbs (as modifiers of adjectives) have category F(FNN)(FNN), and the suffix “-ly” (which changes adjectives to adverbs) has category F(FNN)(F(FNN)(FNN)).)

用类型描述语法范畴的可能性，也在类型化 λ 演算中形式化逻辑理论句法的工作中发挥作用。例如，马丁-勒夫直觉主义类型论所依据的表达式理论就是如此；他于 1981 年布劳威尔百年纪念会上首次描述该理论，见 [Nordström et al., 1990, p.13]。较近的爱丁堡逻辑框架也是一例 [Harper et al., 1993]。更广泛地说，同一联系也用于数学语言学中称为蒙太格语法的领域 [Montague, 1974]。

The possibility of describing grammatical categories by means of types has also played a role in formalizing the syntax of logical theories within typed λ-calculi. This has happened, for example, with the theory of expressions that underlies Martin-Löf’s intuitionistic theory of types (described for the first time by Martin-Löf in his lecture at the Brouwer Centenary in 1981; see [Nordström et al., 1990, p.13]) and the more recent Edinburgh Logical Framework [Harper et al., 1993]. In a broader perspective, the same connection has been exploited in mathematical linguistics in the area known as Montague grammar, [Montague, 1974].

45　这里不综述全部类型论，只讨论与 λ 和 CL 最相关的部分，尤其不讨论 ZF 集合论中隐含的类型概念。46　特别是 [Ajdukiewicz, 1935]，见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §8S1] 的说明。47　以 [Curry, 1948] 为基础；简述见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §8S2, pp.274–275]。

45We shall not try to survey all of type-theory, but only the parts that are most relevant to λ and CL. In particular, we shall not discuss the type-concept implicit in ZF set theory. 46In particular, about [Ajdukiewicz, 1935], see the remark in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §8S1]. 47Based on [Curry, 1948], summarized briefly in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §8S2, pp.274–275].

8.1.2　类型作为集合

8.1.2 Types as sets

[Church, 1940] 的简单类型层级背后有明确的集合论直觉：丘奇以类型表达式 βα 表示从 α 所表示集合到 β 所表示集合的函数集。这一直觉容易扩展为简单类型 λ 演算的模型概念：集合论结构 D 由集合族 {Dα}α∈T 构成，T 为系统全部类型表达式的集合，Dβα 为从 Dα 到 Dβ 的全体函数集，并带有应用函数族 ·α,β : (Dβα × Dα) → Dβ，α,β∈T，对应于把类型 βα 的函数应用于类型 α 的参数，得到类型 β 的结果。这实质上就是莱昂·亨金在 [Henkin, 1950, pp.83–85] 中引入的标准模型。他也考虑一般模型，其中只假定 Dβα 是从 Dα 到 Dβ 的某一类函数。⁴⁸

There was a clear set-theoretic intuition behind the hierarchy of simple types in [Church, 1940]: Church used a type-expression βα to denote a set of functions from the set (denoted by) α to the set (denoted by) β. This intuition can readily be extended to yield a notion of model for the simply typed λ-calculus as a settheoretic structure D consisting of a family of sets {Dα}α∈T, where T is the set of all type-expressions of the system, such that Dβα is the set of all functions from Dα to Dβ, with a family of application-functions ·α,β : (Dβα ×Dα) →Dβ, for α, β ∈T, that correspond to the application of a function of type βα to an argument of type α with result of type β. This notion is essentially that of standard model introduced by Leon Henkin in [Henkin, 1950, pp.83–85], who also considered general models where Dβα is only assumed to be some class of functions from Dα to Dβ.48

把类型解释为集合，带来了一些发展，对后来扩充这一较有限框架也很重要。其中之一是模型 D 与 E 之间的逻辑关系：以类型为指标的关系族 Rα ⊆ Dα × Eα，满足⁴⁹

The interpretation of types as sets led to developments that became important also for later extensions of this rather limited framework. One of these was the notion of logical relation between models D and E, defined as a family of relations Rα ⊆Dα × Eα indexed over types, such that49

Rα→β(f,g)⇔∀x∈Dα∀y∈Eα(Rα(x,y)⇒Rβ(f·x,g·y))

逻辑关系的早期应用之一，是哈维·弗里德曼于 1970 年给出、发表于 [Friedman, 1975] 的完备性证明：类型化 βη 转换相对于简单类型 λ 演算任一建立在无限个体集上的标准模型完备。证明利用模型部分同态的性质：D 到 E 的部分同态，是每个类型 α 上的部分满射族 fα : Dα ⇀ Eα，满足 fβ(x·α,βy) ≃ fα→β(x)·α,βfα(y)。部分同态的图便是 D 到 E 的逻辑关系。

An early instance of the use of logical relations was the proof of completeness of typed βη-conversion with respect to any standard model (over an infinite set of individuals) of simply typed λ-calculus, given by Harvey Friedman in 1970 and published in [Friedman, 1975]. This result exploited the properties of partial homomorphisms of models, where a partial homomorphism from D to E is defined as a family of partial surjections f α : Dα ⇀Eα from D to E for each type α, with the property that f β(x ·α,β y) ≃f α→β(x) ·α,β f α(y). The graph of a partial homomorphism is then a logical relation from D to E.

后来研究表明，逻辑关系为表述和证明简单类型 λ 演算的主要性质提供方便框架。句法方面，泰特式正规化证明中各种“可计算性”概念都可描述为逻辑关系，合流性、η 延后等其他归约性质也可以 [Statman, 1985]。语义方面，应用结构的外延塌缩使用逻辑部分等价关系，这是杰夫·祖克 1971 年的结果，报告于 [Troelstra, 1973, §2.4.5]，另见 [Statman, 1983]。[Plotkin, 1973] 用特殊逻辑关系类刻画无类型 λ 演算 D∞ 模型和简单类型 λ 演算标准模型中的可定义性；“逻辑关系”这一名称似乎首次出现于此，并归于迈克·戈登。另见 [Plotkin, 1980]。

Later research showed that logical relations afford a convenient framework for formulating and proving the main properties of simply typed λ-calculi. On the syntactic side, the various forms of the notion of “computability” used in Tait-style normalization proofs can be described as logical relations, as well as some other properties of reduction like confluence and η-postponement, [Statman, 1985]. On the semantic side, the extensional collapse of an applicative structure uses logical partial equivalence relations (a result from 1971 of JeffZucker, reported in [Troelstra, 1973, §2.4.5]; see also [Statman, 1983]). Special classes of logical relations were used in the characterization of definability in D∞models of the type-free λ-calculus and in standard models of the simply-typed λ-calculus by [Plotkin, 1973] (where the name ‘logical relation’, attributed to Mike Gordon, seems to have first appeared). See also [Plotkin, 1980].

20 世纪 50 年代对高阶可计算性概念的研究，产生许多类型结构，乃至简单类型 λ 的模型，其中函数类型被解释为某个可计算函数或泛函的集合。⁵⁰ 从我们的角度看，尤其重要的是寻找适当的部分可计算泛函概念；这导向理查德·普拉泰克斯坦福博士论文研究的部分单调泛函 [Platek, 1966]，另见 [Longley, 2001, §4.1.1] 和 [Moldestad, 1977]。

The research on higher-order notions of computability in the 1950s produced a host of type structures or even models for simply typed λ in which function types are interpreted by some set of computable function(al)s.50 Especially important from our standpoint is the search for a suitable notion of partial computable functional, leading to the partial monotone functionals studied in Richard Platek’s Stanford PhD thesis [Platek, 1966] (see [Longley, 2001, §4.1.1] and [Moldestad, 1977]).

48　丘奇系统有外延性公理；没有这一公理时，模型定义比 [Henkin, 1950] 复杂，例如见 [Barendregt, 1981, 2nd edn., Ch.5] 或 [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, §11A]。49　下文以如今通行的 α → β 代替丘奇的 βα。50　该领域的全面历史，特别是它与本文主题的诸多交汇，见 [Longley, 2001]。该文 §3.3.1 还指出，[Kreisel, 1959] 是后来 λ 发展中许多关键思想的来源。

48Church’s system had an extensionality axiom; for systems without this, the definition of model would be more complicated than in [Henkin, 1950]; see, for example, [Barendregt, 1981, 2nd edn., Ch.5] or [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, §11A]. 49From now on we write the nowadays-common notation α →β instead of Church’s βα. 50For a thorough historical account of this field, highlighting its many intersections with our present subject, we refer the reader to [Longley, 2001]. Longley (ibid., §3.3.1) also points out [Kreisel, 1959] as the source of many ideas that have played a key role in the later development of λ.

普拉泰克强调的泛函上的序，加上麦卡锡的计算数学理论 [McCarthy, 1963a]，构成达纳·斯科特于 1969 年 10 月撰写、私下传阅的打字稿 [Scott, 1969e] 所述高类型可计算函数理论的基础，另见 [Scott, 1969d]。斯科特提出一种有类型的替代语言，取代 [Böhm, 1966; Böhm and Gross, 1966] 与 [Landin, 1966b] 的无类型编程语言。它相当于简单类型 λ 演算，基本类型 ι 表示整数，o 表示真值，并对所有类型 τ 包含泛函 Y:(τ→τ)→τ，用于形成 τ→τ 类型函数的最小不动点，这受到普拉泰克论文启发；还包含麦卡锡的条件算子。此外有常量 Ωι 与 Ωo，分别表示“未定义”的整数和真值，从而可把部分数值函数描述为连续全函数，在某些参数处取值 Ωι。预期模型把类型解释为域，即具有最小元素 ⊥D 和递增链最小上界的偏序集 D。域上的函数要求连续，即保持链的最小上界。系统判断利用值之间的语义序，特别是有如下归纳规则：

The idea of an ordering on functionals, stressed by Platek, together with the mathematical theory of computation of McCarthy [McCarthy, 1963a], are at the basis of the theory of computable functions of higher type described in a privately circulated typescript written in October 1969 by Dana Scott [Scott, 1969e] (see also [Scott, 1969d]). Scott proposed there a typed alternative to the (type-free) programming languages introduced in [Böhm, 1966; Böhm and Gross, 1966] and [Landin, 1966b], which amounted to a simply typed λ-calculus with ground types ι for integers and o for truth values, and containing a functional Y : (τ →τ) →τ, for all types τ, for forming (least) fixed-points of functions of type τ →τ (suggested by Platek’s thesis), and McCarthy’s conditional operator. In addition, there were constants Ωι and Ωo denoting the “undefined” integer and truth value, respectively, so that partial numerical functions could be described as continuous total functions that may take value Ωι at some argument. The intended model for this system was based on the interpretation of types as domains, i.e. partially ordered sets D with a least element ⊥D and least upper bounds of increasing chains. Functions over domains were required to be continuous: these are the functions that preserve least upper bounds of chains. The judgements of the system exploited the semantical ordering between values: notably, there was a rule of induction of the form:

Φ⊢g(Ω)≤h(Ω)Φ,g(x)≤h(x)⊢g(f(x))≤h(f(x))Φ⊢g(Y(f))≤h(Y(f))

斯科特认为，与例如麦卡锡工作 [McCarthy, 1963a, §8] 产生的递归归纳原理相比，这条规则是系统的主要优点 [Scott, 1969e, §3]。

This rule was considered by Scott to be the main advantage of the system (Scott [Scott, 1969e, §3]), as compared e.g. to the principle of recursion induction arising from the work of McCarthy [McCarthy, 1963a, §8].

所得系统成为 LCF 项目的基础。LCF 是“可计算函数逻辑”的缩写，这是罗宾·米尔纳给斯科特系统的名称。1972 年起，米尔纳领导的斯坦福小组开发该项目。[Gordon et al., 1979] 中的函数式编程语言 ML，最初就是为检验某个 LCF 版本中的证明而开发，见 [Gordon, 2000]。此外，其不含逻辑的类型化 λ 演算部分，成为 PCF 语言，即“可计算函数的编程语言”[Plotkin, 1977]。它广泛用作理论研究中的原型函数式语言，尤其用于研究完全抽象这一重要性质，见后文 §10。

The resulting system was the basis of the LCF project (LCF stands for “Logic for Computable Functions”, the name given to Scott’s system by Robin Milner) developed from 1972 onward by a group at Stanford University led by Milner. The functional programming language ML in [Gordon et al., 1979] was originally developed in order to check proofs in a version of LCF, see [Gordon, 2000]. Furthermore, the typed λ-calculus that constitutes its logic-free part became the language PCF (“Programming language for Computable Functions”, [Plotkin, 1977]), that is widely used as the prototype functional programming language for many theoretical purposes, in particular the study of the important property of full abstraction (see later, §10).

8.1.3　类型作为对象

8.1.3 Types as objects

类型论与范畴论之间的联系，是当今大量研究的基础，其发展沿着约阿希姆·兰贝克与 F. 威廉·劳威尔工作的路线展开。

The connection between type theory and category theory that underlies a large amount of current research shows up along the lines set by the works of Joachim Lambek and F. William Lawvere.

劳威尔在 20 世纪 60 年代初认识到，他后来称为范畴动力学 [Lawvere, 1979] 的领域中，某些一般性的基本数学变换，实际上也是若干对基础研究有意义的其他范畴的基本结构成分。这些变换，正是后来用于定义笛卡尔闭范畴的变换 [Eilenberg and Kelly, 1966]。

The latter realized in the early 1960s that certain basic mathematical transformations of a general nature in what he later called categorical dynamics [Lawvere, 1979] were in fact also basic structural ingredients of several other categories of foundational interest. In fact, these transformations were those used later to define the notion of cartesian closed category [Eilenberg and Kelly, 1966].

兰贝克虽未强调 [Lambek, 1958; Lambek, 1961] 中句法演算结果的范畴论内容，但这里仍提及其思想，因为它们提供了把笛卡尔闭范畴应用于 λ 演算的另一条路线：通过演绎系统，以及受组合逻辑抽象算法（上文 §3）启发的相关函数完备性概念。后来他明确形式化地描述句法演算与闭范畴的联系 [Lambek, 1968; Lambek, 1988; Lambek, 1995]；回头看，这为他与劳威尔的工作提供了统一框架。

Though Lambek did not stress the category theoretic content of his results on the syntactic calculus in [Lambek, 1958; Lambek, 1961], we mention his ideas here because they represent an alternative route to the applications of cartesian closed categories to λ-calculus via deductive systems and a related notion of functional completeness, inspired by the abstraction algorithms of combinatory logic (§3 above). Furthermore, he later explicitly described formally the connection of the syntactic calculus with closed categories [Lambek, 1968; Lambek, 1988; Lambek, 1995], providing, with hindsight, the unifying framework for his work and that of Lawvere.

兰贝克用 §8.1.1 所述范畴语法系统，作为在结合环上双模函子之间构造典范映射的形式工具。⁵¹ 这里利用了句法类型构造子与双模运算之间的下述对应：

Lambek used the system of categorial grammar described in §8.1.1 as a formal tool in the construction of canonical mappings between functors of bimodules over associative rings.51 There, the following correspondence between the constructors of syntactic types and operation on bimodules was exploited:

AB≔A⊗SB;C/B≔HomT(B,C);A\C≔HomR(A,C)

其中 A 为 R-S 双模，B 为 S-T 双模，C 为 R-T 双模。这个例子显然具有闭范畴结构，其形式就是 [Lambek, 1968] 所称的剩余范畴。事实上，早在第一篇发表的句法演算论文 [Lambek, 1958, §10] 中，兰贝克就注意到，句法类型演算的左右除法，与格论中的剩余运算在形式上相似；后者通常用于在代数逻辑中解释蕴涵。

for an R-S-bimodule A, an S-T-bimodule B and an R-T-bimodule C. There is an evident closed category structure in this example, in the form of what was called a residuated category in [Lambek, 1968]. In fact, already in his first published paper on the syntactic calculus, [Lambek, 1958, §10], Lambek noted the formal similarity between the operation of (right and left) division in the calculus of syntactic types and residuation in lattice theory, the operation normally used to interpret implication in algebraic logic.

笛卡尔闭范畴概念逐渐形成，主要通过劳威尔的工作。1959 年，他认识到指数对象 BA 的核心作用，其特征由同构 (C×A→B) ≅ (C→BA) 给出。⁵² 这种变换的早期例子来自物体运动的描述：可视为映射 T×M→E，给出每个时刻每个粒子的位置；等价地，也可视为物体随时间变化的轨迹 T→EM，或各粒子经过的路径 M→ET [Lawvere, 1976, p.123]。

The notion of cartesian closed category emerged gradually, basically through the work of Lawvere who in 1959 became aware of the central role of exponential objects BA, characterized by the isomorphisms C × A →B ∼= C →BA.52 An early example of this transformation came from the description of the motion of a material body seen as a mapping T × M →E that gives the position of each of its particles at each time instant, or equivalently, as its trajectory T →EM through time, or as the path M →ET followed by each of its particles, [Lawvere, 1976, p.123].

对指数对象的研究，对笛卡尔闭范畴概念的发展起了重要作用。劳威尔博士论文的引言章 [Lawvere, 1963] 描述了范畴的范畴，其中两个对象，即范畴 A、B 的指数，是从 A 到 B 的全部函子构成的范畴。⁵³ 后来在 1963—1964 年，他为建立分析的基本框架，研究集合范畴的初等⁵⁴ 公理系统 [Lawvere, 1964]：两个对象 A、B 的指数 BA 在此是定义域 A、陪域 B 的全体函数集。这项工作首次提及指数与 λ 抽象的关系，但没有形式细节。1961—1962 年在伯克利期间，劳威尔已注意到海廷代数是笛卡尔闭范畴。更一般地，具有有限积的预序集有一个幺半结构；它是闭的，当且仅当预序具有剩余运算，即还满足等价式：

There are other instances of exponentiation that played an important role in the development of the notion of cartesian closed category. The introductory chapter of Lawvere’s PhD thesis [Lawvere, 1963] contains the description of the category of categories, where the exponential of two objects (namely, categories) A and B is the category of all functors from A to B.53 Later, in the years 1963–64, Lawvere investigated an elementary54 axiom system for the category of sets in order to develop a basic framework for analysis, [Lawvere, 1964]: the exponential BA of two objects A and B is here the set of all functions with domain A and codomain B. The relation of exponentiation to λ-abstraction is mentioned for the first time in this work, but no formal detail is given. During his stay in Berkeley in 1961–62, Lawvere had noted that Heyting algebras are cartesian closed categories: more generally, a preordered set with finite products has a monoidal structure which is closed if and only if the preorder is residuated, that is, iffit also satisfies the equivalence:

p∧q≤r⇔p≤(q⇒r)

51　这一应用似乎是发展句法演算的主要数学动机之一，源自兰贝克与 G. D. 芬德利 1955 年的合作，描述于未发表手稿 [Lambek and Findlay, 1955]，摘要见 [Lambek, 1961, Appendix II]。据 [Lambek, 1988, p.297]，他早已明白该形式体系与自然语言的联系，但直到后来查阅文献才发现范畴语法论文 [Bar-Hillel, 1953]。52　直观地说，这是把二元映射 f:C×A→B 变成一元映射 f̂:C→BA，其输出是函数；见 §3 的柯里化。53　后来发表于 [Lawvere, 1966]。54　这里“初等”指公理是多类一阶语言的语句，例如一类表示映射，另一类表示对象。

51This application seems to have been one main mathematical motivation for the development of the syntactic calculus. It originated from work carried out by Lambek jointly with G. D. Findlay in 1955, described in the manuscript [Lambek and Findlay, 1955] (unpublished, but summarized in [Lambek, 1961, Appendix II]). According to [Lambek, 1988, p.297], the relation of this formalism to natural language was already clear to him at an early stage, but it was only later that, through a bibliographic search, he discovered the paper [Bar-Hillel, 1953] on categorial grammars. 52Intuitively, these may be thought of as the tranformation of a two-argument mapping f : C × A →B into the one-argument mapping ˆf : C →BA which outputs a function as value. See §3, currying. 53This was later published as [Lawvere, 1966]. 54In this context, “elementary” refers to the fact that the axioms are sentences of a multi-sorted first-order language—for example, with one sort for maps and another for objects.

其中 ⇒ 是剩余运算，例子见 [Eilenberg and Kelly, 1966, Ch.IV, §4]。这是通向后来所谓“命题即类型”对应（§8.1.4）的一条可能路线。

where ⇒is the residuation operation. (This example appears in [Eilenberg and Kelly, 1966, Chapter IV, §4]). This is one of the possible ways of arriving at what was later called the “propositions as types” correspondence (§8.1.4).

劳威尔以如下伴随情形描述带指数的范畴 C 的一般概念 [Lawvere, 1969a]；在与艾伦伯格和凯利讨论后，它被称为笛卡尔闭范畴 [Eilenberg and Kelly, 1966, Ch.IV, §2]：

A general notion of category C with exponentiation, that took the name of cartesian closed category after discussions with Eilenberg and Kelly [Eilenberg and Kelly, 1966, Chapter IV, §2], was described by Lawvere by the following adjoint situations [Lawvere, 1969a]:

  • 一个终对象，由函子 1:1→C 给出，它右伴随于从 C 到单对象范畴 1 的唯一函子；

    – a terminal object, given by a functor 1 : 1 →C right adjoint to the unique functor from C to the category 1 with one object;

  • 双函子 ×:C×C→C，即笛卡尔积，右伴随于对角函子 Δ:C→C×C，后者把 C 的对象 C 映为 C×C 的对象 ⟨C,C⟩；最后，

    – a bifunctor × : C × C →C (cartesian product) right adjoint to the diagonal functor ∆: C →C × C that transforms an object C of C into the object ⟨C, C⟩ of C × C; and finally

  • 对 C 的每个对象 A，函子 A×(·):C→C 有右伴随 (·)ᴬ:C→C。每个伴随 A×(·) ⊣ (·)ᴬ 的余单位，是以 C 的对象 B 为指标的态射族 εB:Bᴬ×A→B，称为求值。

    – a right adjoint (·)A : C →C to the functor A × (·) : C →C, for each object A of C. The counit of each adjunction A × (·) ⊣(·)A is a family of morphisms indexed by objects B of C, εB : BA × A →B, called evaluation.

劳威尔未发表手稿《范畴值高阶逻辑》突出了笛卡尔闭范畴与类型论的明确联系。该手稿于 1967 年洛杉矶集合论研讨会上传阅，内容后来发表于 [Lawvere, 1969a; Lawvere, 1969b; Lawvere, 1970a]。这些论文的核心范畴概念是超学说，旨在捕捉一些后来导向初等拓扑斯概念的例子 [Lawvere, 1970b]。超学说由类型的笛卡尔闭范畴 T 定义，其态射视为项。对 T 中每个类型 X，都有 X 型属性的笛卡尔闭范畴 P(X)，其态射称为 X 上的演绎。P(X) 的终对象视为 X 型恒真属性，笛卡尔积和指数分别表示属性的合取与蕴涵，求值则是分离规则。对 T 中每个项 f:X→Y，有如下伴随：

The explicit connection between cartesian closed categories and type theory was highlighted in Lawvere’s unpublished manuscript “Category-valued higher-order logic”, distributed in 1967 to the Los Angeles Set Theory Symposium, whose contents were later published as [Lawvere, 1969a; Lawvere, 1969b; Lawvere, 1970a]. The central categorical notion in these papers is that of a hyperdoctrine, and was intended to capture some of the examples that would later lead to the notion of elementary topos [Lawvere, 1970b]. A hyperdoctrine is defined by a cartesian closed category T of types, whose morphisms are regarded as terms. For each type X in T, there is a cartesian closed category P(X) of attributes of type X, whose morphisms are called deductions over X. The terminal object of P(X) is regarded as the identically true attribute of type X, whereas cartesian product and exponentiation represent conjunction and implication of attributes, respectively, evaluation being modus ponens. For every term f : X →Y in T, there are adjoint situations X

Σf⊣f*⊣Πf

它们依次对应存在量化、沿 f 的代换和全称量化。

that correspond to existential quantification, substitution along f, and universal quantification, respectively.

超学说的例子当然包括句法超学说：类型是形式类型论的类型符号，X 型属性是自由变量具有与 X 对应类型的公式。从本文观点看，更有趣的例子（另见后文 §8.1.4）受 H. 洛伊希利工作启发 [Läuchli, 1970]。其中以集合论构造概念，给出直觉主义谓词演算的完备语义；其灵感来自克林的可实现性，以及古德曼、克赖泽尔、特勒尔斯特拉和斯科特的构造理论。⁵⁵ 在此例中，T 是集合范畴 Set；对视为类型的任意集合 X，X 型属性范畴是 Set/X，因此每个 X 型属性 φ 可视为以 X 为指标的集合族，第 x 个分量记为 x·φ。这个超学说一个值得注意的特点，是 X 型属性范畴中逻辑联结词的解释，例如 [Lawvere, 1970a]：

Examples of hyperdoctrines include, of course, the syntactical hyperdoctrine whose types are the type symbols of a formal type theory and whose attributes of type X are the formulas whose free variables have types corresponding to X. More interesting from the present point of view (see also later, §8.1.4), is an example suggested by the work of H. Läuchli [Läuchli, 1970], in which a complete semantics for intuitionistic predicate calculus is described in terms of a set-theoretic notion of construction inspired by Kleene’s notion of realizability and the theories of constructions of Goodman, Kreisel, Troelstra and Scott.55 In this example T is the category Set of sets, and for any set X seen as a type, the category of attributes of type X is Set/X, so that each attribute φ of type X may be regarded as an X-indexed family of sets, whose x-th coordinate is written as x · φ. A noteworthy feature of this hyperdoctrine is the interpretation of the logical connectives in the category of attributes of type X, for example [Lawvere, 1970a]:

55　[Läuchli, 1970] 收于 1968 年布法罗会议论文集，但较早手稿已于 1967 年春传阅，同年劳威尔与达纳·斯科特在旧金山讨论过它。另见 [Lawvere, 1996]，由此可知，《范畴值高阶逻辑》手稿也在自动证明研讨会上讨论；会上报告了把证明解释为类型化 λ 项的两篇基础论文 [Scott, 1970a] 与 [Bruijn, 1970]。§8.1.4 将讨论劳威尔与斯科特工作的关系。

55[Läuchli, 1970] appeared in the proceedings of a conference held at Buffalo in 1968, but an earlier manuscript was circulated already in the Spring of 1967 and was discussed by Lawvere and Dana Scott in San Francisco in the same year. See also [Lawvere, 1996], from which it appears that his manuscript “Category-valued higher-order logic” was discussed also at the symposium on Automatic Demonstration, where the two fundamental papers on the interpretation of proofs as typed λ-terms, [Scott, 1970a] and [Bruijn, 1970], were presented. We shall discuss later, in §8.1.4, the relation between the works of Lawvere and Scott.

φ ⊃ ψ 是一个集合族，其第 x 个分量为 (x·ψ)^(x·φ)。展开定义，X 上 φ ⊃ ψ 的证明，是一个函数：对每个 x∈X，把 x·φ 的证明映为 x·ψ 的证明。

– φ ⊃ψ is a family of sets whose x-th coordinate is (x · ψ)(x·φ). Unfolding the definitions, this means that a proof over X of φ ⊃ψ is a function which, for every x ∈X, assigns a proof of x · ψ to a proof of x · φ;

y·Σf(φ) = Σf(x)=y x·φ。这表示，Y 上 Σf(φ) 的证明，是给每个 y∈Y 指派有序对 ⟨x,d⟩ 的函数，其中 f(x)=y，d 是 x·φ 的证明。

– y · P f(φ) = P f(x)=y x · φ. This means that a proof over Y of P f(φ) is a function assigning to every y ∈Y an ordered pair ⟨x, d⟩where f(x) = y and d is a proof of x · φ.

与此同时，兰贝克在一系列关于“演绎系统与范畴”的论文中，利用他所称演绎系统上的等式理论描述范畴 [Lambek, 1968; Lambek, 1969; Lambek, 1972]。这些系统实质上是有向多重图：每个结点 A 有边 1A:A→A，每对边 f:A→B、g:B→C 有边 g∘f:A→C。对于笛卡尔闭范畴，合适的演绎系统还有以下结构 [Lambek, 1974; Lambek, 1980a]：

In the meantime, in a series of papers on “deductive systems and categories”, Lambek was exploiting the description of categories by means of equational theories over what he called deductive systems [Lambek, 1968; Lambek, 1969; Lambek, 1972]. These are essentially (oriented multi)graphs with an edge 1A : A →A for every node A and an edge g ◦f : A →C for every pair of edges f : A →B and g : B →C. In the case of cartesian closed categories, the right kind of deductive system has the following additional structure [Lambek, 1974; Lambek, 1980a]:

  • 结点 T，以及每个结点 A 对应的边 0A:A→T；

    – a node T with an edge 0A : A →T for each node A;

  • 每对结点 A、B 对应结点 A×B，带有边 πA,B:A×B→A、π′A,B:A×B→B；每对边 f:C→A、g:C→B 对应边 ⟨f,g⟩:C→A×B；

    – a node A × B for every pair of nodes A, B, with edges πA,B : A × B →A and π′ A,B : A × B →B and ⟨f, g⟩: C →A × B for every pair of edges f : C →A and g : C →B;

  • 每对结点 A、B 对应结点 Bᴬ，以及边 εB,A:Bᴬ×A→B；每条边 h:C×A→B 对应边 ĥ:C→Bᴬ。

    – a node BA for every pair of nodes A, B, and edges εB,A : BA × A →B and ˆh : C →BA for every edge h : C × A →B.

注意，这样描述的演绎系统是中性的：结点和边既可解释为正直觉主义命题演算的公式与证明，也可分别解释为笛卡尔闭范畴的对象与态射。因此，演绎系统中边之间的等式，既可描述直觉主义相继式证明的某种等价，⁵⁶ 也可作为笛卡尔闭范畴的等式呈现。

Observe that a deductive system as described here is neutral as to the interpretation of its nodes and edges as formulas and proofs of the positive intuitionistic propositional calculus or as objects and morphisms of a cartesian closed category, respectively. Therefore, equations between edges of a deductive system can be interpreted either as describing a notion of equivalence of proofs for intuitionistic sequents56 or as the equational presentation of a cartesian closed category.

笛卡尔闭范畴的等式呈现，使兰贝克能流畅地证明其函数完备性 [Lambek, 1972; Lambek, 1974]，从而完全明确劳威尔已暗示的、与类型化 λ 演算的联系。若 D 是演绎系统，则公式 A 的假设可视为不定元，即新边 x:T→A。向 D 加入不定元 x 所得系统 D[x] 的边 φ(x):B→C，既可解释为依赖假设 x 的直觉主义相继式 B→C 的证明，也可解释为笛卡尔闭范畴中的多项式。[Lambek, 1974, Th.3.6] 证明的下述性质，因此同时涵盖正直觉主义命题演算的演绎定理，以及具有有限积和满射配对的简单类型 λ 演算的函数完备性：

The equational presentation of cartesian closed categories allowed Lambek to give a smooth proof of their functional completeness [Lambek, 1972; Lambek, 1974], thus making fully explicit the connection with typed λ-calculus already hinted at by Lawvere. If D is a deductive system, then an assumption of a formula A may be regarded as an indeterminate, i.e. as a new edge x : T →A. The edges φ(x) : B →C of a deductive system D[x] obtained by adding to D the indeterminate x can be interpreted either as proofs of an intuitionistic sequent B →C depending on the assumption x, or as polynomials in a cartesian closed category. The following property, proved in [Lambek, 1974, Th. 3.6], subsumes therefore both the deduction theorem of the positive intuitionistic propositional calculus and the functional completeness of a simply typed λ-calculus with finite products (and surjective pairing):

对 D[x:T→A] 中每个 φ(x):B→C，都有 D 中的 f:A×B→C 与之关联，使得

With every φ(x):B →C in D[x: T →A] there is associated in D an f : A×B →C such that

f∘⟨x∘0B,1B⟩=xφ(x):B→C

其中 =x 是把 D 的边等价关系扩张到 D[x] 所得的关系。

where =x is the extension of the equivalence of edges of D to D[x].

56　证明等价是兰贝克工作中反复出现的主题：它早已是研究句法演算的动机之一，导向一种基于 [Lambek, 1968] 的范畴论协调定理证明技术 [Kelly and Mac Lane, 1972]，也为 [Szabo, 1974; Szabo, 1978] 与 [Mann, 1975] 所研究的证明范畴分析开辟道路。

56The theme of equivalence of proofs is a recurring one in Lambek’s works: it was already a motivation for his study of the syntactic calculus, leading to a technique for proving coherence theorems in category theory [Kelly and Mac Lane, 1972] based on [Lambek, 1968], and also opened the way for the categorical analysis of proofs pursued in [Szabo, 1974; Szabo, 1978] and [Mann, 1975].

函数抽象 λx:A.φ(x) 于是直接定义为 ψ̂:B→Cᴬ，其中 ψ=f∘⟨π′B,A,πB,A⟩。类型论范畴形式化的一个成果，是使人能谈论类型，而不必假定它们严格说来就是集合，因为结构足够丰富的范畴具有内部逻辑，仍能在相当程度上重现集合论论证。劳威尔在初等拓扑斯研究中系统利用了这一点，把它们视为“可变集合”的宇宙 [Lawvere, 1970b; Lawvere, 1972; Lawvere, 1976]。

Functional abstraction λx: A . φ(x) is then just defined to be bψ : B →CA, where ψ = f ◦⟨π′ B,A, πB,A⟩. One of the outcomes of the categorical formalization of type theories consisted in providing a way of speaking of types without necessarily assuming that they are sets in a strict sense, because categories with a rich enough structure have an internal logic that still allows one to reproduce set-theoretical arguments to a significant extent. This was exploited systematically by Lawvere in his work on elementary topoi, regarded as universes of “variable sets”, [Lawvere, 1970b; Lawvere, 1972; Lawvere, 1976].

这一洞见为域理论最先进的发展奠定基础，尤其是把综合域理论（下文 §10）作为统一构造数学与计算理论的全面框架。

This insight set the stage for the most advanced developments in domain theory, especially the idea of synthetic domain theory (§10 below) as a comprehensive framework unifying constructive mathematics and computation theory.

8.1.4　类型作为命题

8.1.4 Types as propositions

把类型解释为笛卡尔闭范畴的对象，并注意到这种范畴恰好对应正直觉主义命题演算，只是通向如今称为“命题即类型”对应的类型与公式类比的一条路线。如 §4.2 所述，这一对应最早发表的线索可追溯到 [Curry, 1934a] 与 [Curry, 1934b]；[Curry and Feys, 1958, §9E] 首次明确把它描述为：直觉主义蕴涵逻辑的可证公式，与可赋给闭项的类型表达式之间的同构。

The interpretation of types as objects of a cartesian closed category, and the observation that such a category corresponds precisely to positive intuitionistic propositional calculus, is only one way of arriving at the analogy between types and formulas that is nowadays called the “propositions as types” correspondence. As mentioned in §4.2, the first published hints of this correspondence date back to [Curry, 1934a] and [Curry, 1934b], and it was first described explicitly in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9E], as an isomorphism between the provable formulae of intuitionistic implicational logic and the type-expressions assignable to closed terms.

20 世纪 50 年代初，卡鲁·梅雷迪思观察到，该逻辑中的证明对应复合组合子，某些逻辑片段对应受限制的项集。但其观察直到后来才发表，见 C. A. Meredith 与 A. N. Prior《命题演算公理学札记》§7，《圣母大学形式逻辑杂志》4（1963），171–187 页。

(In the early 1950s, Carew Meredith had observed that proofs in this logic corresponded to composite combinators, and that certain fragments of this logic corresponded to restricted sets of terms, but his observations were not published till later, see §7 of Notes on the axiomatics of the propositional calculus by C. A. Meredith and A. N. Prior, Notre Dame Journal of Formal Logic 4 (1963), 171–187.)

1969 年，霍华德把柯里的对应扩展为：直觉主义逻辑相继式演算版本中的证明，与类型化 λ 项之间的对应。他还继 [Tait, 1965] 之后观察到，切消去是项正规化的结果。霍华德 1969 年手稿非正式传阅，经 [Prawitz, 1971] 与 [Girard, 1972] 后成为这一对应的经典来源，尤其在证明论文献中。它最终发表于 [Howard, 1980]。粗略地说，霍华德贡献背后的思想，可描述为把哥德尔有限类型原始递归泛函理论的类型集扩展到海廷算术公式，从而推广该理论。

In 1969 Curry’s correspondence was extended by Howard to one between proofs in a sequent calculus version of intuitionistic logic and typed λ-terms. He further observed (after [Tait, 1965]) that cut-elimination was a consequence of the normalization of terms. Howard’s 1969 manuscript circulated informally and became the locus classicus of the correspondence, especially in the proof-theoretic literature, after [Prawitz, 1971] and [Girard, 1972]. It was eventually published in [Howard, 1980]. (Roughly, the idea behind Howard’s contribution may be described as a generalization of Gödel’s theory of primitive recursive functionals of finite type by extending the set of types to formulas of Heyting arithmetic.)

大约同时，独立于这些证明论研究者、动机也很不同的另外两项工作，同样导向系统地围绕证明与项对应建立的形式体系。其技术与哲学洞见，至今仍是类型化 λ 演算用于数学证明机械化工作的基础。它们是达纳·斯科特关于“构造有效性”的工作 [Scott, 1970a]，明确以分析直觉主义者所用构造概念为动机；以及尼古拉斯·德布鲁因用于检验普通数学证明正确性的 Automath 语言族 [Bruijn, 1980]。

Independently of these proof-theorists and with very different motivations, but at about the same time, there were also two other efforts that led to formalisms centred systematically around the correspondence between proofs and terms, and whose technical and philosophical insights still underlie current work on applications of typed λ-calculi to the mechanization of mathematical proofs. These were Dana Scott’s work on “constructive validity” [Scott, 1970a], motivated explicitly by an attempt to analyze the notion of construction as used by the intuitionists, and Nicolaas de Bruijn’s Automath family of languages for checking the correctness of ordinary mathematical proofs, [Bruijn, 1980].

Automath 项目约始于 1967 年：德布鲁因开始考虑设计一种形式语言，使完全形式化的数学证明能由计算机程序检验正确性 [Bruijn, 1970]。⁵⁷ Automath 语言的设计不依赖任何数学基础哲学立场；事实上，只需问机器为检验正确性需要哪些信息，就能描述 Automath 文本（术语中称为一本书）的整体格式。文本先分为行，每行由以下部分构成：（1）标识符，供书中后文引用该行；（2）定义，说明标识符是原始概念、自由变量，还是由此前标识符形成的复合表达式缩写；（3）上下文，记录该行哪些假设有效、哪些标识符当前可见；最后，（4）标识符的范畴，包括原始范畴 type。⁵⁸ Automath 的上下文结构以自然演绎格式描述证明，其灵感来自 Algol 的分块结构 [Naur and others, 1963] 与数学家组织证明方式的相似性。⁵⁹ 德布鲁因熟悉 λ 记号，并在分析工作中系统使用；因此自然地把有类型 λ 项纳入 Automath，通过绑定自由变量撤销假设，使上下文既能扩张，也能缩小。

The starting point of the Automath project was around 1967, when de Bruijn began to look at the problem of devising a formal language such that the correctness of a completely formalized mathematical proof can be verified by a computer program [Bruijn, 1970].57 The design of the Automath languages did not depend on any philosophical position concerning the foundations of mathematics; in fact, it is possible to describe the overall format of an Automath text (called a book in the Automath jargon) by asking what items of information a machine would need in order to check its correctness. First of all, the text is subdivided into lines, each of them composed of the following items: (1) an identifier to refer to that line later in the book; (2) a definition, indicating whether the identifier is a primitive notion, a free variable or the abbreviation of a compound expression formed from previous identifiers; (3) a context that keeps track of which assumptions are valid and which identifiers are currently visible at that line, and finally (4) a category for the identifier, including a primitive category type.58 The context structure of the Automath languages is a way of describing proofs in a natural deduction format, and was suggested by the similarity between the block structure of the programming language Algol [Naur and others, 1963] and the way mathematicians structure proofs.59 The λ-notation was familar to de Bruijn, who used it systematically in his work in analysis, and it was natural for him to include (typed) λ-terms in Automath in order to discharge assumptions by binding free variables, thus making contexts shrink as well as expand.

57　这里依据 [Bruijn, 1994]，其中有若干关于 Automath 语言的历史说明。更多细节见 [Bruijn, 1980]，以及 [Nederpelt et al., 1994] 的编者引言；后者收集了德布鲁因等人的 Automath 基础论文。

57Our description here is based on [Bruijn, 1994], which contains several remarks of historical interest on the Automath languages. More details can be found in [Bruijn, 1980], and the editors’ introduction to [Nederpelt et al., 1994], which is a collection of the basic papers on Automath by de Bruijn and others.

从本节观点看，更相关的是 Automath 每行范畴部分的解释。核心形式洞见，是观察到非形式数学话语中证明与对象扮演相同角色，从而统一处理二者。陈述定理 T 时，通常先声明对象范畴，例如“设 x 为整数”，以及对象须满足的性质，例如“假定 x>0”。定理结论于是陈述依赖自由名称 x 的性质；只要具体对象 k 是整数，并有 k>0 的证明，就可代入。因此，更忠实的定理表示应是 T(k,π)，同时显出对 k 及 k>0 的证明 π 的依赖。在上下文 Γ 的某行中，若标识符 a 的定义项是范畴 C 的表达式 e，则 a 有两种解释：C 型对象 e 的名称；或者，若 C 可解释为命题 P 的证明类型 Proof(P)，⁶⁰ 则是 P 的证明 e 的名称。类型在形式上不等同于命题；而是每个命题可赋予一个类型，即其证明类型，断言命题就相当于说其证明类型有居民 [Bruijn, 1970]。⁶¹ 这种“证明即对象”范式，特化为依照 [Heyting, 1956] 对逻辑常量的构造解释：例如，蕴涵 A⊃B 的构造是把 A 的每个构造映为 B 的构造的函数。[Bruijn, 1994, p.205] 承认，这是把证明类解释为类型的一条线索。⁶²

More relevant from the point of view of this Section is the interpretation of the category part of an Automath line. The leading formal insight here consists in unifying the treatment of proofs and objects by observing that in informal mathematical discourse the two notions play the same role. In the statement of a theorem T it is usual to declare the category of the objects involved, e.g. “Let x be an integer,” and the properties that these objects have to satisfy, e.g. “Assume that x > 0.” The conclusion of the theorem then states a property that depends on the (free) name x, which can be instantiated to any specific object k provided that k is an integer and that we have a proof that k > 0. Then the theorem T is more faithfully represented by an expression of the form T(k, π), showing its dependence on both k and a proof π that k > 0.
