In a line where the identifier a has the expression e of category C as definiens (in a context Γ), a has two possible interpretations: as the name of the object e of type C, or as the name of a proof e of a proposition P, provided that C can be interpreted as the type Proof(P) of proofs of P.60 Types are not formally identical to propositions; rather, to every proposition can be assigned a type, the type of its proofs, and asserting a proposition amounts to saying that the type of its proofs is inhabited [Bruijn, 1970].61 This paradigm of “proofs as objects” specializes to the constructive interpretation of logical constants following [Heyting, 1956] where, for example, a construction of an implication A ⊃B is a function that maps each construction of A to a construction of B. This is recognized in [Bruijn, 1994, p.205] to have been one of the clues to the interpretation of proof classes as types.62

Automath 语言族把类型化 λ 抽象记为 [x:A]B，其中 B 也可能是类型。若上下文 Γ 加入 x:A 的假设后，表达式 e（可能依赖 x）有类型 B，则抽象 [x:A]e 的类型是 [x:A]B。但注意，表达式 B 也可依赖变量 x:A，即 B 中可有 x 的自由出现。类型 [x:A]B 的一种集合论解释，是给每个 A 型参数 a 指派 B(a) 型结果的函数类型，可写作 Πx:A B(x)。⁶³

Typed λ-abstraction is denoted, in languages of the Automath family, by [x:A]B where B might be a type. If, in a context Γ extended with the assumption that x: A, the expression e (possibly depending on x) has type B, then the abstraction [x: A]e has type [x: A]B; but observe that here the expression B can also depend on the variable x: A (i.e., B may contain free occurrences of x). One interpretation of the type [x: A]B is set-theoretic: it is the type of functions that assign a result of type B(a) to every argument a of type A, a type that may be written as Q x:A B(x).63

58　从自然演绎证明的完整形式化出发，启发式说明 Automath 行格式的动机，见 [Daalen, 1980]。除标识符外，各部分对应通常的赋型判断 Γ⊢e:C，即上下文 Γ、表达式 e、范畴 C。59　见 [Bruijn, 1994]；德布鲁因所用自然演绎形式接近 [Fitch, 1952]。值得指出，Algol 60 的分块结构也使兰丁以 λ 演算形式分析该语言 [Landin, 1965]。60　德布鲁因论文把该类型记为 TRUE(P)。61　这类似克赖泽尔对 HAω 的修正可实现性 [Kreisel, 1959]：若 A、B 的实现子类型分别为 σ、τ，则 A⊃B 的实现子类型为函数集 σ→τ。可实现性历史见 [Oosten, 2002]。62　德布鲁因直接从海廷处学到逻辑常量意义的构造解释；1952—1960 年两人在阿姆斯特丹共事。

58For a heuristic explanation of how the format of Automath lines can be motivated starting from a complete formalization of natural deduction proofs, see [Daalen, 1980]. Observe that, except for the identifier part, the components of a line correspond to those of an ordinary typing judgement Γ ⊢e : C, for a context Γ, an expression e and a category C. 59See [Bruijn, 1994]; the natural deduction formalism used by de Bruijn is close to that used by [Fitch, 1952]. It is worth remarking that the block structure of Algol 60 led Landin to the formal analysis of that language by means of λ-calculus [Landin, 1965]. 60In de Bruijn’s papers, this type was denoted by TRUE(P). 61This is similar to Kreisel’s notion of modified realizability [Kreisel, 1959] for HAω, where the type of realizers of a formula A ⊃B is the function-set σ →τ, if σ is the type of realizers of A and τ of those of B. See [Oosten, 2002] for a history of realizability. 62The constructive explanation of the meaning of logical constants was learned by de Bruijn directly from Heyting, who was one of his colleagues in Amsterdam from 1952 to 1960.

当 B 不依赖 x 时，[x:A]B 只是函数类型 A→B 的另一记法。另一种解释是逻辑的：若 A、B 分别表示证明类 Proof(P)、Proof(Q)，则 [x:Proof(P)]Proof(Q) 表示证明类 Proof(∀x:P.Q)；若 Q 不含 x 的自由出现，公式 ∀x:P.Q 就特化为 P⊃Q。

When B does not depend on x, [x: A]B is just another notation for the function type A →B. Another interpretation is logical: if A and B represent the proof classes Proof(P) and Proof(Q), respectively, then the type [x: Proof(P)] Proof(Q) represents the proof class Proof(∀x: P. Q), where the formula ∀x: P. Q specializes to P ⊃Q when Q does not contain free occurrences of x.

Automath 语言族后来的语言 [Daalen, 1973] 采用更统一的方法：除 type 范畴外，还有 prop 范畴，意在表示命题证明的类型。若 A:prop，且在 x:A 时有 B:prop，则 [x:A]B 可读作 ∀x:A.B 的缩写；若 x:A 在 B 中不自由，则读作 A⊃B。

A more uniform approach is obtained in later languages of the Automath family, [Daalen, 1973], where besides the category type there is a category prop intended to represent the type of proofs of propositions. If A: prop and B : prop whenever x: A, then [x: A] B can be read as an abbreviation of ∀x: A. B and, when x: A is not free in B, of A ⊃B.

[Scott, 1970a] 的背景不同且更复杂。斯科特希望建立形式构造演算，落实海廷的主张：“逻辑定理与数学定理没有本质区别，因为二者都断言我们已成功完成满足某些条件的构造”[Heyting, 1958]。⁶⁴

A different and more complex background was behind [Scott, 1970a]. Scott was interested in setting up a formal calculus of constructions that would substantiate Heyting’s claim that “there is no essential difference between logical and mathematical theorems, because both sorts of theorems affirm that we have succeeded in performing constructions satisfying certain conditions” [Heyting, 1958].64

[Scott, 1970a] 处在几条线索的汇流处。一方面，克赖泽尔的论文 [Kreisel, 1962; Kreisel, 1965] 使斯科特关注寻找证明与构造理论的问题，以此奠定直觉主义逻辑的解释。古德曼继承这一计划 [Goodman, 1970]，以其 1968 年由斯科特指导的斯坦福博士论文为基础；他偏离原表述，允许以无类型组合子表示的部分函数。

The work [Scott, 1970a] lay at the confluence of several threads. On the one hand Kreisel’s papers [Kreisel, 1962; Kreisel, 1965] aroused Scott’s interest in the problem of finding a theory of proofs and constructions on which the interpretation of intuitionistic logic could be based. Kreisel’s project was taken up by Goodman [Goodman, 1970] (based on his PhD thesis in Stanford in 1968, supervised by Dana Scott), who deviated from the original formulation in allowing partial functions, represented by type-free combinators.

另一方面，洛伊希利 [Läuchli, 1965; Läuchli, 1970] 研究克林对直觉主义逻辑递归可实现性解释 [Kleene, 1945] 的集合论版本。上文 §8.1.3 已看到，这也影响劳威尔的范畴逻辑工作。⁶⁵

On the other hand, there was the work of Läuchli [Läuchli, 1965; Läuchli, 1970] on a set-theoretical version of Kleene’s recursive realizability interpretation of intuitionistic logic [Kleene, 1945], that we have seen in §8.1.3 above to have influenced also Lawvere’s work in categorical logic.65

斯科特把洛伊希利与劳威尔已利用的联系置于中心：全称量词对应一个以指标参数化的种类族的笛卡尔积；他也考虑其对偶，即这种族的和，显然对应存在量化。概括地说，斯科特系统是“以构造方式公理化函数及函数集合族理论的一次尝试”[Scott, 1970a, p.241]。⁶⁶ 系统使用直觉主义相继式 Δ⊢A，其中公式形如 A∈B 或 A=B，项 A、B 依上下文可表示函数或种类，这里的种类应理解为类型。项构造操作包括类型化函数抽象 λx∈A.B，满足标准规则，但特别排除规则 ξ，以允许内涵解释。该抽象的类型是依赖积，由如下规则给出：

Scott took as central the connection, already exploited by Läuchli and Lawvere, between the universal quantifier and the cartesian product of an indexed family of species, and considered also the dual notion of the sum of such a family, with the evident connection to existential quantification. In general terms, Scott’s system was “an attempt at axiomatizing in a constructive way a theory of both functions and families of sets of functions” [Scott, 1970a, p.241].66 The system of Scott uses intuitionistic sequents ∆⊢A where formulas A are of the two shapes A ∈B and A = B, for terms A, B that may denote functions or species depending on the context, where species here should be understood as types. Term-forming operations include typed functional abstraction λx∈A. B, which satisfies standard rules with the notable exception of rule (ξ) to allow for an intensional interpretation. The type of the above λ-abstract is a dependent product given by the rule:

63　若 B 中有自由 x，则这是依赖类型；其他例子见 §8.3 和 §5.4 的 G。64　[Scott, 1970a, pp.237–242] 详细说明其动机，也是本文叙述的依据。斯科特构造理论的早期发展还受 Automath 设计影响：1968 年 11 月，他在阿姆斯特丹休假期间，于特勒尔斯特拉研讨班报告了含德布鲁因语言讨论的初稿 [Scott, 1968]。同年 12 月，论文在凡尔赛自动证明会议宣读；会议论文集也收录 Automath 第一篇正式论文 [Bruijn, 1970]。65　俄罗斯逻辑学家尤·T. 梅德韦杰夫继柯尔莫哥洛夫之后，以有限问题解释直觉主义命题演算的正片段 [Medvedev, 1962]，与洛伊希利解释很接近，特别是二者都用笛卡尔积、不交并、集合指数解释合取、析取、蕴涵。66　以纯范畴论处理参数化集合族的尝试，约始于劳威尔 1970 年在达尔豪斯大学的讲座，在 [Lawvere, 1972] 中发展，导向指标范畴理论 [Johnstone et al., 1978]，可视为其早期超学说工作（§8.1.3）的延续。[Bénabou, 1985] 使用纤维化的路线与之相关；二者都与 §8.3 将讨论的依赖类型及高阶类型论语义有关。

63If B contains x free, this is a dependent type; for other examples, see §8.3 and G in §5.4. 64A detailed description of the motivations behind [Scott, 1970a], on which our account is based, is contained in that paper on pp.237–242. The development of Scott’s theory of constructions was also influenced at an early stage by the design of Automath: a preliminary version of the above paper, containing a discussion of de Bruijn’s language, was presented in November 1968 at Troelstra’s seminar [Scott, 1968], while Scott was on sabbatical leave in Amsterdam. Δ,x∈A⊢B∈CΔ⊢(λx∈A.B)∈(∀x∈A.C)

其中 x 不在 Δ 中出现。

where x does not occur in Δ.

这同时就是 ∀ 引入规则。对偶的消去规则，是应用的赋型规则：

which is at the same time the ∀-introduction rule. The dual elimination rule is the typing rule for application:

Δ,f∈(∀x∈A.B),x∈A⊢f(x)∈B

通常的非依赖函数类型成为 A→B ≔ ∀x∈A.B，其中 B 不依赖 x∈A。在这一认同下，类型与命题的对应变得明显；事实上，该构造演算中的如下定理：

The usual (non-dependent) function type becomes A →B =def ∀x ∈A . B where B does not depend on x ∈A. It is under this identification that the correspondence between types and propositions becomes evident, and in fact a theorem of this calculus of constructions like

x∈A⊢[∀f∈(∀x∈A.B).f(x)]∈(∀x∈A.B)→B

就是逻辑中的全称实例化公理 [Scott, 1970a, p.248]。该文第 260 页对这一对应的一般机制给出最清晰的说明：

is the logical axiom of universal instantiation [Scott, 1970a, p.248]. The clearest statement of how the correspondence works in general is to be found in this paper (p.260):

“我们最初把种类和构造视为数学对象，发现有一些简单公理支配其性质。后来逐渐看出，这些性质与形式逻辑中熟悉的性质高度类似。于是，我们坚持把逻辑公式读作，更准确地说，解释为构造理论中具有数学意义的项，从而把这一类比变成信条。这种解释要求，通过给出属于公式之解释的显式构造来断言有效性。有效性通过从构造公理证明相应隶属断言来确立。……命题不再简单退化为两个真值之一，而是由一个复杂的可能构造种类表示，这些构造在设想中可用于验证它。”

“We began by regarding species and constructions as mathematical objects and found that there were some simple axioms governing their properties. It then became slowly apparent that these properties were highly analogous to properties familiar from formal logic. We then turned this analogy into a dogma by insisting that the logical formulas be read (better: interpreted) as (mathematically meaningful) terms of the theory of constructions. This interpretation requires that validity be asserted by the act of giving an explicit construction belonging to the (interpretation of the) formula. Validity is established by giving a proof from the axioms for constructions of the membership assertion. [. . . ] a proposition does not simply degenerate to one of two truth values but instead is represented by a complex species of possible constructions that conceivably can be used in its validation.”

斯科特对这一对应的完整描述，几乎逐字遵循 [Heyting, 1956; Heyting, 1958] 对逻辑常量意义的说明：

Scott’s full description of the correspondence follows almost verbatim the explanations of the meaning of the logical constants given in [Heyting, 1956; Heyting, 1958]:

公式构造
A∧B一对构造，第一个证成 A，第二个证成 B。
A∨B第一分量为 0 或 1 的有序对；为 0 时第二分量证成 A，为 1 时证成 B。
⊤证成为 0，因为 ⊤≔{0}。
⊥不存在已知证成，因为 ⊥≔{}。
∀x∈A.B把每个 a∈A 映为 B(a) 的证成的构造。
∃x∈A.B第一分量为某个 a∈A、第二分量证成 B(a) 的有序对。
FormulaConstruction
A ∧ Ba pair of constructions, the first of which justifies A, and the second B
A ∨ Ba pair whose first coordinate is either 0 or 1: if 0 then the second coordinate justifies A; if 1, then B
⊤the justification is 0, as ⊤ =def {0}
⊥no justification is known, as ⊥ =def { }
∀x ∈ A . Ba construction that maps every element a ∈ A into a justification of B(a)
∃x ∈ A . Ba pair whose first coordinate is an element a ∈ A and whose second coordinate justifies B(a).

马丁-勒夫的直觉主义类型论系统地使用命题与类型的对应。该理论首次提出于 1971 年，后来经历多次修订，参见 [Martin-Löf, 1971b; Martin-Löf, 1972b; Martin-Löf, 1975; Martin-Löf, 1984]。⁶⁷ 马丁-勒夫于 1968 年 12 月初从霍华德处得知这一对应，已在 1969 年 3 月撰写、最终发表于 [Martin-Löf, 1972a] 的论文中，用它研究 [Tait, 1965] 所隐含的无穷证明论。直觉主义类型论的最初方案包含类型常量 V，使 A:V 表示 A 是类型，并有公理 V:V。这使系统十分简洁：原始概念实质上可缩减为类型化 λ 抽象、依赖积及常量 V，借助罗素与普拉维茨的编码，定义如下基本类型构造子，即逻辑联结词 [Russell, 1903, §§18,19; Prawitz, 1965, Ch.V, Thm.1]：

The correspondence between proposition and types was used systematically in the intuitionistic type theory of Martin-Löf. That theory was first proposed in 1971 but then underwent several revisions; cf. [Martin-Löf, 1971b; Martin-Löf, 1972b; Martin-Löf, 1975; Martin-Löf, 1984].67 Martin-Löf had learned about this correspondence from Howard in early December 1968, and had exploited it already in a paper, written in March 1969 and eventually published as [Martin-Löf, 1972a], on the infinitary proof theory implicit in [Tait, 1965]. The first proposal of intuitionistic type theory included a type constant V such that A : V meant that A is a type, with the axiom that V : V . This assumption allowed for a very succinct system: the primitive notions could be reduced, essentially, to typed λ-abstraction, dependent product and the constant V , using the coding of Russell and Prawitz to define the following basic type constructors (i.e., logical connectives) [Russell, 1903, §§18,19], [Prawitz, 1965, Ch. V, Thm. 1]:

⊥ ≔ ∀X:V.X；A×B ≔ ∀X:V.(A→B→X)→X；A+B ≔ ∀X:V.(A→X)→(B→X)→X；∃x:A.B(x) ≔ ∀X:V.(∀x:A.(B(x)→X))→X。

⊥ =def ∀X : V . X A × B =def ∀X : V . (A →B →X) →X A + B =def ∀X : V . (A →X) →(B →X) →X ∃x :A . B(x) =def ∀X : V . (∀x : A . (B(x) →X) →X.

采用“所有类型的类型”这一强非直谓假设，有实质性动机，依据是同时接受：

There were substantial motivations for making the strong impredicative assumption of a type of all types, based on the simultaneous acceptance of

“以下三条原则。第一，像非直谓二阶逻辑那样对命题量化。第二，罗素的类型学说：命题函数的有意义范围形成类型，因此特别地，只有对某个类型的全部对象量化才有意义。第三，认同命题与类型。”[Martin-Löf, 1971a, p.5]

“the following three principles. First, quantification over propositions, as in impredicative second order logic. Second, Russell’s doctrine of types according to which the ranges of significance of propositional functions form types so that, in particular, it is only meaningful to quantify over all objects of a certain type. Third, the identification of propositions and types” [Martin-Löf, 1971a, p.5].

然而，该理论被发现不一致，因为其中能编码一种布拉利-福尔蒂悖论。⁶⁸ 后续版本去除了其非直谓性质 [Martin-Löf, 1972b; Martin-Löf, 1975; Martin-Löf, 1982; Martin-Löf, 1984]。概括地说，结果可描述为在依赖类型框架中发展的迭代归纳定义的直觉主义理论。⁶⁹

However, the theory turned out to be inconsistent, as a form of the Burali-Forti paradox could be coded in it.68 The impredicative character of the theory was removed in its subsequent versions [Martin-Löf, 1972b; Martin-Löf, 1975; Martin-Löf, 1982; Martin-Löf, 1984]. Broadly, the outcome can be described as an intuitionistic theory of iterated inductive definitions developed in the framework of dependent types,69

但这里对类型与命题对应的利用，比前述系统更加系统。这一对应启发人们像 [Gentzen, 1935] 那样，以引入和消去规则表述类型构造操作的规则。这自动产生一种把项视为演绎的归约概念，如 [Prawitz, 1965; Prawitz, 1971]，可直接从中读出计算规则。所得系统于是成为包含自身逻辑的函数式编程语言，其公式视为程序规约，正如马丁-勒夫在 [Martin-Löf, 1982] 中指出的那样。⁷⁰

but here the correspondence between types and propositions is even more systematically exploited than in the systems discussed earlier. The correspondence suggests a formulation of the rules for type-forming operations as introduction and elimination rules, as in [Gentzen, 1935]. This yields automatically a notion of reduction for terms as deductions as in [Prawitz, 1965; Prawitz, 1971], from which computation rules can be read offdirectly. The resulting system then becomes a functional programming language including its own logic, whose formulas are regarded as program specifications, as Martin-Löf himself pointed out in [Martin-Löf, 1982].70

正是该文首次勾勒出证明论与函数式编程语言理论之间的对应，对面向逻辑的函数式编程产生广泛的概念与术语影响。特别地，在认同命题与类型后，引入规则的行为类似 [Landin, 1964] 的构造子；逻辑常量则对应通过指定典范元素，即最外层符号为构造子的元素，来形成新类型的操作，类似自 [Burstall, 1977] 起函数式语言中的具体数据类型，见 [Peyton Jones, 1987, §4.1.4]。

It was that paper that first outlined a correspondence between proof-theory and the theory of functional programming languages that had a widespread conceptual and terminological influence on logically-oriented functional programming. In particular, under the identification of propositions with types, the introduction rules behave as the constructors of [Landin, 1964], while logical constants correspond to operations that form new types by specifying what are their canonical elements (i.e., those whose outermost symbol is a constructor), like the concrete data types of functional programming languages since [Burstall, 1977] (see [Peyton Jones, 1987, §4.1.4]).

67　马丁-勒夫于 1970 年 10 月开始这项工作，源自其此前关于迭代归纳定义的直觉主义理论、种类理论、直觉主义简单类型论之正规形定理的证明论工作，以及对范畴论的兴趣。他认为范畴论未清晰区分外延相等与定义相等，因而不足以作为基础框架。68　吉拉尔证明了这一点 [Girard, 1972, III, Annexe A]；[Coquand, 1986] 研究其悖论。[Jacobs, 1989] 从另一角度研究该系统，认为吉拉尔悖论可视为良基集合宇宙上的米里马诺夫悖论 [Mirimanoff, 1917] 的一个版本。69　这一恰当描述来自 [Coquand, 1999]。70　[Martin-Löf, 1982] 描述计算规则的具体技术，与如今所谓“结构操作语义”密切相关，参见 [Plotkin, 1981; Kahn, 1988]。

67Martin-Löf’s work on this theory began in October 1970. It grew out of earlier proof-theoretical work on normal form theorems (Haupts ̈atze) for the intuitionistic theory of iterated inductive definitions, the theory of species and intuitionistic simple type theory, and also an interest in category theory, which was found inadequate as a foundational framework because of its lack of a clear distinction between extensional and definitional equality. 68This was proved by Girard [Girard, 1972, III, Annexe A], and his paradox was studied in [Coquand, 1986]; for a study of this system from a different point of view, see [Jacobs, 1989], who suggested that Girard’s paradox could be viewed as a version of the paradox of Mirimanoffon the universe of well-founded sets [Mirimanoff, 1917]. 69This appropriate description was given in [Coquand, 1999]. 70The particular technique used in describing the computation rules in [Martin-Löf, 1982] is closely related to what is now called “structured operational semantics”, cf. [Plotkin, 1981] and [Kahn, 1988].

从哲学方面看，这些思想也符合始于 [Gentzen, 1935, §II.5.13] 的意义理论：逻辑常量的意义由自然演绎系统中的引入规则决定，相应消去规则则依赖这一意义。正是这一思想，使 [Prawitz, 1965] 精确表述了 [Lorenzen, 1955] 的“反演原则”，并有助于分离出自然演绎推导的归约规则。[Hancock and Martin-Löf, 1975] 首次针对一种简单原始递归定义语言，研究马丁-勒夫直觉主义类型论常量的意义；[Martin-Löf, 1984; Martin-Löf, 1985; Martin-Löf, 1987] 使研究更加成熟。它们与直觉主义逻辑常量语义、一般构造意义理论，以及数学命题直觉主义解释的哲学基础研究高度相关。该领域截至 1985 年的综述见 [Sundholm, 1986]。

On the more philosophical side, these ideas are also in accordance with a theory of meaning that started from [Gentzen, 1935, §II.5.13], according to which the meaning of a logical constant is determined by its introduction rules in a system of natural deduction, whereas the corresponding elimination rules depend on this meaning. It was this idea that led to a precise formulation in [Prawitz, 1965] of the “inversion principle” of [Lorenzen, 1955], where it contributed to isolating the reduction rules for derivations in natural deduction. The meanings of the constants of Martin-Löf’s intuitionistic theory of types were first studied in [Hancock and Martin-Löf, 1975] for a simple language of primitive recursive definitions; these studies were brought to a more mature state with [Martin-Löf, 1984; Martin-Löf, 1985; Martin-Löf, 1987], and were very relevant to the work on the semantics of intuitionistic logical constants, and more generally to the constructive theories of meaning and the philosophical foundations of the intuitionistic interpretation of mathematical propositions (see [Sundholm, 1986] for a survey of this area until 1985).

自 20 世纪 70 年代初，罗伯特·康斯特布尔及其合作者也在康奈尔大学开展了与马丁-勒夫类型论多方面相关的工作。康斯特布尔认为，认识到各种构造性证明可编译为可执行代码，源于克林 40 年代的可实现性工作 [Constable, 1982, p.3]。例如，“对所有整数 n，存在整数 y，使 R(n,y)”的证明，可编译为满足 R(n,f(n)) 的可计算函数 f。

Work related in several ways to Martin-Löf’s theory of types had also been carried out at Cornell University by Robert Constable and his collaborators from the early 1970s. Constable attributed to Kleene’s work on realizability in the 1940s the awareness that various kinds of constructive proofs can be compiled into executable code, [Constable, 1982, p.3]. For instance a proof of an assertion of the form “for all integers n there is an integer y such that R(n, y)” can be compiled into a computable function f such that R(n, f(n)).

他在 [Constable, 1971] 中提出，某些构造形式系统可直接作为编程语言，并据此设计编程逻辑 [Constable and O’Donnell, 1978]。对系统的进一步改进 [Constable, 1980; Constable, 1985; Bates and Constable, 1985]，加上 [Martin-Löf, 1982] 和爱丁堡 LCF（§8.1.2）的思想，最终导向 Nuprl 系统 [Constable and others, 1986]，它在马丁-勒夫直觉主义类型论的一个版本中直接操作证明。

He proposed in [Constable, 1971] that certain constructive formal systems could be used directly as programming languages, and designed a programming logic based on this idea in [Constable and O’Donnell, 1978]. Further refinements of this system, [Constable, 1980; Constable, 1985; Bates and Constable, 1985], together with the inclusion of ideas from [Martin-Löf, 1982] and from Edinburgh LCF (§8.1.2), led eventually to the Nuprl system [Constable and others, 1986] that manipulated proofs directly in a version of Martin-Löf’s intuitionistic type theory.

Nuprl 小组还把“类型即命题”思想扩展到经典逻辑。特别地，蒂姆·格里芬与切坦·默西 [Griffin, 1990; Murthy, 1991] 观察到：λ 演算控制算子的续延传递风格翻译 [Felleisen et al., 1987]，对应于经典逻辑到直觉主义逻辑的双重否定翻译，例如 [Friedman, 1978] 所述。

From the researchers in the Nuprl group also emerged an extension of the “types as propositions” idea to classical logic. In particular, Tim Griffin and Chetan Murthy ([Griffin, 1990], [Murthy, 1991]) observed a correspondence between the continuation passing style translation of control operators for λ-calculus [Felleisen et al., 1987] and the double-negation translation of classical into intuitionistic logic as described, for example, in [Friedman, 1978].

8.2　早期正规化证明

8.2 Early normalization proofs

从句法观点看，类型化项系统最理想的性质包括弱正规化 WN，即每个有类型项都有正规形；更好的是强正规化 SN，即不存在无穷归约。

From a syntactic point of view, the most desirable properties of a system of typed terms include the weak normalization (WN) property that every typed term has a normal form, and, better, the strong normalization (SN) property that no infinite reductions exist.

如 §5.1 所述，图灵首先证明了 λ 或 CL 的这类性质。他的证明适用于具有纯箭头类型的 λ 项及 β 归约。该证明在他去世后从遗稿中的打字笔记发现，已知写于 1942 年之前，但直到约 40 年后才发表于 [Turing, 1980]。

The first proof of such a property for λ or CL was by Turing, as mentioned in §5.1. Turing’s proof covered λ-terms with pure arrow types and β-reduction. It was found in a typewritten note among his papers after his death, and is known to have been composed before 1942, but was not published until about 40 years later, when it appeared as [Turing, 1980].

与此同时，柯里在 50 年代用切消去作出很不同的 WN 证明；这是最早发表的证明，见 [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9F6, Cor.9F9.2]。⁷¹

In the meantime Curry made a very different WN proof in the 1950s using cutelimination, and this was the first proof to be published; it appeared in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §9F6, Cor. 9F9.2].71

图灵的方法在 60、70 年代被独立重新发现多次，例如詹姆斯·H. 莫里斯未发表博士论文 [Morris, 1968, p.107, Thm.2]，以及加雷尔·波廷格 [Pottinger, 1978, p.448]，后者加入序数。最早发表的图灵式证明见 [Andrews, 1971, p.417, Prop.2.7.3]；安德鲁斯把证明来源记为詹姆斯·加德，他与安德鲁斯一样是丘奇 60 年代的学生，而定理归为“民间传说”。

The method used by Turing was re-discovered independently several times in the 1960s and ’70s, for example by James H. Morris in his unpublished thesis [Morris, 1968, p.107 Thm. 2], and by Garrel Pottinger in [Pottinger, 1978, p.448] (with ordinals added in). The first published proof using Turing’s method was in [Andrews, 1971, p.417, Prop. 2.7.3], where the author’s source for the proof was given as James Guard, who was, like Andrews, a 1960s student of Church, and the theorem was attributed to “folklore”.

60 年代，哥德尔《辩证法》论文 [Gödel, 1958]，以及克赖泽尔在斯坦福主持的相关思想研究，成为最初刺激。粗略地说，哥德尔把一阶算术解释到无逻辑的有限类型原始递归泛函理论 T 中，并以有限主义推理，从 T 中所有数值类型闭项，即 0 型闭项，都计算为唯一数码的假设，推出算术一致性。T 可形式化为加入原始递归算子的类型化 CL 或 λ 的版本；上述可计算性性质便成为扩充系统所有 0 型闭项满足 WN 的断言。⁷² 因此，验证这类 CL、λ 扩展的 WN 定理成为重要问题；截至 1966 年，至少出现五种不同证明。泰特约在 1963 年作出两种：一种发表于 [Tait, 1965]，适用于加入某些无穷项的 λ 版本，但基于类似 CL 弱归约的弱 λ 归约；另一种见 1963 年斯坦福研讨班非正式传阅笔记第五节附录 B，直到 [Tait, 1967] 才正式发表，适用于加入原始递归与条形递归算子的 CL。它引入如今基本标准的泰特方法：精心组织对类型的归纳，定义蕴涵可正规化性的可计算性谓词，再对项归纳证明所有项满足该谓词。⁷³

For stronger type-systems, several WN proofs were made in the 1960s. The initial stimulus was Gödel’s Dialectica paper [Gödel, 1958], together with studies of the ideas behind it led by Kreisel in Stanford University. Roughly speaking, Gödel interpreted first-order arithmetic in a logic-free theory T of primitive recursive functionals of finite type, and deduced the consistency of arithmetic by finitary reasoning from an assumption that all closed terms in T of numerical type (closed type-0 terms) computed to unique numerals. One way of formalizing T was as a version of typed CL or λ augmented by primitive-recursion operators, and the above computability property of T then became the statement that WN held for all closed type-0 terms of the augmented CL or λ..72 This drew the problem of verifying the WN theorem for such extensions of CL and λ to the forefront, and at least five different WN proofs came into being by 1966. Two were made around 1963 by Tait. One of these was published in [Tait, 1965]; it covered a version of λ augmented by certain infinite terms, but based on a weak λ-reduction analogous to weak reduction in CL. The other was reported in the informally circulated notes of a seminar held at Stanford in 1963 (Section V Appendix B), but was not actually published until [Tait, 1967]; it covered CL augmented by operators for primitive recursion and bar-recursion. It introduced the method due to Tait that is now more or less standard, of defining by a carefully organised induction on types a computability predicate that implies normalizability, and proving by induction on terms that all terms satisfy this predicate.73

哥德尔 T 的其他早期正规化证明包括：路易斯·桑奇斯 1965 年对 (CL+R)t 的证明 [Sanchis, 1967, Thm.8]；尤斯图斯·迪勒 1966 年对 (λβ+R′)t 的证明 [Diller, 1968, §6]；花谷义人 1966 年 [Hanatani, 1966]；日向茂 1967 年 [Hinata, 1967]；A. G. 德拉加林 1968 年 [Dragalin, 1968]。更多由哥德尔 T 引出的正规化证明，见 [Troelstra, 1973, §§2.2.1–2.3.13，尤其 2.2.35]。

Other early normalization proofs for versions of Gödel’s T included ones in 1965 by Luis Sanchis for (CL+R)t [Sanchis, 1967, Thm. 8], in 1966 by Justus Diller for (λβ + R′)t [Diller, 1968, §6], in 1966 by Yoshito Hanatani [Hanatani, 1966], in 1967 by Shigeru Hinata [Hinata, 1967], and in 1968 by A. G. Dragalin [Dragalin, 1968]. For more on normalization proofs stemming from Gödel’s T, see [Troelstra, 1973, §§2.2.1–2.3.13, esp. 2.2.35].

SN 可视为计算系统的安全性质。但最初人们并不关注它，因为一致性证明只需 WN；若泰特有意，[Tait, 1967] 的 WN 证明几乎无需修改就能得到 SN。第一个明确的 SN 证明是上述桑奇斯 1965 年的证明。霍华德于 1968 年通过直接给项赋序数证明 SN [Howard, 1970]；它适用于加入 R 的弱 λ 归约，却不适用于完整 λβR 归约。扩展到后者，几乎等了 30 年，直到贡纳尔·维尔肯与安德烈亚斯·魏尔曼的 [Wilken and Weiermann, 1997]。60 年代之后，多数正规化研究者尽可能以 SN 为目标。

The property SN can be seen as a safety feature for computing systems. But at first there was no interest in this property, as WN was all that was needed in consistency proofs; the WN proof in [Tait, 1967] would have needed almost no change to give SN had Tait wanted it. The first explicit SN proof was Sanchis’ 1965 proof mentioned above. An SN proof by directly assigning ordinals to terms was made by Howard in 1968, [Howard, 1970]; it worked for weak λ-reduction augmented by R, but not for full-strength λβR-reduction. (An extension to that reduction had to wait for nearly 30 years, until Gunnar Wilken and Andreas Weiermann’s [Wilken and Weiermann, 1997].) After the 1960s, most workers studying normalization aimed to prove SN whenever possible.

71　柯里的 WN 定理针对具有 βη 强归约的 CL，经 λ-CL 翻译，由其基于 λβ 的系统切消去定理推出，见 §5.4。72　哥德尔 T 的一种简单形式化是 [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, 第1版, pp.297–299] 概述的 (CL+R)t：弱归约 CL，加上每个类型表达式 τ 对应的常量 Rτ，规则为 RτXY0 ▷ X，RτXY(succ n) ▷ Yn(RτXYn)。这里省略类型，0 与 succ 是原子。该系统的 WN，事实上 SN 证明，见同书附录 2，基于 [Tait, 1967]。顺便说，虽然 Rτ 足以形式化 T，[Tait, 1967] 和许多其他形式化实际上等价于稍强的算子 R′τ，规则为 R′τXY(succ Z) ▷ YZ(R′τXYZ)。73　[Tait, 2001, §5] 认为，哥德尔 1938 年未发表笔记及 1941 年未正式出版讲座已预示其可计算性思想，见 [Gödel, 1995, *1938a, *1941]。但本文只关心明确的 WN 证明，这些来源均无此证明。

71Curry’s WN theorem was for CL with βη-strong reduction. It was deduced via a λ-CL translation from his cut-elimination theorem for a system based on λβ, see §5.4 above. 72A simple formalization of Gödel’s T is the system (CL+R)t defined in outline in [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, 1st edition, pp.297–299]. It consists of CL with weak reduction plus a constant Rτ for every type-expression τ, with reduction-rules RτXY 0 ▷X, RτXY (succ n) ▷Y n(RτXY n). (Types are omitted here, and 0 and succ are atoms.) A proof of WN (indeed SN) for (CL+R)t is in [Hindley and Seldin, 1986, 1st edition, Appendix 2], based on the method in [Tait, 1967]. By the way, although Rτ was adequate to formalize T, [Tait, 1967] and many other formalizations were actually equivalent to a slightly stronger operator R′ τ with the rule R′ τXY (succ Z) ▷ Y Z(R′ τXY Z). 73In [Tait, 2001, §5] Tait expressed the opinion that his computability idea had been anticipated by Gödel in unpublished notes in 1938 and lectures which were given in 1941 but not published in print, see [Gödel, 1995, items *1938a, *1941]. But our interest here is only in explicit proofs of WN, and none occurs in these sources.

根岑式逻辑自然演绎系统的正规化证明也与此相关，因为这类系统中的演绎可视为一种类型化 λ 项。下文会提及其中一些；其他证明请参阅主流证明论史。

Normalization proofs for Gentzen-style natural deduction systems of logic are also relevant here, because deductions in such systems can be viewed as a kind of typed λ-terms. Some such proofs will be mentioned in the following sections. For others, the reader is referred to the history of mainstream proof theory.

8.3　高阶类型论

8.3 Higher-order type theories

1953 年，竹内外史提出高阶逻辑相继式演算 GLC，并猜想其具有切消去性质：若公式在 GLC 中有推导 D，则有不使用切规则的推导 D* [Takeuti, 1953]。

In 1953, Gaisi Takeuti formulated a sequent calculus GLC for higher-order logic and conjectured that the cut-elimination property would hold for it, i.e. that if a formula had a derivation D in GLC then it would have a derivation D∗in which the cut-rule was not used, [Takeuti, 1953].

13 年后，终于找到 GLC 二阶片段的两个证明 [Tait, 1966; Prawitz, 1967]；高桥元男与达格·普拉维茨又证明整个 GLC 的情形 [Takahashi, 1967; Prawitz, 1968]。但这些证明均未给出从 D 求 D* 的过程，而都依赖库尔特·许特的结果：把猜想归结为证明每个部分赋值都可扩展为全赋值 [Schütte, 1960]。

After 13 years, two proofs of Takeuti’s conjecture were eventually found for the second-order fragment of GLC [Tait, 1966; Prawitz, 1967], and Moto-o Takahashi and Dag Prawitz found proofs for the whole of GLC [Takahashi, 1967; Prawitz, 1968]. But these proofs gave no procedure for finding D∗from D; they all relied on a result of Kurt Schütte which reduced the conjecture to the problem of proving that every partial valuation could be extended to a total valuation [Schütte, 1960].

让-伊夫·吉拉尔于 1970 年给出第一个包含求 D* 过程的证明，见 [Girard, 1971; Girard, 1972]。

The first proof to contain a procedure to find D∗was made by Jean-Yves Girard in 1970, see [Girard, 1971; Girard, 1972].

吉拉尔提出两个类型化 λ 演算系统：F 对应 GLC 二阶片段，如今称为 Fω 的系统对应完整 GLC；他通过证明二者 SN 解决竹内问题。其 SN 证明的关键思想“可归约候选集”，发展自泰特可计算性方法 [Tait, 1967]，很快被其他证明论研究者采用、扩展，此后广泛使用。⁷⁴

Girard formulated two systems of typed λ-calculus, F corresponding to the second-order fragment of GLC, and the system nowadays called Fω corresponding to full GLC, and he solved Takeuti’s problem via proofs of SN for these systems. The key idea in his SN proofs, candidats de r ́eductibilit ́e, was a development of Tait’s computability method, [Tait, 1967], and was very quickly adopted and extended by other workers in proof-theory and has been used widely since.74

吉拉尔系统 F 在本文历史中尤其重要。⁷⁵ 其主要创新是可形成全称量化类型 ∀t.σ，以及把该类型的项 M 应用于类型 τ 的操作。相应地，有抽象 Λt.M，表示一个函数，其应用于类型 τ 后归约为类型 [τ/t]σ 的项 M{τ}。这种扩展隐含非直谓性：∀t.σ 中类型变量 t 遍历所有类型，包括 ∀t.σ 本身；因此该演算归约的 SN 证明远非显然。

Girard’s system F is particularly important in our story.75 Its main novelties were the possibility of forming universally quantified types ∀t.σ, and the operation of applying a term M of type ∀t.σ to a type τ. Corresponding to this operation there was an abstraction operation Λt.M to represent a function whose application to a type τ reduced to the term M{τ} of type [τ/t]σ. The impredicativity implicit in this extension, whereby the type variable t in ∀t.σ ranged over all types, including ∀t.σ itself, made the SN proof for reductions in this calculus far from obvious.

F 发明后的最初几年，其影响局限于证明论；但以类型为参数的 λ 项思想，也在当时编程语言理论中日益重要，例如 [Cheatham Jr. et al., 1968]。因此约翰·雷诺兹于 1974 年独立重新发明几乎相同的系统 [Reynolds, 1974]，并不奇怪。⁷⁶ 发展量化类型系统的一个动机，是形式化克里斯托弗·斯特雷奇关于编程语言多态性的思想，尤其是参数多态，见 [Strachey, 1967, §3.6.4]。例如 map 把函数 f 应用于列表所有元素。若 f:σ→τ，则自然给 map(f) 赋予依赖参数 σ、τ 的类型 List(σ)→List(τ)。使用全称量化类型，可给 map 赋型：

The impact of system F was limited to proof theory in the years immediately following its invention, but the idea of having λ-terms taking types as parameters was also becoming important in those years in the theory of programming languages (see, for example, [Cheatham Jr. et al., 1968]). It is not surprising, then, that almost the same system was independently reinvented in 1974 by John Reynolds, [Reynolds, 1974].76 One motivation for the development of a system of quantified types was the formalization of ideas on polymorphism in programming languages expressed by Christopher Strachey (see, e.g., [Strachey, 1967, §3.6.4]), especially the notion of parametric polymorphism. Take, for example, the function map that applies a function f to all elements of a list. If f : σ →τ, then it is natural to assign to map(f) the type List(σ) →List(τ) that depends parametrically on σ and τ. By using universally quantified types, map could be assigned the type

∀s.∀t.(s→t)→(List(s)→List(t))

74　吉拉尔方法及若干采用该方法的 SN 证明的分析，见 [Gallier, 1990]。75　F 的介绍见 [Girard et al., 1989]。76　雷诺兹常指出，他于 1974 年 4 月在吉拉尔任教的巴黎第七大学会议上报告论文，但听众无人注意到两个系统的相似性，也无人提醒他吉拉尔的工作。

74An analysis of Girard’s method, and of several SN proofs that use it, is [Gallier, 1990]. 75For an introduction to system F, see [Girard et al., 1989]. 76Reynolds has often pointed out that he presented his paper in April 1974 at a meeting at Paris VII University, where Girard was teaching, but nobody in the audience noticed the similarity of the two systems or brought Girard’s work to his attention.

一个重要观察是，多态项可表示基本数据结构。例如，自然数可表示为如下多态丘奇数码：

An important remark was that polymorphic terms can be used to represent basic data structures; for example the natural numbers can be represented by polymorphic Church numerals of the form

Λt.λf:t→t.λx:t.f(⋯(fx)⋯)nat=∀t.(t→t)→(t→t)

见 [Girard, 1972, p.I.5.5]。[Girard, 1972, pp.I.5.2–I.5.3] 及引用 [Russell, 1903] 的 [Martin-Löf, 1971b, §3] 还给出如下表示：

see [Girard, 1972, p. I.5.5]. Representations which occurred in [Girard, 1972, pp. I.5.2, I.5.3], also in [Martin-Löf, 1971b, §3] which referred to [Russell, 1903], were

σ×τ=Λt.((σ→(τ→t))→t);σ+τ=Λt.((σ→t)→((τ→t)→t))

布尔值表示为：

The boolean values had representations

Λt.λx:t.λy:t.x;Λt.λx:t.λy:t.ybool=∀t.t→(t→t)

由 s 型元素构成的列表 [e1,…,en]，在 [Reynolds, 1983, p.520] 中表示为项：

The representation of a list [e1, . . . , en] of elements of some type s appeared in [Reynolds, 1983, p.520] as the term

Λt.λf:s→(t→t).λx:t.fe1(⋯(fenx)⋯)list(s)=∀t.(s→(t→t))→(t→t)

据称其“灵感来自 C. 伯姆设计的一种列表函数式编码”。

and was said to have been “suggested by a functional encoding of lists devised by C. Böhm”.

多态类型项表示的另一个来源是 [Leivant, 1983]；它对伯姆与贝拉尔杜奇有所影响。[Böhm and Berarducci, 1985] 系统说明如何把多类项代数编码为多态 λ 演算的闭类型，并把其上的迭代定义函数编码为多态项。⁷⁷

Another source for representations by polymorphically typed terms was [Leivant, 1983]; this had some influence on Böhm and Berarducci [Böhm and Berarducci, 1985] which contained a systematic account of how to encode many-sorted term algebras as closed types of the polymorphic λ-calculus, together with the iteratively defined functions over them as polymorphic terms.77

我们已指出，雷诺兹发展多态类型化 λ 演算的主要动机，是通过描述多态项 Λt.M 对输入类型的一致依赖，形式澄清斯特雷奇的参数性概念。非正式地，这种项定义的参数函数，其行为不应依赖输入类型，即对所有输入类型都应相同 [Reynolds, 1983, p.519]。主要技术困难是比较类型不同的值。雷诺兹提出参数性的语义判据：形如 Λt.M 的项，应保持不同类型所对应集合之间某些递归定义的关系 [Reynolds, 1983]。⁷⁸ 但他的参数性概念基于多态类型的集合论解释，扩展亨金的一般模型（§8.1.2）。因此，这种关系式参数性描述与逻辑关系理论有若干相似处，尤其是 [Plotkin, 1980] 对可定义性的处理。

We have remarked that the main motivation for Reynolds’ development of the polymorphic typed λ-calculus was the formal clarification of Strachey’s notion of parametricity, by describing the uniform dependence of a polymorphic term Λt.M upon an input type. Informally, the behaviour of a parametric function defined by such a term should not depend on the input type or, in other words, should be the same for all input types [Reynolds, 1983, p.519]. The main technical difficulty was to compare values whose types were different, and Reynolds proposed a semantic criterion of parametricity, consisting in the property of terms of the form Λt.M of preserving certain hereditarily defined relations between sets corresponding to different types [Reynolds, 1983].78 Reynolds’ notion of parametricity was based, however, on a set-theoretical interpretation of polymorphic types extending the general models of Henkin (§8.1.2 above). Accordingly, the relational account of parametricity showed several similarities with the theory of logical relations, in particular with the treatment of definability in [Plotkin, 1980].

[Reynolds, 1983] 猜想多态类型化 λ 演算存在集合论模型。但一年后，雷诺兹自己推翻了猜想：他证明，集合与函数范畴上的函子 (2^X)^X 可由类型表达式定义，因而应有初始代数 A≅(2^A)^A；基数论证表明这不可能。⁷⁹

The existence of a set-theoretic model for the polymorphic typed λ-calculus was conjectured in [Reynolds, 1983]. But a year later Reynolds disproved his own conjecture by showing that the functor (over the category of sets and functions) (2X)X is definable by a type expression, hence should have an initial algebra A ∼= (2A)A, which is impossible by a cardinality argument.79

77　除原始论文外，文献中有若干这类表示的说明，例如 [Girard et al., 1989, §§11.3–11.5] 与 [Leivant, 1990, §3]。78　基于这种关系式参数性方法的形式系统，见 [Abadi et al., 1993; Plotkin and Abadi, 1993]。参数性综述另见 [Longo et al., 1993, §1; Longo, 1995]。

77There are several accounts of these representations in the literature: for example [Girard et al., 1989, §§11.3,11.4,11.5] and [Leivant, 1990, §3], besides the original papers. 78Formal systems based on this relational approach to parametricity are described in [Abadi et al., 1993] and [Plotkin and Abadi, 1993]. For a survey of parametricity see also [Longo et al., 1993, §1] and [Longo, 1995].

F 的模型论始于 [Girard, 1972, §II.5]，其中描述了基于克林应用结构 ⟨N,·⟩ 的模型 HEO2，以及高阶推广 HEOn；n·m 表示第 n 个部分递归函数应用于参数 m 的结果。每个类型 σ 被解释为一对：集合 Dσ⊆N×{σ}，及其上的等价关系 =σ。

The model theory of system F was initiated by Girard in his [Girard, 1972, §II.5], which contained the description of a model HEO2 (and its generalizations to higher-orders HEOn) based on the Kleene applicative structure ⟨N, ·⟩, where n · m denoted the result of applying the n-th partial recursive function to the argument m. Every type σ was interpreted as a pair consisting of a set Dσ ⊆N × {σ} and an equivalence relation =σ over Dσ.

函数类型 σ→τ 的解释由以下条款规定：

For functional types σ →τ this interpretation was specified by the clauses:

  • Dσ→τ 由所有这样的对 (e,σ→τ) 构成：对 Dσ 中任意 (e1,σ)、(e2,σ)，e·ei 在 i=1,2 时均有定义并落在 Dτ 中；且只要 (e1,σ)=σ(e2,σ)，就有 (e·e1,τ)=τ(e·e2,τ)。

    – Dσ→τ is the set of all pairs (e, σ →τ) such that for all (e1, σ) and (e2, σ) in Dσ, e · ei is defined for i = 1, 2 and is an element of Dτ, and (e · e1, τ) =τ (e · e2, τ) whenever (e1, σ) =σ (e2, σ);

  • (e1,σ→τ)=σ→τ(e2,σ→τ)，当且仅当对 Dσ 中所有 (x,σ)，有 (e1·x,τ)=τ(e2·x,τ)。

    – (e1, σ →τ) =σ→τ (e2, σ →τ) iff(e1 · x, τ) =τ (e2 · x, τ) for all (x, σ) in Dσ.

全称类型的解释使用原始类型 P：包含 0 的集合 A⊆N，加上 A 上的等价关系。D∀t.σ 及其等价关系 =∀t.σ 由以下条款规定：

The interpretation of for-all-types used the notion of primary type P, defined as a set A ⊆N containing 0 together with an equivalence relation on A. Then the pair D∀t.σ with the equivalence =∀t.σ was specified by the clauses:

  • D∀t.σ 由所有这样的对 (e,∀t.σ) 构成：对每个原始类型 P，均有 (e,[P/t]σ)∈D[P/t]σ；

    – D∀t.σ is the set of all pairs (e, ∀t.σ) such that (e, [P/t]σ) ∈D[P/t]σ for all primary types P;

  • (e1,∀t.σ)=∀t.σ(e2,∀t.σ)，当且仅当对所有原始类型 P，均有 (e1,[P/t]σ)=[P/t]σ(e2,[P/t]σ)。

    – (e1, ∀t.σ) =∀t.σ (e2, ∀t.σ) iff(e1, [P/t]σ) =[P/t]σ (e2, [P/t]σ) for all primary types P.

经过一些简化，见 [Troelstra, 1973, §§2.4.11,2.9.7]，这成为多态类型化 λ 演算可能最著名的模型：部分等价关系 PER 模型，即在 ⟨N,·⟩ 这样的无类型结构上，或后来在某个无类型 λ 演算模型上，取对称且传递的关系 [Scott, 1976, §7]。⁸⁰

With some simplifications, see [Troelstra, 1973, §§2.4.11, 2.9.7], this was to become probably the best-known model for the polymorphic typed λ-calculus, the model based on partial equivalence relations (PERs), i.e., symmetric and transitive relations over some type-free structure like ⟨N, ·⟩or, later, over some model of untyped λ-calculus [Scott, 1976, §7].80

雷诺兹关于多态性不存在集合论模型的证明，本质上诉诸经典逻辑。事实上，两年后人们发现，在初等拓扑斯内部逻辑提供的构造性元理论中，可把范畴论构造内部化，把该拓扑斯视为构造集合宇宙，从而挽救多态 λ 演算背后的集合论直觉。

Reynolds’ proof of the non-existence of set-theoretic models of polymorphism made an essential appeal to classical logic. In fact, two years after Reynolds’ result, it turned out that, within a constructive metatheory provided by the internal logic of an elementary topos, the set-theoretic intuitions behind the polymorphic λ-calculus could be rescued by internalizing the constructions of category theory within such a topos, looking at the latter as a constructive universe of sets.

早在 1984 年，尤金尼奥·莫吉向访问比萨的马丁·海兰德描述了一种观点：把多态 λ 演算模型视为拓扑斯 E 内部的笛卡尔闭范畴 M，并要求 M 具有以自身对象为指标的全部积。莫吉于 1986 年找到模型：取 E 为 [Hyland, 1982] 的有效拓扑斯，M 为适度集合范畴，它等价于熟悉的、应用结构 ⟨N,·⟩ 上的 PER 范畴，直接关联吉拉尔 F 的 HEO2 模型。[Hyland, 1988] 随后找到使 M 成为小完备范畴所需的恰当完备性概念。

Already in 1984, Eugenio Moggi described to Martin Hyland, then visiting Pisa, a way of looking at a model of polymorphic λ-calculus as an internal cartesian closed category M in a topos E, with M having all products indexed over the objects of M itself. Moggi found such a model in 1986 by taking E to be the effective topos of [Hyland, 1982] and M as the category of modest sets, which was equivalent to the familiar category of partial equivalence relations over the applicative structure ⟨N, ·⟩, directly related to Girard’s HEO2 model of system F. The right notion of completeness needed to make M a small complete category was then found by [Hyland, 1988].

值得注意的是，PER 族的交在内部化后成为它们的积。正是构造集合宇宙内部存在这样的小完备范畴，才使多态类型 ∀X.A[X] 可粗略解释为以 M 自身为指标的 M 中元素族之积 ΠX∈M AX。⁸¹ 特别地，M 的完备性蕴涵每个函子 T:M→M 都有初始代数 ⟨A,a:TA→A⟩；这恰是构造 F 集合论模型时的障碍。

Remarkably, the intersection of a family of partial equivalence relations becomes their product under internalization. It is the existence of such a small complete category inside a universe of constructive sets that enables one to interpret a polymorphic type ∀X.A[X], roughly, as a product Q X∈M AX of a family of elements of M indexed by M itself.81 In particular, completeness of M implies that every functor T : M →M has an initial algebra ⟨A, a : TA →A⟩, which is the stumbling block to the construction of a set-theoretic model of system F.

79　普洛特金在 1984 年 7 月 16 日给雷诺兹的信中推广其结果，导向 [Reynolds and Plotkin, 1990]。顺便说，[Reynolds, 1974] 曾提出其第一个多态系统的模型，但警告讨论中有“严重缺口”；他后来指出，警告确有道理，因为该模型并不成立。80　[Bruce et al., 1990] 给出把 PER 用作类型解释的历史说明：[Myhill and Shepherdson, 1955] 为一阶函数类型引入它们，[Kreisel, 1959] 再扩展到简单类型。

79Reynolds’ result was generalized by Plotkin in a letter to Reynolds dated July 16, 1984. His remarks led to [Reynolds and Plotkin, 1990]. (By the way, [Reynolds, 1974] contained a proposal for a model of Reynolds’ first polymorphic system, but with a warning that its discussion contained a “serious lacuna”. As he has pointed out since, this warning turned out to be justified, in that the proposed model did not work.) 80Some historical remarks on the notion of PER as an interpretation of types are given in [Bruce et al., 1990], where we learn that they were introduced in [Myhill and Shepherdson, 1955] for types of first-order functions, and then extended to simple types by [Kreisel, 1959].

从本文观点看，值得注意，这些发展本质上利用范畴论语言，尤其是指标范畴概念 [Johnstone et al., 1978]；它已成为依赖类型理论语义描述的基本工具，可视为劳威尔 70 年代超学说与拓扑斯工作的自然发展。

From the present point of view, it is of some interest to observe that these developments exploit in an essential way the language of category theory, especially the notion of indexed category [Johnstone et al., 1978], which has become a fundamental tool in the semantical description of theories of dependent types and may be regarded as a natural outgrowth of Lawvere’s work on hyperdoctrines and topoi in the 1970s.

另一个把多态性与依赖类型结合的系统，是蒂埃里·科康与热拉尔·于埃自 1984 年起发展的构造演算。它在始于科康第三阶段博士论文的一系列论文中描述：[Coquand, 1985; Coquand and Huet, 1985; Coquand and Huet, 1988; Coquand, 1990]。它被简洁地描述为“一种自然表达直觉主义高阶逻辑之海廷语义的类型论版本”[Coquand, 1990, p.91]。为避免吉拉尔在马丁-勒夫非直谓类型论中发现的悖论，系统限制类型与命题的认同，只假定每个命题关联其证明类型。但保留了上文第 34 页所引马丁-勒夫引入所有类型之类型的另外两点：对命题量化，以及类型是命题函数有意义范围的学说。因此，系统与 [Martin-Löf, 1971b] 很相似，也类似某些 Automath 系统，再扩展以允许对 type 的元素量化；[Bruijn, 1970, §12.6] 已设想这种可能。[Coquand, 1985, §4, Cor.1] 给出弱正规化证明，并提示如何扩展吉拉尔可归约候选集概念以证明 SN。[Seldin, 1987, §4.3; Seldin, 1997, §3] 给出推导和项的 SN 证明。

A polymorphic type ∀t.σ is, implicitly, a dependent type (Πt:type). σ, provided suitable rules about the constant type are added to systems with dependent types like those of §8.1.4 or §5.4. As mentioned in §5.4, a general system of dependent types was developed and studied from 1972 to 1975 by Seldin, [Seldin, 1979]. But a stronger system offering an integration of polymorphism with dependent types was the calculus of constructions, developed by Thierry Coquand and G ́erard Huet from 1984 onwards. It was described in a series of papers starting with Coquand’s Th`ese de Troisi`eme Cycle: [Coquand, 1985], [Coquand and Huet, 1985], [Coquand and Huet, 1988], [Coquand, 1990]. The calculus of constructions has been concisely described as “a version of type theory that expresses naturally Heyting semantics of intuitionistic higher-order logic” [Coquand, 1990, p.91]. Paradoxes like that described by Girard for Martin-Löf’s impredicative version of intuitionistic type theory were avoided by adopting a restricted form of the identification between types and propositions, which only assumed that to each proposition is associated the type of its proofs. But the other two points in Martin-Löf’s argument for introducing a type of all types quoted on page 34 above, namely quantification over propositions and the doctrine of types as ranges of significance of propositional functions, were preserved. The resulting system was therefore quite similar to that described in [Martin-Löf, 1971b], and also to some of the systems in the Automath family, extended by quantification over elements of type, a possibility already envisaged in [Bruijn, 1970, §12.6]. For the calculus of constructions a weak normalization proof was given in [Coquand, 1985, §4, Cor. 1], together with some hints on how Girard’s notion of candidat de r ́eductibilit ́e might be extended to give an SN proof for this system. A proof of SN for deductions and for terms was included in [Seldin, 1987, §4.3] and [Seldin, 1997, §3].

8.4　交类型与递归类型

8.4 Intersection types and recursive types

在类型表示集合的系统中，简单类型论语言的一种自然扩展，是加入 σ∩τ 这样的类型，表示 σ、τ 所指集合的交。⁸² 这一扩展确实在 70 年代末实现。但其历史并不像这个思想的自然程度可能使人预期的那样简单。

In systems where types denote sets, a natural extension of the language of simple type theory would be to adjoin types of the form σ ∩τ to denote the intersection of whatever sets were denoted by σ and τ.82 This extension was actually made in the late 1970s. Its history was, however, less simple than might be expected given the naturalness of the idea.

交类型系统通常是“柯里式”的：以规则给无类型项赋型，一个项可有无限多个类型；而非每个项具有唯一内置类型的“丘奇式”。它们使用类型，也不像罗素和丘奇那样把它作为避免悖论的安全装置，而只是作为描述项形式计算行为的标签。

Systems of intersection types are usually “Curry-style” systems, in which types are assigned to untyped terms by rules and an infinite number of types may be assigned to one term, rather than “Church-style” systems, in which each term has a unique built-in type. They also use types, not as safety-devices to avoid paradoxes, as Russell and Church did, but simply as labels to describe the formal computational behaviour of terms.

81　莫吉于 1986 年 2 月 10 日在当时由阿尔伯特·迈耶主持的 types 电子论坛著名消息中宣布该结果。其结果与背景的详述见 [Longo and Moggi, 1992; Asperti and Longo, 1991]。82　本小节与 §8.5 中，“类型”是“类型表达式”的简称。

81This result was announced in a famous message by Moggi to the types electronic forum, at that time moderated by Albert Meyer, dated February 10, 1986. The result and its background are discussed in much more detail in [Longo and Moggi, 1992] and [Asperti and Longo, 1991]. 82In the present subsection and §8.5, “type” is short for “type-expression”.

按伯姆关于把 λ 演算用作编程语言的思想（§6.3），数据和程序由 λ 项表示，视为随 β 归约演化的计算状态，正规形对应终态。由此产生的程序等价概念认为：若对每个参数 F，PF 与 P′F 或者都有正规形且 PF=βP′F，或者都无正规形，则程序，即 λ 项 P、P′ 等价。⁸³ 为使这种等价与可转换性吻合，P、P′、F 具有正规形被发现至关重要，见 [Böhm and Dezani, 1975]。这促使人们按正规形应用于其他正规形时的可正规化性，对正规形分类。

According to ideas of Böhm on the use of λ-calculus as a programming language (cf. §6.3), data and programs are represented by λ-terms thought of as states of a computation that evolves by applications of the rule of β-reduction, with normal forms corresponding to final states. The notion of program equivalence that arises from this approach regards two programs (i.e. λ-terms) P and P ′ as equivalent if, for every argument F, either PF and P ′F both have normal form and PF =β P ′F, or both fail to have a normal form.83 In order to match this notion of equivalence with convertibility, it turned out to be essential that P, P ′ and F have normal forms, see [Böhm and Dezani, 1975]. This remark prompted a search for a classification of normal forms according to their normalizability when applied to other normal forms.

自 1975 年起，伯姆及其都灵的前学生玛丽安焦拉·德扎尼与马里奥·科波定义并分析了合适分类，见 [Böhm and Dezani, 1975; Dezani, 1975; Böhm et al., 1977]。[Dezani, 1975] 引入类型主题：给每个正规形赋有限序列 (m,j,h1,…,hn) 或 (ω,h1,…,hn)，称为其“类型”，编码其计算行为。类型又出现于写于 1977 年的 [Coppo and Dezani, 1978]：借助涉及类型间两个形式定义关系，即等价与预序的新规则，给所有正规形赋简单类型，包括先前简单类型论中不可赋型的正规形。原子类型 0 表示全部正规形集合，1 表示应用于任意有限正规形序列后总归约为正规形的项集。

Starting in 1975, a suitable classification was defined and analysed by Böhm and his former students in Turin, Mariangiola Dezani and Mario Coppo; see [Böhm and Dezani, 1975], [Dezani, 1975], and [Böhm et al., 1977]. The theme of types was introduced in [Dezani, 1975]; each normal form was given a finite sequence (m, j, h1, . . . , hn) or (ω, h1, . . . , hn), called its “type”, which encoded a description of its computational behaviour. Types appeared again in [Coppo and Dezani, 1978], written in 1977, in which simple types were assigned to all normal forms, including those untypable in previous simple type theories, by new rules involving two formally defined relations between types, an equivalence and a pre-order. The atomic types were 0, to represent the set of all normal forms, and 1 for the set of all terms whose application to any finite sequence of normal forms always reduces to a normal form.

1977 年，科波与德扎尼提出给项赋有限类型序列，以说明它表示有限交中的成员。这发表于 [Coppo and Dezani, 1978]；其中结果包括：λI 演算中，一个项所得类型在转换下不变。

In 1977 Coppo and Dezani put forward the idea of assigning a finite sequence of types to a term to say that this term denoted a member of a finite intersection. This was published in [Coppo and Dezani, 1978], and the results in that paper included the theorem that the types received by a term were invariant under conversion in the λI-calculus.

图卢兹的帕特里克·萨莱扩展了序列思想 [Sallé, 1978, §5]。他加入表示全集的新原子类型常量 ω，从而把转换不变性从 λI 扩展到完整 λK 演算，并描述非 ω 类型与头正规形的联系，见 [Sallé, 1978, Thms.9–12] 及合著 [Coppo et al., 1979, Thms.1,2]。含序列和 ω 的系统在 [Coppo et al., 1980; Coppo et al., 1981] 中继续研究。前一篇 §5 中，贝蒂·文内里证明，把闭项解释为其全部类型的集合，可得到新的无类型 λ 演算模型。

The sequence idea was extended by Patrick Sall ́e in Toulouse, [Sall ́e, 1978, §5]. He adjoined a new atomic type-constant ω to denote the universal set, and by this means he extended the conversion-invariance theorem from λI to the full λKcalculus, and described the connection between non-ω types and head-normal forms, see [Sall ́e, 1978, Thms. 9–12] and the joint paper [Coppo et al., 1979, Thms. 1, 2]. The system with sequences and ω was further studied in [Coppo et al., 1980] and [Coppo et al., 1981]. In §5 of the former paper, Betti Venneri showed that, by interpreting a closed term as the set of all its types, one could obtain a new model of untyped λ-calculus.

波廷格也独立发明交类型思想，并在 [Pottinger, 1980] 中证明若干基本性质。

The intersection idea was also invented independently by Pottinger, who proved some of its basic properties in [Pottinger, 1980].

虽然交类型系统完全满足原始目的，却并非语义完备。1980 年，科波、德扎尼、巴伦德雷赫特共同 [Barendregt et al., 1983]，以及欣德利独立 [Hindley, 1982]，首次提出使其完备的额外推导规则。[Barendregt et al., 1983] 的系统很快成为标准：类型由变量和 ω 经构造子 →、∩ 建立，并有定义类似子集关系的类型关系 ≤ 的规则。如今常称“生成引理”的结果 [Barendregt et al., 1983, Lemma 2.8] 证明主要规则可逆，由此推出推导正规化定理 [同文, Cor.4.9]。该文 §3 定义了有趣的滤子模型类中的第一个 λ 模型，其元素是全体类型集中的滤子，即对 ≤ 向上闭合、对 ∩ 闭合的集合。它们与 §9.1 将讨论的斯科特原始构造密切相关。

But although the intersection system was perfectly adequate for its original purpose, it was not semantically complete. Suitable extra deduction-rules to make it complete were first formulated in 1980, by Coppo, Dezani and Barendregt together, [Barendregt et al., 1983], and by Hindley independently, [Hindley, 1982]. The system given in [Barendregt et al., 1983] soon became standard: its types were built from variables and ω by means of constructors →and ∩, and it contained rules defining a relation ≤between types analogous to the subset relation. In what is nowadays often called the “generation lemma” [Barendregt et al., 1983, Lemma 2.8] the invertibility of its main rules was proved, and from this a normalization theorem for deductions was deduced, [Barendregt et al., 1983, Cor. 4.9]. In [Barendregt et al., 1983, §3] the first of the interesting class of λ-models called filter models was defined, whose elements were filters (i. e., ≤-upward closed and ∩-closed sets) in the set of all types. These models turned out to be closely related to the original Scott construction to be discussed below in §9.1.

83　这接近 §7.1 讨论的 [Morris, 1968] 的上下文等价概念。

83This is close to the notion of contextual equivalence in [Morris, 1968] discussed in §7.1.

此后十年，人们深入研究 [Barendregt et al., 1983] 的系统，得到许多有趣结果，例如西莫内塔·龙基·德拉罗卡与文内里对主类型的分析 [Ronchi della Rocca and Venneri, 1984; Ronchi della Rocca, 1988]。交类型应用包括：在 Algol 类语言中控制干扰的类型系统 [Reynolds, 1989]，以及函数式程序静态分析 [Coppo and Ferrari, 1993]。

Over the next 10 years the system of [Barendregt et al., 1983] was studied intensively, with many interesting results emerging, for example an analysis of its principal types by Simonetta Ronchi della Rocca and Venneri in [Ronchi della Rocca and Venneri, 1984] and [Ronchi della Rocca, 1988]. Applications of intersection types included their use in type systems for the control of interference in Algol-like languages, [Reynolds, 1989] and in static analysis of functional programs, [Coppo and Ferrari, 1993].

用类型描述项行为性质，促成 80 年代若干其他类型系统，用于静态检测 λ 项与函数式程序的性质。其中递归类型，例如 [MacQueen et al., 1984; Coppo, 1985; Cardone and Coppo, 1991]，自然扩展柯里类型推断系统，加入 μt.σ 表示方程 t=σ 的解，类型变量 t 可在 σ 中出现。循环类型表达式的可能性已在 [Morris, 1968] 考虑，见 §7.1。显然，递归赋型 λ 项不具有正规化性质。例如 ΔΔ，其中 Δ≡λx.xx，只要给 x 类型 μt.t→σ，它等价于 (μt.t→σ)→σ，就可赋任意类型 σ。但基本递归类型推断系统有主赋型算法，相当直接地扩展柯里系统算法（§8.5）；因此，λ 演算加入算术原语等特殊常量时，它是防止错误应用的实用工具 [Wand, 1987; Cardone and Coppo, 1991]。

The use of types for describing behavioural properties of terms motivated several other type systems in the 1980s, for statically detecting properties of λ-terms and (functional) programs. Among these, the recursive types (see, e.g., [MacQueen et al., 1984], [Coppo, 1985] and [Cardone and Coppo, 1991]) were a natural extension of Curry’s type inference system by adjoining types of the form μt.σ to represent the solution of the equation t = σ, where the type variable t may occur in σ. (The possibility of having circular type expressions was already considered in [Morris, 1968], see above §7.1.) Clearly, recursively typed λ-terms lack the normalization property, e.g. ∆∆, where ∆≡λx.xx, can be assigned every type σ, provided x is given type μt.t →σ (which is equivalent to (μt.t →σ) →σ). But there is a principal typing algorithm for the basic type inference system for recursive types (extending fairly directly the one for Curry’s system, §8.5), and this makes it a rather practical tool for preventing incorrect applications when special constants like e.g. arithmetical primitives are added to the λ-calculus [Wand, 1987; Cardone and Coppo, 1991].

递归类型语义系统利用 60 年代末发明的、求解涉及集合之递归方程的技术。典型例子是纯外延 λ 演算模型满足的 D=D→D，其解将在 §9.1 详述。

The semantics of recursive types systematically exploited the techniques invented towards the end of the 1960s for solving recursive equations involving sets. Typical among these is the solution of the equation D = D →D satisfied by a model of pure, extensional λ-calculus that will be discussed at some length in §9.1.

把递归类型加入 F 等高阶类型系统，或含子类型关系的系统，也很有趣。卢卡·卡尔代利与彼得·韦格纳首先在 [Cardelli and Wegner, 1985] 提出这种系统，以分析面向对象编程语言的某些特征，开创全新研究领域，见 [Gunter and Mitchell, 1994; Pierce, 2002]。

Recursive types are also interesting when added to higher order type systems like system F, or to systems that include a subtyping relation: such a system was first proposed by Luca Cardelli and Peter Wegner in [Cardelli and Wegner, 1985] as a tool for analyzing certain features of object-oriented programming languages, originating a whole new field of research; see, for example, [Gunter and Mitchell, 1994] and [Pierce, 2002].

8.5　简单类型算法

8.5 Algorithms for simple types

对给定项与类型系统，可赋型性测试判定无类型项 M 能否在系统中赋型；若还能给任意可赋型项赋主类型，即按系统规则可得的最一般类型，就称为主类型算法。它对语言中无无类型项的丘奇式系统没有意义，只适用于以规则给无类型项赋型的柯里式系统，参见 §4.2。

For a given system of terms and types, a typability test decides whether an untyped term M is typable in the system; it is called a principal type algorithm if it furthermore assigns to any typable term its principal type, i.e. the most general type it can receive under the system’s rules. Such algorithms have no meaning for a Church-style type-system, which has no untyped terms in its language, but only for Curry’s style, in which types are assigned to untyped terms by rules, cf. §4.2.

§4.2 提到，奎因的分层公式概念基于柯里式方法 [Quine, 1937, pp.78–79]。1942 年，M. H. 纽曼发明分层测试，并证明分层等价于可赋型性 [Newman, 1943, §§1–4]。他还把算法扩展为 λ 演算的可赋型性测试，并证明正确性。可惜其叙述颇抽象，据我们所知，直到准备本文时，后来研究可赋型性测试的人都未读过该文。⁸⁴

In §4.2 we mentioned that Quine’s concept of stratified formula was based on a Curry-style approach, [Quine, 1937, pp. 78–79]. In 1942 a test for stratification was invented by M. H. Newman and stratification was proved equivalent to typability, [Newman, 1943, §§1–4]. But Newman also extended his algorithm to give a typability test for λ-calculus, with a proof of its correctness. Unfortunately his exposition was rather abstract, and his paper was not read by any later worker on typability tests until the preparation of the present history, as far as we know.84

[Curry and Feys, 1958, §§9B2–4] 非正式描述了更直接且能给出主类型的测试。粗略地说，对给定项 M，列出 M 的全部子项出现，以类型变量表示其类型，写出这些变量之间的方程，再尽可能一般地求解。该书详细演算若干例子，却未把方法明确写成形式算法。柯里后来在私人笔记 [Curry, 1966] 和论文 [Curry, 1969a] 中给出形式算法及正确性证明。

A more direct test, which moreover gave principal types, was described informally in [Curry and Feys, 1958, §§9B2–4]. Roughly speaking, for a given term M its method is to list all M’s subterm-occurrences with their types given as typevariables, write out equations connecting these variables, and then solve these equations as generally as possible. Several examples were worked out in detail in [Curry and Feys, 1958], but the method was not stated explicitly as a formal algorithm. Curry later wrote it out as a formal algorithm, with a proof of its correctness, in private notes [Curry, 1966] and a paper [Curry, 1969a].

84　[Newman, 1943] 概述见欣德利扩展评论《M. H. 纽曼的 λ 演算可赋型性算法》，《逻辑与计算杂志》，拟于 2006/7 年出版。纽曼的测试不是主类型算法，其论文没有主类型概念。

84For an outline of [Newman, 1943], see Hindley’s extended review M. H. Newman’s typability algorithm for lambda calculus, Journal of Logic and Computation, to appear, 2006/7. Newman’s test was not a principal type algorithm; indeed his paper contained no concept of principal type.)

欣德利于 1967 年为 CL 构造并证明了另一主类型算法，借助 [Robinson, 1965] 的合一算法节省工作，见 [Hindley, 1969b, §3]。柯里与欣德利在撰写各自 1969 年论文时通信，但有意保持方法不同。

A different principal type algorithm, which used the unification algorithm of [Robinson, 1965] to save itself some work, was made and proved correct by Hindley for CL in 1967, [Hindley, 1969b, §3]. (Curry and Hindley communicated during the writing of [Curry, 1969a] and [Hindley, 1969b] but deliberately kept their approaches different.)

詹姆斯·H. 莫里斯在博士论文 [Morris, 1968, Ch.4, §E] 中独立于柯里、欣德利 1966—1969 年工作，为 λ 构造柯里式方程求解算法并证明正确性。

A Curry-style equation-solving algorithm for λ was made, with a correctness proof, by James H. Morris in his thesis [Morris, 1968, Ch. 4, §E], independently of Curry’s and Hindley’s 1966–69 work.

70、80 年代，罗宾·米尔纳领导的爱丁堡小组发展 ML。它包含 λ 加 let 算子的赋型系统；米尔纳在 [Milner, 1978, §4.1, Algorithm W] 描述其主类型算法。该算法像 [Hindley, 1969b] 一样基于合一，但独立发明。[Damas and Milner, 1982] 重写并扩展它；路易斯·达马斯在博士论文 [Damas, 1984] 证明正确性。

Then the 1970s and ’80s saw the development of the programming language ML by the group led by Robin Milner in Edinburgh. ML included a type-assignment system for λ plus the extra operator let, and Milner described a principal type algorithm for it in [Milner, 1978, §4.1, Algorithm W]. This algorithm was unificationbased like that in [Hindley, 1969b], though it was invented independently. It was rewritten and extended in [Damas and Milner, 1982], and its correctness was proved by Luis Damas in his thesis [Damas, 1984].

1980 年后，出现各种更复杂系统的可赋型性与主类型算法，有的依赖已有合一算法，有的自足，综述见 [Tiuryn, 1990]。长期开放的问题是吉拉尔 F（§8.3）是否存在可赋型性测试；丹尼尔·莱万特在 [Leivant, 1983] 提出，乔·韦尔斯于 1994 年否定解决，事实上他证明该系统连类型检查也不可判定 [Wells, 1994; Wells, 1999]。

After 1980, typability and principal-type algorithms were made for various more complex systems, some depending on pre-existing unification algorithms and some being self-sufficient: for a survey see [Tiuryn, 1990]. A long-standing open problem was the existence of a typability test for Girard’s system F (see §8.3 above); this was raised by Daniel Leivant in [Leivant, 1983], and was solved in the negative by Joe Wells in 1994; indeed Wells proved that not even type-checking for this system was decidable, [Wells, 1994; Wells, 1999].

奇怪的是，主类型算法历史并不限于类型论。在命题即类型对应下，原子为变量的简单类型成为命题公式，主类型为 τ 的组合子 M，成为通过称为凝聚分离的命题规则得到的 τ 的证明。

But the history of principal type algorithms is not confined to type theory, strange to say. Under the propositions-as-types correspondence, simple types whose atoms are variables become propositional formulas, and a combinator M with principal type τ becomes a proof of τ by the propositional rule called condensed detachment.

粗略地说，凝聚分离规则对应从 P、Q 的主类型构造 PQ 的主类型。都柏林逻辑学家卡鲁·梅雷迪思在 1954 年之前发明，首次印行使用见 [Lemmon et al., 1957, §9]。他从未发表形式表述，但其堂弟戴维·梅雷迪思于 1954 年向他学到规则，写成程序，并于 1957 年在美国 UNIVAC1 计算机运行。⁸⁵

Roughly speaking, the condensed detachment rule corresponds to the construction of the principal type of a term PQ from those of P and Q. It was invented by the Dublin logician Carew Meredith at some time before 1954, and was first used in print in [Lemmon et al., 1957, §9]. Meredith never published a formal statement of it, but his cousin David Meredith was taught the rule by him in 1954, wrote it up as a program, and ran it on the computer UNIVAC1 in the U.S.A. in 1957.85

因此，截至 1957 年，主类型算法的关键步骤不仅已形式表述，而且已在机器上实现。

Thus, by 1957 the key step in a principal-type algorithm had not only been stated formally, but had been implemented on a machine.

再往前看，[Łukasiewicz, 1939, 英文版, p.276] 使用了非常类似凝聚分离特例分析的论证。卢卡西维茨归功于塔尔斯基；[Łukasiewicz and Tarski, 1930, 英文版, p.44] 提到的方法，也许就是所指方法。但那里并未实际描述，“也许”是猜测而非历史。更多评论见 [Kalman, 1983, §4]。

Looking further back: an argument very like an analysis of a particular case of condensed detachment was used in [ Lukasiewicz, 1939, Engl. edn., p.276]. Lukasiewicz attributed his method to Tarski, and in [ Lukasiewicz and Tarski, 1930, Engl. edn., p.44] a method was mentioned which might perhaps be the one to which he referred. But the method was not actually described there, and “might perhaps” is conjecture not history. (See [Kalman, 1983, §4] for further comments.)

纯 λ 与 CL 简单类型论的其他算法，包括舒克里-贝·本-耶莱斯于 1979 年构造的计数算法，用于计数、列举给定类型的正规居民，见 [Ben-Yelles, 1979, Ch.3] 或 [Hindley, 1997, Ch.8]。⁸⁶ 萨比娜·布罗达与路易斯·达马斯后来修改其方法，得到只计数、列举主正规居民的算法 [Broda and Damas, 1999]。

Other algorithms for the simple type theory of pure λ and CL include the counting algorithm made by Choukri-Bey Ben-Yelles in 1979 for counting and listing the normal inhabitants of a given type, see [Ben-Yelles, 1979, Ch. 3] or [Hindley, 1997, Ch. 8].86 Ben-Yelles’ method was later modified by Sabine Broda and Luis Damas to give an algorithm which counts and lists just the principal normal inhabitants, [Broda and Damas, 1999].

85　凝聚分离规则的形式表述见 [Kalman, 1983, §2] 或 [Hindley, 1997, §7D]；更多历史见这些文献及 [Meredith, 1977]。该规则类似 J. A. 罗宾逊著名的归结规则，但早于后者。

85For a formal statement of the condensed detachment rule, see [Kalman, 1983, §2] or [Hindley, 1997, §7D]. For further historical information, see those references and [Meredith, 1977]. The rule has similarities with the well known resolution rule of J. A. Robinson, and pre-dates it.

还发表了若干从某类型任意居民构造其主居民的算法。第一个见 [Hindley, 1969b, §6]，适用于完整 λK。其他算法也适用于 λI 等受限项集，由罗伯特·迈耶于 1986 年、格里戈里·明茨与塔内尔·塔梅特于 1988 年、诺曼·梅吉尔与马丁·邦德于 1994 年构造，见 [Meyer and Bunder, 1988; Meyer et al., 1991, §4; Mints and Tammet, 1991; Megill and Bunder, 1996]。

Several algorithms for constructing a principal inhabitant of a type from any other inhabitant have also been published. The first was in [Hindley, 1969b, §6], for full λK. Others, which worked also for restricted sets of terms such as λI, were made by Robert Meyer in 1986, Grigori Mints and Tanel Tammet in 1988, and Norman Megill and Martin Bunder in 1994; see [Meyer and Bunder, 1988], [Meyer et al., 1991, §4], [Mints and Tammet, 1991], [Megill and Bunder, 1996].

如戴维·梅雷迪思在 [Meredith, 1980] 指出，这类算法证明了：凝聚分离规则对相应受限 λ 演算所对应的弱蕴涵逻辑是完备的。

As David Meredith noted in [Meredith, 1980], such an algorithm provides a proof that the condensed detachment rule is complete for the weak implicational logic corresponding to the restricted λ-calculus in question.

9　λ 的模型

9 Models for λ

9.1　斯科特的 D∞ 模型

9.1 Scott’s D∞model

第一个无类型 λ 的集合论模型，由达纳·斯科特在普林斯顿哲学系休假、赴牛津与斯特雷奇合作期间构造。⁸⁷ [Scott, 1969c, p.51] 给出思想产生的确切日期：1969 年 11 月 14 日。仅一个月前，他还在为斯特雷奇撰写、私下传阅的手稿 [Scott, 1969e] 中，强烈主张以有类型方案取代 [Böhm, 1966; Böhm and Gross, 1966; Landin, 1966b] 定义的无类型编程语言，见 §8.1.2。该模型出人意料，表明尽管他先前批评无类型语言，它们仍可获得主流数学意义。而其所用手段，恰是先前有类型替代方案所依据概念的自然发展。

The first set-theoretical model for untyped λ was made by Dana Scott while he was in Oxford to work with Strachey during a leave from the Princeton Philosophy Department.87 In [Scott, 1969c, p.51] he gives a precise date for the idea: 14 November 1969. Only one month before, he had strongly advocated a typed alternative to the (untyped) programming languages defined in [Böhm, 1966; Böhm and Gross, 1966] and [Landin, 1966b], in a privately circulated manuscript written for Strachey ([Scott, 1969e], mentioned in §8.1.2 above). His model was unexpected, and showed that despite his previous criticisms of untyped languages they could be given a mainstream mathematical sense. Further, the means he used for this turned out to be a natural outgrowth of the notions on which his earlier typed alternative had been based.

斯科特模型构造的最初版本以完备格为域 [Scott, 1969b]。后来在 [Scott, 1970c] 中，他引入连续格，其代数特征是：完备格 D 中，对每个 y∈D，

In the first versions of Scott’s model construction, domains were complete lattices, [Scott, 1969b]. In a later account of the construction in [Scott, 1970c], he introduced continuous lattices, characterized algebraically as those complete lattices D where, for every y ∈D,

y=⋁{⋀U|y∈U, U is Scott-open ∧U⊆D}

其拓扑特征则是：T₀ 空间中，从子空间 X⊆Y 到 D 的每个连续函数 f:X→D，都可扩展为连续函数 f̄:Y→D。⁸⁸ 正是这种扩张性质，使连续格特别适合“一致的部分函数外延理论”[Scott, 1973, p.178]；更确切地说，适合“一种直接关联可计算性的函数外延理论，其中函数本身，而非其句法表示或程序，可作为对象处理”（斯科特，见 [Stoy, 1977, p.xxviii]）。⁸⁹

or topologically as those T0-spaces such that every continuous f : X →D from a subspace X ⊆Y can be extended to a continuous f : Y →D.88 It was this extension property that made continuous lattices especially attractive for “a coherent theory of partial functions (in extension)” [Scott, 1973, p.178], and more precisely for “an extensional theory of functions, related directly to computability, where functions (rather than syntactic representations or programs for the functions) can be treated as objects in themselves” (Scott, in [Stoy, 1977, p.xxviii]).89

86　类型 τ 的居民，是任意具有 τ 类型的闭项 M。若不含 β 可约式，则是正规的；若 τ 是其主类型，则是主居民。87　合作历史及模型发明背景，见斯科特为 [Stoy, 1977] 所写前言、图灵奖演讲 [Scott, 1977]、[Scott, 2000]，以及 [Scott, 1969e] 的 1993 年出版版评论。88　细节见 [Scott, 1972]。这里 ⋁、⋀ 为通常格运算。域 D 的斯科特开子集 U 向上闭合，且对有向集 Δ⊆D，若 ⋁Δ∈U，则 U∩Δ≠∅。f:D→D′ 斯科特连续，当且仅当它单调且保持 D 有向子集的最小上界。[Nerode, 1957; Nerode, 1959]，甚至更早的 [Uspenskii, 1955]，已考虑这种拓扑的实例。代数与拓扑概念的互动，对域理论后续发展富有成效，见 §10。89　连续格在分析、泛函分析、拓扑代数中被重新发现并应用，使其理论进一步发展，见 [Gierz et al., 1980]。与区间分析的联系见 [Scott, 1970b, p.87]。

86An inhabitant of a type τ is any closed term M with type τ. It is normal iffit contains no β-redexes. It is principal iffτ is its principal type. 87The history of this collaboration, and the circumstances in which the model was invented, are given in Scott’s foreword to [Stoy, 1977], in his Turing Award lecture [Scott, 1977], in [Scott, 2000], and in his comments in the 1993 published version of [Scott, 1969e]. 88See [Scott, 1972] for details. Here W and V are the usual lattice operators. A Scott-open subset U of a domain D is an upward closed set such that if W ∆∈U for a directed ∆⊆D then U ∩∆̸= ∅. A function f : D →D′ is (Scott-) continuous iffit is monotonic and preserves the least upper bounds of directed subsets of D. An instance of this topology had already been considered in [Nerode, 1957; Nerode, 1959], and even before, in [Uspenskii, 1955]. The interplay of algebraic and topological notions has been a fruitful one in recent developments of domain theory, see §10 later. 89The theory of continuous lattices gained further impetus from their rediscovery and application in analysis, functional analysis and topological algebra, as documented in [Gierz et al., 1980]. Their connection with interval analysis was pointed out in [Scott, 1970b, p.87].

整体看，域与斯科特连续函数形成笛卡尔闭范畴，指数对象 [D→E] 是 D 到 E 的斯科特连续函数完备格，因此适合类型化 λ 演算语义（§8.1.3）。但其中还包含反身域 D∞，非平凡地在同构意义下解方程 D∞=[D∞→D∞]，从而得到具有 βη 转换的无类型 λK 演算模型。

From a global point of view, domains and Scott-continuous functions formed cartesian closed categories where the exponential objects [D →E] were the complete lattices of Scott-continuous functions from D to E: this made them suitable for the semantics of typed λ-calculus (see §8.1.3). But they also contained reflexive domains D∞, that solved the equation D∞= [D∞→D∞] non-trivially (up to isomorphism), and these yielded models of the untyped λ-K-calculus with βη-conversion.

引导斯科特构造反身域的洞见，实质上是把连续函数 f:D→D 的最小不动点迭代构造，推广到域上的运算，尤其 F(D)=[D→D]：

The insight that led Scott to the construction of reflexive domains consisted basically in extending to operations on domains, in particular F(D) = [D →D], the iterative construction of the least fixed-point of a continuous function f : D →D as fix(f) =def _

fix(f)≔⋁n∈ωfⁿ(⊥)(1)

斯科特后来承认，扩展所需技术是构造中最原创的步骤 [Scott, 1973, p.181]：给定域 D、E，从 D 到 E 的嵌入-投影对，是连续函数 i:D→E、j:E→D，满足 j∘i=idD、i∘j⊑idE，其中函数序逐点定义。嵌入-投影对在域之间定义逼近关系，类似偏序描述的域元素间逼近关系。反身域由如下投影序列的逆极限构造：

The technical device needed for this extension was later recognized by Scott himself [Scott, 1973, p.181] to be the most original step of his construction: given domains D and E, an embedding-projection pair from D to E is a pair of (Scott-) continuous functions i : D →E, j : E →D such that j ◦i = idD and i ◦j ⊑idE, where the order relation on functions is defined pointwise. Embedding-projection pairs defined an approximation relation on domains in analogy to the approximation relation on elements of domains described by the partial ordering. Reflexive domains were built as inverse limits of sequences of projections

⟨Dn←jnDn+1⟩n∈ω

其中 D0 任意，Dn+1=[Dn→Dn]，并适当选择初始嵌入-投影对 ⟨i0,j0⟩。这些空间是“罗素-策梅洛理论简单类型的函数空间对应物”[Scott, 1969b, p.24]；斯科特始终把模型看作无限类型函数空间，而非无类型函数空间 [Scott, 1975, pp.348–349]。⁹⁰

where D0 was arbitrary and Dn+1 was defined as [Dn →Dn], with a suitable choice of the initial embedding-projection pair ⟨i0, j0⟩. These spaces were “function-space analogues to the simple types of the Russell-Zermelo theory” [Scott, 1969b, p.24], and Scott always regarded his models as spaces of functions of infinite type, rather than of untyped functions, [Scott, 1975, pp.348–349].90

D∞ 之后，其他研究者很快发明若干模型，见 §9.3；同时深入研究 D∞ 结构及其在编程语言指称语义中的应用。最早结果可能来自戴维·帕克 [Park, 1976]：他于 1970 年初证明，罗森布卢姆-柯里组合子 Y 在 D∞ 中的解释，等于按式（1）迭代定义的最小不动点算子。另一方面，他也证明，若改变 D0 到 D1 的初始嵌入-投影对，这种可定义性结果可能失效。

Soon after Scott’s D∞models, several other models were invented by other workers, see 9.3 below, and there was intensive research on the structure of D∞and its application to the denotational semantics of programming languages. Probably the first result in this area was due to David Park [Park, 1976], who proved in early 1970 that the interpretation of the Rosenbloom-Curry combinator Y in D∞ coincided with the least fixed point operator defined iteratively as in (1) above. On the negative side, Park also showed that this definability result could be ruined by a different choice of the initial embedding-projection pair ⟨i0, j0⟩from D0 to D1.

9.2　计算性质与指称性质

9.2 Computational and denotational properties

70 年代初，人们广泛研究 λ 项操作性质与其作为 D∞ 元素的指称之间的关系。特别有趣的是：模型格结构中，元素序抽象地反映信息量，例如 [Scott, 1970c, p.171]；如何把它与操作上某些项比另一些项信息更多的直觉联系起来？例如，存在 λ 项 X，使对所有上下文 C[ ] 及正规形 N，

A subject of extensive research in the early 1970s was the relation between the operational properties of λ-terms and their denotations as elements of some D∞ model. A particularly interesting problem was to relate the lattice structure of the models, where the order relation among elements reflected their amount of information in an abstract sense (see, for example, [Scott, 1970c, p.171]), to the intuition that some terms are more informative than others in an operational sense. For example, there are λ-terms X such that, for all contexts C[ ] and all normal forms N,

C[X]=βN⇒∀M.C[M]=βN(2)

90　实际上，[Scott, 1969a] 的第一篇构造说明使用嵌入序列的直极限，得到等价结果。这是如今所谓极限-余极限重合 [Smyth and Plotkin, 1982] 的首例，是构造之范畴论版本的核心。

90Actually, in his first note on the construction, in [Scott, 1969a], Scott used direct limits of sequences of embeddings, obtaining an equivalent result. This was the first instance of what is now called the limit-colimit coincidence [Smyth and Plotkin, 1982], that lies at the heart of the categorical versions of the construction.

ΔΔ 是其中一例，Δ≡λx.xx。这类项不给周围上下文 C[ ] 贡献任何信息，因而可在任意模型中解释为最小元素 ⊥。当时斯特雷奇在牛津的研究生克里斯托弗·沃兹沃思，在 1971 年博士论文及后续两篇论文 [Wadsworth, 1971; Wadsworth, 1976; Wadsworth, 1978] 中深入研究此问题。⁹¹ 他精确而易懂地刻画：若 M 有正规形，M 的归约如何可说是从越来越好的正规形逼近中展开信息。他以表示未定义值的新常量 Ω 扩展 λ 演算，并把扩展语言中不含 β 可约式的 λ-Ω 项定义为近似正规形。⁹² 可说近似正规形 A 除其 Ω 出现处外，与普通 λ 项匹配；沃兹沃思据此定义 M 的近似正规形集合。主要结果之一是“极限定理”[Wadsworth, 1976, Thm.5.2]，后来称“逼近定理”[Barendregt, 1981, Thm.19.2.2]：M 在斯科特 D∞ 模型中的解释，是其近似正规形解释的最小上界。

one of these is ∆∆, where ∆≡λx.xx. Such terms contribute no information to the surrounding context C[ ], and could therefore be interpreted as the least element ⊥in any model. This problem was investigated thoroughly by Christopher Wadsworth, who was at the time a graduate student of Strachey at Oxford University, in his 1971 PhD thesis and two later papers, [Wadsworth, 1971; Wadsworth, 1976; Wadsworth, 1978].91 He found a precise and easily grasped characterization of the sense in which reduction of a term M can be said to develop information out of better and better approximations of a normal form of M (if one exists). He extended the λ-calculus with a new constant Ωrepresenting the undefined value, and defined an approximate normal form to be a term of the extended language (a λ-Ω-term) without β-redexes.92 An approximate normal form A could then be said to match an ordinary λ-term except at occurrences of Ωin A, and this was exploited by Wadsworth in defining the set of approximate normal forms of M. One of his main results was the “limit theorem”, [Wadsworth, 1976, Thm. 5.2], later called the “approximation theorem”, [Barendregt, 1981, Thm. 19.2.2], which stated that the interpretation of a term M in Scott’s D∞model is the least upper bound of the interpretations of its approximate normal forms.

贯穿其中的关键技术概念，是项 M 有头正规形，即可转换为 λx1…xn.zX1…Xm，n,m≥0。把发散计算对应于无头正规形项，被发现很自然：无头正规形项满足性质（2）[Wadsworth, 1976, Cor.5.5]，且每个这类项在标准 D∞ 模型中的解释都是 ⊥（同文 Cor.5.3）。

A key technical concept throughout was that of a term M having a head normal form, that is, being convertible to a term of the form λx1 . . . xn.zX1 . . . Xm, for some n, m ≥0. The identification of diverging computations with terms without a head normal form was found to be a natural one: for terms without head normal form, property (2) held, [Wadsworth, 1976, Cor. 5.5], and furthermore the interpretation of every such term in standard D∞models was the least element ⊥(ibid., Corollary 5.3).

独立地，亨克·巴伦德雷赫特在 1971 年 6 月博士论文 [Barendregt, 1971] 中，建议把发散计算对应于他所称不可解项，即不存在 k≥0 及项序列 X1,…,Xk，使得

Independently, Henk Barendregt, in June 1971 in his PhD thesis [Barendregt, 1971], proposed identifying diverging computations with what he called unsolvable terms, namely those λ-terms M such that

MX1⋯Xk=βI(3)

当时他试图基于递归论概念构造 λK 模型，关联 [Kreisel, 1958] 为简单类型 λ 演算描述的遗传递归运算模型。⁹³

held for no sequence of terms X1, . . . , Xk with k ≥0. Barendregt was then attempting to construct a model for the λ-K-calculus based on recursion-theoretic notions, related to the model of hereditarily recursive operations for the simply typed λ-calculus described in [Kreisel, 1958].93

自然的问题是：ΔΔ 等不可正规化项，在递归论解释中是否有定义？⁹⁴ 但由部分递归函数发散诱导的未定义概念太强。通过分析那些虽无正规形、却有有意义计算行为，因而不能一致地全部等同的项，⁹⁵ 才逐渐形成把未定义等同于不可解的观点。把全部不可解项等同所得理论的一致性，在 [Barendregt, 1971, Cor.3.2.15 与 Remark 3.2.16] 中以证明论手段证明。此外，以不可解项表示部分递归函数 f 的未定义值 f(n)，还得到所有部分递归函数均 λ 可定义的更简单新证明 [Barendregt, 1971, §1.3; Barendregt, 1981, §8.4]。

A natural problem in this setting was to find out whether non-normalizing terms like ∆∆were defined in the recursion-theoretic interpretation.94 However, the notion of undefinedness induced by divergence of partial recursive functions turned out to be too strong, and the identification of undefinedness with unsolvability emerged through an analysis of terms that do not have a normal form, yet possess a meaningful computational behavior that prevents them from all being identified consistently.95 The consistency of the theory resulting from the identification of all unsolvable terms was then proved by proof-theoretic means in [Barendregt, 1971, Cor. 3.2.15 and Remark 3.2.16]. Also, by using unsolvable terms to represent undefined values f(n) of a partial recursive function f, Barendregt obtained a simpler new proof that all partial recursive functions were λ-definable, [Barendregt, 1971, §1.3] or [Barendregt, 1981, §8.4].

91　沃兹沃思同期另一重要贡献（1970 年 10 月），是以续延作为跳转指称语义的技术。因忙于博士论文，合著报告 [Strachey and Wadsworth, 1974] 延迟发表。历史见 [Wadsworth, 2000]。几乎同时另有多人发明续延，见 [Reynolds, 1993]。92　该扩展由斯科特在 1970 年 10 月谈话中建议，见 [Wadsworth, 1976, fn.4, p.506]。93　[Barendregt, 1996] 清楚说明这一尝试及其积极结果，特别包括：对非 [Plotkin, 1974] 后来构造之通用生成子的 F、G，ω 规则，即若对所有闭项 Z 有 FZ=GZ 则 F=G，在 λβη 转换下部分成立。94　在该解释中，Δ 成为部分递归函数 φe，使 φe(x)≃φx(x)。95　例子是 λx.x(ΔΔ)K 与 λx.x(ΔΔ)(KI)，把二者等同会立即导出 K=KI。

91Another important contribution of Wadsworth dating back from the same period (October 1970) was the idea of continuations as a technique for the denotational semantics of jumps. Publication of this, as a joint report [Strachey and Wadsworth, 1974], was delayed because of work on his thesis. The history of his work on continuations can be found in [Wadsworth, 2000]. The notion of continuation was also invented by several other people at about the same time; see the historical account in [Reynolds, 1993]. 92This extension was suggested to Wadsworth by Dana Scott in conversation, in October 1970, see [Wadsworth, 1976, fn.4, p.506]. 93[Barendregt, 1996] gives a very clear account of this attempt and of the positive results arising from it. These include, in particular, the partial validity of the ω-rule (i.e. if FZ = GZ for all closed terms Z then F = G) for λβη-conversion, when F and G are not universal generators like those constructed later in [Plotkin, 1974]. 94In this interpretation, ∆becomes a partial recursive function φe such that φe(x) ≃φx(x). 95An example is the pair of terms λx.x(∆∆)K and λx.x(∆∆)(KI), whose identification would lead immediately to the equation K = KI.

沃兹沃思与海兰德独立认识到，M 无头正规形当且仅当不可解 [Wadsworth, 1971, p.94a; Wadsworth, 1976, Cor.5.3]，于是立即应用极限定理，就得到巴伦德雷赫特一致性结果的语义版本。另一方面，巴伦德雷赫特于 1973 年证明：λI 中，不可解当且仅当无正规形 [Barendregt, 1973]。

It was Wadsworth (and Hyland, independently) who realized that a term M had no head normal form if and only if it was unsolvable [Wadsworth, 1971, p.94a], [Wadsworth, 1976, Cor. 5.3], allowing him to obtain a semantical version of Barendregt’s consistency result by an immediate application of his limit theorem. (On the other hand, for the λI-calculus, Barendregt proved in 1973 that a term is unsolvable if and only if it has no normal form, [Barendregt, 1973].)
